# Section: connectors (3048 pages)

- `docs/connectors.md` — **Connectors**: Workato connectors securely connect and exchange data between your business apps with authentication, triggers, and actions for automated workflows.
- `docs/connectors/a2a.md` — **A2A Protocol**: Agent2Agent (A2A) Protocol is an open standard that enables communication and collaboration between AI agents.
- `docs/connectors/a2a/get-task-status-action.md` — **A2A Protocol - Get task status action**: The Get task status action retrieves the current state and available artifacts of an A2A Protocol task.
- `docs/connectors/a2a/resume-task.md` — **A2A Protocol - Resume a task**: You can resume a task in the INPUT_REQUIRED state after receiving a text response.
- `docs/connectors/a2a/send-task-action.md` — **A2A Protocol - Send task action**: The Send task action sends a message to the connected agent in the A2A Protocol connector. The action either returns with the new task ID, or waits until the agent reaches a terminal state to return artifacts.
- `docs/connectors/a2a/use-cases.md` — **A2A Protocol - Common use cases**: This document includes common use cases for the A2A Protocol connector in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/aconex.md` — **Aconex**: Aconex is a cloud-based project management and collaboration platform for the construction and engineering industries. The Aconex connector helps you manage documents, workflows, and communications across project teams globally.
- `docs/connectors/aconex/check-document-registration-status-action.md` — **Aconex - Check document registration status action**: The Check document registration status action checks the background job for the status of large file registration in Aconex.
- `docs/connectors/aconex/copy-project-action.md` — **Aconex - Copy project action**: The Copy project action copies an existing project in Aconex
- `docs/connectors/aconex/create-mail-action.md` — **Aconex - Create mail action**: The Create mail action creates a new mail item in Aconex.
- `docs/connectors/aconex/delete-draft-mail-action.md` — **Aconex - Delete draft mail action**: The Delete draft mail action deletes one or more draft mail items in Aconex.
- `docs/connectors/aconex/download-record-action.md` — **Aconex - Download record action**: The Download record action downloads a document or mail attachments from Aconex.
- `docs/connectors/aconex/get-mail-metadata-action.md` — **Aconex - Get mail metadata action**: The Get mail metadata action retrieves the metadata for a mail item in Aconex.
- `docs/connectors/aconex/list-records-action.md` — **Aconex - List records action**: The List records action retrieves a list of all records in the object type you select in Aconex.
- `docs/connectors/aconex/new-updated-documents-trigger.md` — **Aconex - New/updated documents trigger**: The New/updated documents trigger monitors Aconex for new or updated documents.
- `docs/connectors/aconex/new-updated-mail-trigger.md` — **Aconex - New/updated mail trigger**: The New/updated mail trigger monitors Aconex for new or updated mail items.
- `docs/connectors/aconex/register-document-action.md` — **Aconex - Register document action**: The Register document action registers a document in Aconex.
- `docs/connectors/aconex/search-records-action.md` — **Aconex - Search records action**: The Search records action retrieves a list of records that match your search criteria in Aconex.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory.md` — **Active Directory**: Connect Active Directory to Workato to automate user, group, and resource management. The connector authenticates with LDAP through an on-premise agent (OPA).
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/add-group-action.md` — **Active Directory - Add group action**: The Add group action in the Active Directory connector lets you create a new group, using a reference group's attributes as a template.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/add-user-action.md` — **Active Directory - Add user action**: The Add user action in the Active Directory connector lets you create a new user account, using a reference user's attributes as a template.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/add-user-to-group-action.md` — **Active Directory - Add user to group action**: The Add user to group action in the Active Directory connector lets you add a user account to a group you specify.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/add_entry.md` — **Active Directory - Add entry action**: The Add entry action in the Active Directory connector lets you create a new entry, using a reference entry's attributes as a template.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/delete-entry-action.md` — **Active Directory - Delete entry action**: The Delete entry action in the Active Directory connector lets you delete an entry you specify by distinguished name (DN).
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/disable-a-user-account-action.md` — **Active Directory - Disable a user account action**: The Disable a user account action in the Active Directory connector lets you disable a user account you specify by distinguished name.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/move-user-to-organizational-unit-action.md` — **Active Directory - Move user to organizational unit action**: The Move user to organizational unit action in the Active Directory connector lets you move a user account to an organizational unit you specify.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/new_entry_trigger.md` — **Active Directory - New entry trigger**: The New entry trigger in the Active Directory connector monitors for newly created entries and fires when an entry matches your search filter.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/remove-user-from-group-action.md` — **Active Directory - Remove user from group action**: The Remove user from group action in the Active Directory connector lets you remove a user account from a group you specify.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/rename-entry-action.md` — **Active Directory - Rename entry action**: The Rename entry action in the Active Directory connector lets you change the distinguished name (DN) of an entry you specify.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/scheduled_trigger.md` — **Active Directory - Scheduled entry search trigger (Batch)**: The Scheduled entry search batch trigger in the Active Directory connector searches entries on a schedule and processes matches in batches.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/search-groups-action.md` — **Active Directory - Search group action (Batch)**: The Search group batch action in the Active Directory connector retrieves groups matching your criteria and processes them in batches.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/search-users-action.md` — **Active Directory - Search users action (Batch)**: The Search users batch action in the Active Directory connector retrieves user accounts matching your criteria and processes them in batches.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/search_entries.md` — **Active Directory - Search entries action (Batch)**: The Search entries batch action in the Active Directory connector retrieves entries matching your criteria and processes them in batches.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/set-password-to-user-action.md` — **Active Directory - Set password to user action**: The Set password to user action in the Active Directory connector sets a new password for a user account over a secure LDAP connection.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/update-user-action.md` — **Active Directory - Update user action**: The Update user action in the Active Directory connector lets you update the attributes of an existing user account you specify.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/update_entry.md` — **Active Directory - Update entry action**: The Update entry action in the Active Directory connector lets you update the attributes of an existing entry you specify by distinguished name.
- `docs/connectors/active_directory/updated_entry_trigger.md` — **Active Directory - New/updated entry trigger**: The New/updated entry trigger in the Active Directory connector monitors for newly created or updated entries matching your search filter.
- `docs/connectors/adobe-commerce-magento.md` — **Adobe Commerce - Powered by Magento**: The Adobe Commerce (Magento) connector lets you automate e-commerce workflows: add customers and products, search orders, and trigger recipes on new events.
- `docs/connectors/adobe_experience_manager.md` — **Adobe Experience Manager**: The Adobe Experience Manager connector lets you manage AEM folders and assets, search and update assets, and trigger recipes when assets or folders change.
- `docs/connectors/adp_workforce.md` — **ADP Workforce Now**: The ADP Workforce Now connector lets you automate HR and payroll workflows, including scheduled searches for worker records.
- `docs/connectors/adp_workforce/scheduled_worker_search.md` — **ADP Workforce Now - Scheduled worker search trigger**: The Scheduled worker search trigger in the ADP Workforce Now connector picks up workers matching a search filter on a schedule, in batches.
- `docs/connectors/ai-by-workato.md` — **AI by Workato**: The AI by Workato connector lets you summarize, translate, categorize, parse, and analyze text and draft emails using built-in AI models.
- `docs/connectors/ai-by-workato/analyze-image-action.md` — **AI by Workato - Analyze image action**: The Analyze image action in the AI by Workato connector answers questions about an image or PDF based on the instructions you provide.
- `docs/connectors/ai-by-workato/analyze-text-action.md` — **AI by Workato - Analyze text action**: The Analyze text action in the AI by Workato connector extracts specific information from a piece of text based on the instructions you provide.
- `docs/connectors/ai-by-workato/categorize-text-action.md` — **AI by Workato - Categorize text action**: The Categorize text action in the AI by Workato connector lets you classify or sort text into predefined categories for routing, tagging, and filtering.
- `docs/connectors/ai-by-workato/draft-email-action.md` — **AI by Workato - Draft email action**: The Draft email action in the AI by Workato connector lets you generate personalized email subject lines and body content from a short description.
- `docs/connectors/ai-by-workato/parse-text-action.md` — **AI by Workato - Parse text action**: The Parse text action in the AI by Workato connector extracts structured data such as names, dates, and addresses from unstructured text.
- `docs/connectors/ai-by-workato/summarize-text-action.md` — **AI by Workato - Summarize text action**: The Summarize text action in the AI by Workato connector lets you condense long text into concise summaries with a defined maximum word count.
- `docs/connectors/ai-by-workato/translate-text-action.md` — **AI by Workato - Translate text action**: The Translate text action in the AI by Workato connector lets you automatically translate source text from one language into another.
- `docs/connectors/airtable.md` — **Airtable**: Connect Airtable to Workato to automate workflows with your cloud-based relational database platform.
- `docs/connectors/airtable/create-record-action.md` — **Airtable - Create record action**: The Create record action in the Airtable connector lets you create a new record in the base and table you select, with dynamic column fields.
- `docs/connectors/airtable/delete-record-action.md` — **Airtable - Delete record action**: The Delete record action in the Airtable connector lets you delete a specific record from a base and table by its record ID.
- `docs/connectors/airtable/get-record-action.md` — **Airtable - Get record action**: The Get record action in the Airtable connector lets you fetch a single record from a base and table by its record ID.
- `docs/connectors/airtable/list-records-action.md` — **Airtable - List records action**: The List records action in the Airtable connector lets you retrieve records from a base and table you specify, including their ID, name, and created time.
- `docs/connectors/airtable/new-record-trigger.md` — **Airtable - New record trigger**: The New record trigger in the Airtable connector fires when a new record is detected in a table you monitor, checking every five minutes by default.
- `docs/connectors/airtable/new-updated-record-trigger.md` — **Airtable - New or updated record trigger**: The New or updated record trigger in the Airtable connector fires when a record is created or an existing record is updated in a table you monitor.
- `docs/connectors/airtable/search-records-action.md` — **Airtable - Search records action**: The Search records action in the Airtable connector lets you find records in a base and table by the record names you specify.
- `docs/connectors/airtable/update-record-action.md` — **Airtable - Update record action**: The Update record action in the Airtable connector lets you update a specific record in a base and table by its record ID.
- `docs/connectors/airtop.md` — **Airtop**: The Airtop connector lets you launch and manage cloud browser sessions and use AI actions to scrape, query, and interact with web pages in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/airtop/click-element-action.md` — **Airtop - Click element action**: The Click element action enables you to click an element described in natural language in Airtop.
- `docs/connectors/airtop/create-record-action.md` — **Airtop - Create record action**: The Create record action enables you to create a session or window in Airtop.
- `docs/connectors/airtop/delete-record-action.md` — **Airtop - Delete record action**: The Delete record action enables you to terminate a session or close a window in Airtop.
- `docs/connectors/airtop/extract-paginated-list-action.md` — **Airtop - Extract paginated list action**: The Extract paginated list action enables you to extract a list across multiple pages with AI in Airtop.
- `docs/connectors/airtop/get-record-action.md` — **Airtop - Get record action**: The Get record action enables you to retrieve a session or window by ID in Airtop.
- `docs/connectors/airtop/load-url-in-window-action.md` — **Airtop - Load URL in window action**: The Load URL in window action enables you to navigate a browser window to a URL in Airtop.

- `docs/connectors/airtop/scrape-page-content-action.md` — **Airtop - Scrape page content action**: The Scrape page content action enables you to extract the current page as markdown in Airtop.
- `docs/connectors/airtop/search-records-action.md` — **Airtop - Search records action**: The Search records action enables you to search and retrieve a list of sessions or windows in Airtop.
- `docs/connectors/airtop/type-text-action.md` — **Airtop - Type text action**: The Type text action enables you to type text into the browser window in Airtop.
- `docs/connectors/airwallex.md` — **Airwallex**: The Airwallex connector lets you automate payments and transfers to and from your Airwallex account across more than 150 currencies in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/airwallex/config-operation-action.md` — **Airwallex - Config operation action**: The Config operation action enables you to query for active merchant configurations in Airwallex.
- `docs/connectors/airwallex/create-record-action.md` — **Airwallex - Create record action**: The Create record action enables you to create the specified record in Airwallex.
- `docs/connectors/airwallex/execute-operation-action.md` — **Airwallex - Execute operation action**: The Execute operation action enables you to execute available operations that are not CRUD operations in Airwallex.
- `docs/connectors/airwallex/get-record-details-by-id-action.md` — **Airwallex - Get record details by ID action**: The Get record details by ID action enables you to retrieve the details of a specific record in Airwallex.

- `docs/connectors/airwallex/search-records-action.md` — **Airwallex - Search records action**: The Search records action enables you to search and retrieve a list of records for an object in Airwallex.
- `docs/connectors/airwallex/update-record-action.md` — **Airwallex - Update record action**: The Update record action enables you to update a record you specify in Airwallex.
- `docs/connectors/akeneo.md` — **Akeneo**: The Akeneo connector lets you search, retrieve, upsert, and download product records in Workato, and trigger recipes on product changes.

- `docs/connectors/akeneo/upsert-record-action.md` — **Akeneo - Upsert record action**: The Upsert record action creates or updates a record, such as a product, in Akeneo.

- `docs/connectors/amazon-ses.md` — **Amazon SES**: The Amazon SES connector enables you to use workflows like auto-responders, email unsubscribe systems, and applications that generate customer support tickets from incoming emails.

- `docs/connectors/amazon-ses/update-object.md` — **Amazon SES - Update object action**: The Update object action updates an object in Amazon SES.

- `docs/connectors/amazon-sns/new-message-trigger.md` — **Amazon SNS - New message trigger**: The New message trigger monitors a message topic and picks up newly published messages.
- `docs/connectors/amazon-sns/publish-message-action.md` — **Amazon SNS - Publish message action**: The Publish message action publishes a message to a specified topic.
- `docs/connectors/amplify.md` — **Amplify**: The Amplify connector enables you to automate strategy execution workflows, managing strategic projects, aligning goals, and tracking progress.
- `docs/connectors/amplify/create-record-action.md` — **Amplify - Create record action**: The Create record action enables you to create a record in Amplify.
- `docs/connectors/amplify/delete-record-action.md` — **Amplify - Delete record action**: The Delete record action enables you to delete a specific record in Amplify.
- `docs/connectors/amplify/execute-operation-action.md` — **Amplify - Execute operation action**: The **Execute operation** action enables you to perform an operation using Amplify. The list of operations is dynamically generated based on the API endpoints found in the Swagger specification file for Amplify. If an API endpoint doesn’t match any Create, Get, Search, Update, or Delete actions, it is displayed in the **Execute operation** action.
- `docs/connectors/amplify/get-record-details-by-id-action.md` — **Amplify - Get record details by ID action**: The Get record details by ID action enables you to retrieve the details of a specific record in Amplify.
- `docs/connectors/amplify/search-records-action.md` — **Amplify - Search records action**: The Search records action enables you to retrieve a list of records for an object in Amplify.
- `docs/connectors/amplify/update-record-action.md` — **Amplify - Update record action**: The Update record action enables you to update the details of a specific record in Amplify.
- `docs/connectors/analytics-cloud.md` — **Analytics Cloud**: The Analytics Cloud connector uses the standard Salesforce connector to upload data from external sources such as ERP systems and databases.
- `docs/connectors/anaplan.md` — **Anaplan**: The Anaplan connector lets you automate planning and forecasting: run import, export, process, and deletion batches and upload or download files.
- `docs/connectors/anaplan/download-dump-file.md` — **Anaplan - Download dump file action**: The Download dump file action in the Anaplan connector lets you download the dump file from the latest task of a process and return its contents.
- `docs/connectors/anaplan/download-file-action.md` — **Anaplan - Download file action**: The Download file action in the Anaplan connector lets you download a file from a selected workspace and model and return its contents.
- `docs/connectors/anaplan/run-deletion-action.md` — **Anaplan - Run deletion batch action**: The Run deletion batch action in the Anaplan connector lets you start a deletion task in a model and monitor its progress until it completes.
- `docs/connectors/anaplan/run-export-action.md` — **Anaplan - Run data export batch action**: The Run data export batch action in the Anaplan connector lets you run a data export process from a model and track its progress to completion.
- `docs/connectors/anaplan/run-import-action.md` — **Anaplan - Run data import batch action**: The Run data import batch action in the Anaplan connector lets you import data into a model and track the import process to completion.
- `docs/connectors/anaplan/run-process-action.md` — **Anaplan - Run process batch action**: The Run process batch action in the Anaplan connector lets you start a process in a model, monitor each task, and return results on completion.
- `docs/connectors/anaplan/upload-file-action.md` — **Anaplan - Upload file action**: The Upload file action in the Anaplan connector lets you upload file contents to a selected workspace and model, including CSV options.
- `docs/connectors/anthropic.md` — **Anthropic**: Anthropic provides large language models, including the Claude family, for tasks such as text generation, analysis, and conversation.

- `docs/connectors/anthropic/categorize-text-action.md` — **Anthropic - Categorize text action**: The Categorize text action chooses the category that fits the input text from a list of user-defined categories in Anthropic.

- `docs/connectors/anthropic/parse-text-action.md` — **Anthropic - Parse text action**: The Parse text action processes inputted text to find specific information based on defined guidelines in Anthropic.
- `docs/connectors/anthropic/send-messages-to-models-action.md` — **Anthropic - Send messages to models action**: The Send messages to models action sends a message to an Anthropic model you select and returns its response.
- `docs/connectors/anthropic/summarize-text-action.md` — **Anthropic - Summarize text action**: The Summarize text action summarizes inputted text into a shorter version.
- `docs/connectors/anthropic/translate-text-action.md` — **Anthropic - Translate text action**: The Translate text action translates inputted text into a different language.
- `docs/connectors/apify.md` — **Apify**: Use the Apify connector in Workato to find Actors, run them, and map dataset items into recipe steps.

- `docs/connectors/apify/start-actor-action.md` — **Apify - Start actor action**: The Start actor action starts an Actor run in Apify and returns without waiting for it to finish.
