- `docs/connectors/assemblyai.md` — **AssemblyAI**: Transcribe audio, export subtitles, and run LLM prompts over transcripts, then trigger recipes when jobs finish.
- `docs/connectors/assemblyai/apply-llm-to-transcript-action.md` — **AssemblyAI - Apply LLM to transcript action**: Run an LLM Gateway chat completion over a transcript's text, or a free-form prompt, in AssemblyAI.
- `docs/connectors/assemblyai/completed-transcript-trigger.md` — **AssemblyAI - Completed transcript trigger**: Fire a recipe when a transcript job finishes using a static webhook you set on the submit action.
- `docs/connectors/assemblyai/create-record-action.md` — **AssemblyAI - Create record action**: Submit an uploaded audio file for transcription without waiting for the job to finish.
- `docs/connectors/assemblyai/delete-record-action.md` — **AssemblyAI - Delete record action**: Permanently redact a transcript's content in AssemblyAI.
- `docs/connectors/assemblyai/export-subtitles-action.md` — **AssemblyAI - Export subtitles action**: Download SRT or VTT subtitle text for a completed transcript in AssemblyAI.
- `docs/connectors/assemblyai/generate-speech-understanding-action.md` — **AssemblyAI - Generate speech understanding action**: Run translation, speaker identification, custom formatting, summarization, or action item extraction on a transcript in AssemblyAI.
- `docs/connectors/assemblyai/get-record-action.md` — **AssemblyAI - Get record action**: Retrieve a transcript by ID, including its full text and word-level details, from AssemblyAI.
- `docs/connectors/assemblyai/search-records-action.md` — **AssemblyAI - Search records action**: List transcripts in AssemblyAI, with filters for status, creation date, and pagination.
- `docs/connectors/assemblyai/transcribe-and-wait-action.md` — **AssemblyAI - Transcribe and wait action**: Submit an audio file for transcription and poll AssemblyAI until the transcript completes, in a single action.
- `docs/connectors/assemblyai/upload-audio-file-action.md` — **AssemblyAI - Upload audio file action**: Send raw audio bytes to AssemblyAI and get back a URL you can pass to a transcription request.
- `docs/connectors/authhub.md` — **AuthHub**: The AuthHub connector lets you automate access management: search records, get record details, and add or remove members from groups.
- `docs/connectors/authhub/add-member-to-a-group-action.md` — **AuthHub - Add member to a group action**: The Add member to a group action adds multiple members to a group in AuthHub.
- `docs/connectors/authhub/get-record-details-by-id-action.md` — **AuthHub - Get record details by ID action**: The Get record details by ID action retrieves a record from AuthHub.
- `docs/connectors/authhub/remove-member-from-a-group-action.md` — **AuthHub - Remove member from a group action**: The Remove member from a group action removes multiple members from an AuthHub group.
- `docs/connectors/authhub/search-records-action.md` — **AuthHub - Search records action**: The Search records action retrieves a list of records from AuthHub matching your search criteria.
- `docs/connectors/aws-comprehend.md` — **AWS Comprehend**: The AWS Comprehend connector provides text analysis capabilities to extract key phrases, entities, sentiment, and language insights from unstructured text data in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/aws-comprehend/classify-document-action.md` — **AWS Comprehend - Classify document action**: The Classify document action analyzes a document in real-time using a custom AWS Comprehend's classification model in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/aws-comprehend/create-record-action.md` — **AWS Comprehend - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a record in AWS Comprehend.
- `docs/connectors/aws-comprehend/get-record-details-action.md` — **AWS Comprehend - Get record details action**: The Get record details action retrieves the details of a record, such as a document classification job using AWS Comprehend in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/aws-comprehend/start-document-classification-job.md` — **AWS Comprehend - Start document classification job action**: The Start document classification job action starts an asynchronous job to classify a collection of documents using a custom AWS Comprehend model.
- `docs/connectors/aws-glue.md` — **AWS Glue**: Use the AWS Glue connector to prepare and transform data for analytics.
- `docs/connectors/aws-glue/get-job-details-action.md` — **AWS Glue - Get job details action**: The Get job details action retrieves the definition and configuration of an AWS Glue job.
- `docs/connectors/aws-glue/get-job-run-details-action.md` — **AWS Glue - Get job run details action**: The Get job run details action retrieves the metadata for a job run in AWS Glue.
- `docs/connectors/aws-glue/get-job-run-status-action.md` — **AWS Glue - Get job run status action**: The Get job run status action retrieves the status of an AWS Glue job.
- `docs/connectors/aws-glue/list-job-runs-action.md` — **AWS Glue - List job runs action**: The List job runs action retrieves a list of job runs for a specific AWS Glue job.
- `docs/connectors/aws-glue/new-file-in-s3-trigger.md` — **AWS Glue - New file in S3 trigger**: The New file in S3 trigger monitors AWS Glue for a new file in AWS S3.
- `docs/connectors/aws-glue/new-updated-job-run-trigger.md` — **AWS Glue - New/updated job run trigger**: The New/updated job run trigger monitors AWS Glue for new or updated job runs.
- `docs/connectors/aws-glue/start-run-a-glue-job-action.md` — **AWS Glue - Start/run a Glue job action**: The Start/run a Glue job action starts a new run of an AWS Glue job.
- `docs/connectors/aws-glue/stop-a-running-glue-job-action.md` — **AWS Glue - Stop a running Glue job action**: The Stop a running Glue job action stops a running job for an AWS Glue job you select.
- `docs/connectors/aws-inspector-2.md` — **AWS Inspector2**: The AWS Inspector2 connector enables you to automate vulnerability management workflows by retrieving and responding to security findings in AWS.
- `docs/connectors/aws-inspector-2/add-tags-action.md` — **AWS Inspector2 - Add tags action**: The Add tags action attaches metadata (tags) to resources, such as findings or reports, using key-value pairs.
- `docs/connectors/aws-inspector-2/create-filter-action.md` — **AWS Inspector2 - Create filter action**: The Create filter action defines custom filters for findings.
- `docs/connectors/aws-inspector-2/get-sbom-export-action.md` — **AWS Inspector2 - Get SBOM export action**: The Get SBOM Export action retrieves the status and details of a Software Bill of Materials (SBOM) export operation.
- `docs/connectors/aws-inspector-2/list-findings.md` — **AWS Inspector2 - List findings action**: The List findings action retrieves a list of findings in your environment.
- `docs/connectors/aws-inspector-2/new-findings-trigger.md` — **AWS Inspector2 - New findings trigger**: The New Findings trigger monitors AWS Inspector2 for new findings.
- `docs/connectors/aws-inspector-2/search-vulnerabilities-action.md` — **AWS Inspector2 - Search vulnerabilities action**: The Search vulnerabilities action searches for information about known vulnerabilities, such as CVEs.
- `docs/connectors/aws_lambda.md` — **AWS Lambda**: The AWS Lambda connector enables you to invoke serverless functions and automate event-driven workflows with AWS Lambda in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID (formerly Azure Active Directory)**: The Microsoft Entra ID connector lets you manage identity: create, update, search users and groups, manage members and licenses, and trigger on changes.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/add-remove-user-license-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Add or remove user license action**: Add or remove subscriptions for the user. You can also enable and disable specific plans associated with a subscription.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/connection-setup.md` — **How to connect to Microsoft Entra ID**: Set up and connect Microsoft Entra ID in Workato, including registering an app, granting Microsoft Graph permissions, and generating a client secret.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/create-group-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Create group action**: Creates a new security group anonymously. Groups created without specifying owners will not be modifiable.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/create-user-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Create user action**: Creates a new security group anonymously. Groups created without specifying owners will not be modifiable.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/custom-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Custom action**: Build your own Microsoft Entra ID action with an HTTP request.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/delete-group-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Delete group action**: This action deletes a group in Microsoft Entra ID.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/delete-user-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Delete user action**: Deletes a user in Microsoft Entra ID.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/get-group-by-id-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Get group details by ID action**: Returns the default subset of available properties and relationships of a group object.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/get-user-by-id-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Get user details by ID action**: Returns the default subset of properties of a user object.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/get-user-license-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Get user license action**: Retrieve a list of License details objects for enterprise users.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/new-deleted-user-trigger.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - New deleted user trigger**: The New deleted user trigger in the Microsoft Entra ID connector fires when a user is deleted from your directory.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/new-group-trigger.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - New group trigger**: The New group trigger in the Microsoft Entra ID connector fires when a new group is created in your directory.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/new-member-to-group-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Add member to group action**: Adds member to a group in Microsoft Entra ID.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/new-updated-group-trigger.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - New/updated group trigger**: The New/updated group trigger in the Microsoft Entra ID connector fires when a group is created or updated in your directory.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/new-updated-member-trigger.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - New/updated member (real-time) trigger**: The New/updated member (real-time) trigger monitors Microsoft Entra ID for new or updated members.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/new-updated-user-trigger.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - New/updated user trigger**: The New/updated user trigger in the Microsoft Entra ID connector fires when a user is created or updated in your directory.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/new-user-trigger.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - New user trigger**: The New user trigger in the Microsoft Entra ID connector fires when a new user is created in your directory.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/remove-member-from-group-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Remove member from group action**: Removes a member from a group. You will not be able to remove a member from groups with dynamic memberships.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/revoke-user-sign-in-session-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Revoke sign-in session action**: Revokes user sign-in session.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/search-group-member-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Search group members action**: Retrieve a list of the group's direct members using search filters.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/search-transitive-group-member-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Search transitive group members action**: Get a list of the group's transitive members using search filters.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/search-users-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Search users action**: Retrieve a list of users using search filters. If more records exceed the specified limit, this action will paginate through the remaining records.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/update-group-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Update group action**: Updates the properties of a group object in Microsoft Entra ID.
- `docs/connectors/azure-ad/update-user-action.md` — **Microsoft Entra ID - Update user action**: Updates a user in Microsoft Entra ID.
- `docs/connectors/azure-devops.md` — **Azure DevOps**: The Azure DevOps connector lets you work with Azure DevOps projects, boards, builds, and pipelines in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/azure-devops/create-record-action.md` — **Azure DevOps - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a new record in Azure DevOps.
- `docs/connectors/azure-devops/get-record-action.md` — **Azure DevOps - Get record action**: The Get record action retrieves a record in Azure DevOps.
- `docs/connectors/azure-devops/new-event-trigger.md` — **Azure DevOps - New event trigger**: The New event trigger monitors Azure DevOps for new events.
- `docs/connectors/azure-devops/new-updated-work-item-trigger.md` — **Azure DevOps - New/Updated work item (batch) trigger**: The New/Updated work item (batch) trigger monitors Azure DevOps for new or updated work items.
- `docs/connectors/azure-devops/new-work-item-trigger.md` — **Azure DevOps - New work item (batch) trigger**: The New work item (batch) trigger monitors Azure DevOps new work items.
- `docs/connectors/azure-devops/search-records-action.md` — **Azure DevOps - Search records action**: The Search records action searches for records in Azure DevOps.
- `docs/connectors/azure-devops/update-record-action.md` — **Azure DevOps - Update record action**: The Update record action updates a record in Azure DevOps.
- `docs/connectors/azure-devops/upload-work-item-attachment-action.md` — **Azure DevOps - Upload work item attachment action**: The Upload work item attachment action uploads an attachment to Azure DevOps.
- `docs/connectors/azure-file-storage.md` — **Azure File Storage**: The Azure File Storage connector lets you automate file management on cloud file shares: get file contents and upsert or delete files.
- `docs/connectors/azure-file-storage/create-directory-action.md` — **Azure File Storage - Create directory action**: The Create directory action creates a new directory in Azure File Storage under a specified share or parent directory.
- `docs/connectors/azure-file-storage/delete-directory-action.md` — **Azure File Storage - Delete directory action**: The Delete directory action deletes an empty directory from Azure File Storage.
- `docs/connectors/azure-file-storage/delete-file-action.md` — **Azure File Storage - Delete file action**: The Delete file action deletes an existing file from Azure File Storage.
- `docs/connectors/azure-file-storage/get-file-contents-action.md` — **Azure File Storage - Get file contents action**: The Get file contents action enables you to download the contents of an existing file from Azure File Storage.
- `docs/connectors/azure-file-storage/rename-directory-action.md` — **Azure File Storage - Rename directory action**: The Rename directory action renames an existing directory within the same file share in Azure File Storage.
- `docs/connectors/azure-file-storage/search-files-and-directories-action.md` — **Azure File Storage - Search files and directories action**: The Search files and directories action lists the files and directories under a directory path in Azure File Storage.
- `docs/connectors/azure-file-storage/upsert-file-action.md` — **Azure File Storage - Upsert file action**: The upsert file action enables you to create a new file or update an existing one with the specified content.
- `docs/connectors/azure-monitor/actions.md` — **Azure Monitor - Actions**: Browse the actions supported by the Azure Monitor connector in Workato for sending and injecting custom logs into Azure Monitor.
- `docs/connectors/azure-monitor/actions/inject-custom-logs.md` — **Azure Monitor - Inject custom logs action**: The Inject custom logs action sends custom log events to Azure Monitor. It allows you to define the structure of your log events and send them to a specified Data Collection Rule (DCR) stream.
- `docs/connectors/azure-monitor/actions/send-custom-log.md` — **Azure Monitor - Send custom log action**: Deprecated action that sends custom logs to Azure Monitor using the HTTP Data Collector API. Migrate to the Inject custom logs action.
- `docs/connectors/azure-openai.md` — **Azure OpenAI**: Azure OpenAI is a collaboration between Microsoft Azure and OpenAI, an artificial intelligence research organization. Azure OpenAI is a cloud-based platform that enables users to build and deploy AI models.
- `docs/connectors/azure-openai/chatgpt-conversations.md` — **Azure OpenAI - Send messages to ChatGPT action**: The Send messages to ChatGPT action sends a message to Chat GPT and gathers a response using model GPT 3.5 Turbo from Azure OpenAI.
- `docs/connectors/azure-openai/complete-text-prompt.md` — **Azure OpenAI - Complete text prompt action**: The Complete text prompt action generates completions for a given prompt using OpenAI's language models.
- `docs/connectors/azure-openai/generate-images.md` — **Azure OpenAI - Generate images action**: The Generate images action generates high-quality images from text descriptions using machine learning models, such as Dall-E.
- `docs/connectors/azure-openai/generate-text-embedding.md` — **Azure OpenAI - Generate text embedding action**: The Generate text embedding action allows you to represent text data as numerical vectors.
- `docs/connectors/azure_blob_storage.md` — **Azure Blob Storage**: Azure Blob Storage enables you to create data lakes for analytics and provides storage for building powerful cloud-native and mobile apps.
- `docs/connectors/azure_blob_storage/create-container-action.md` — **Azure Blob Storage - Create container action**: The create container action creates a container in the connected Azure Blob Storage account.
- `docs/connectors/azure_blob_storage/download-blob-contents-action.md` — **Azure Blob Storage - Download blob contents action**: The download blob contents action downloads a blob in the connected Azure Blob Storage account.
- `docs/connectors/azure_blob_storage/generate-pre-signed-url-action.md` — **Azure Blob Storage - Generate pre-signed URL action**: The generate pre-signed URL action generates a pre-signed URL for a blob in Azure Blob Storage.
- `docs/connectors/azure_blob_storage/get-blob-properties-action.md` — **Azure Blob Storage - Get blob properties action**: The get blob properties action retrieves the properties of a blob in the Azure Blob Storage account, including its metadata.
- `docs/connectors/azure_blob_storage/get-container-properties-action.md` — **Azure Blob Storage - Get container properties action**: The get container properties action retrieves the properties of a container in the Azure Blob Storage account.
- `docs/connectors/azure_blob_storage/new-blob-trigger.md` — **Azure Blob Storage - New blob trigger (real-time)**: The New blob trigger picks up new blobs that are created in Azure Blob Storage immediately.
- `docs/connectors/azure_blob_storage/new-event-trigger.md` — **Azure Blob Storage - New event trigger (real-time)**: The New event trigger activates when specified blob storage events occur in monitored containers.
- `docs/connectors/azure_blob_storage/search-blob-action.md` — **Azure Blob Storage - Search blobs action**: The search blobs action searches blobs in your Azure Blob Storage account.
- `docs/connectors/azure_blob_storage/search-containers-action.md` — **Azure Blob Storage - Search containers action**: The search containers action searches the containers in your Azure Blob Storage account.
- `docs/connectors/azure_blob_storage/update-blob-metadata-action.md` — **Azure Blob Storage - Update blob metadata action**: The update blob metadata action updates the metadata attached to a blob in the connected Azure Blob Storage account.
- `docs/connectors/azure_blob_storage/upload-blob-action.md` — **Azure Blob Storage - Upload blob action**: The upload blob action uploads a blob to the connected Azure blob Storage account.
- `docs/connectors/azure_monitor.md` — **Azure Monitor**: Azure Monitor is a comprehensive monitoring and observability platform for Azure resources. Send custom logs from your Workato recipes to Azure Monitor Logs for centralized monitoring, troubleshooting, and compliance tracking.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr.md` — **BambooHR**: The BambooHR connector lets you automate HR workflows: create, update, and list employees, manage time off requests, run reports, and trigger on changes.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/create-custom-report-action.md` — **BambooHR - Create custom employee report action**: The Create custom employee report action in the BambooHR connector lets you build a report on employees from selected fields to fetch large volumes of data.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/create-employee-action.md` — **BambooHR - Create employee action**: The Create employee action in the BambooHR connector lets you create a new employee record with a first name, last name, and optional fields.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/create-table-record.md` — **BambooHR – Create table record of employee action**: The Create table record of employee action adds a new row to a selected BambooHR employee table.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/create-update-time-off-request-action.md` — **BambooHR - Create/update time off request action**: The Create/update time off request action in the BambooHR connector lets you create or update a time off request on behalf of an employee.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/delete-table-record.md` — **BambooHR – Delete table record action**: The Delete table record action removes a row from a selected BambooHR employee table.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/get-company-report-by-id-action.md` — **BambooHR - Get company employee report by ID action**: The Get company employee report by ID action in the BambooHR connector lets you fetch an existing employee report by its report ID.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/get-employee-details-action.md` — **BambooHR - Get employee details by ID action**: The Get employee details by ID action in the BambooHR connector lets you retrieve default and custom fields for an employee by employee ID.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/get-report-by-id-v2-action.md` — **BambooHR – Get report by ID (v2) action**: The Get report by ID (v2) action retrieves a report from BambooHR using the Custom Reports API.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/get-table-records-of-employee-action.md` — **BambooHR - Get table records of employee action**: The Get table records of employee action in the BambooHR connector lets you retrieve table data for an employee identified by the employee ID.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/list-employees-in-directory-action.md` — **BambooHR - List employees in directory action**: The List employees in directory action in the BambooHR connector lets you retrieve all employees in your instance along with their details.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/list-time-off-requests-action.md` — **BambooHR - List time off requests action**: The List time off requests action in the BambooHR connector lets you list time off requests between two dates, with filters to narrow the result.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/new-employee-realtime-trigger.md` — **BambooHR - New employee trigger (real-time)**: The New employee real-time trigger in the BambooHR connector fires through a webhook each time a new employee is created in your instance.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/new-employee-trigger.md` — **BambooHR - New employee trigger**: The New employee trigger in the BambooHR connector picks up new employees once every poll interval, processing each one as a separate job.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/schedule-custom-report-trigger.md` — **BambooHR - Schedule custom employee report trigger**: The Schedule custom employee report trigger in the BambooHR connector runs a report on a schedule and returns new or updated employees between runs.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/update-employee-action.md` — **BambooHR - Update employee action**: The Update employee action in the BambooHR connector lets you update an existing employee record identified by the employee ID.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/update-table-record.md` — **BambooHR – Update table record of employee action**: The Update table record of employee action updates a row in a selected BambooHR employee table.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/update-time-off-request-status-action.md` — **BambooHR - Update time off request status action**: The Update time off request status action in the BambooHR connector lets you approve, deny, or cancel a specified time off request.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/updated-employee-realtime-trigger.md` — **BambooHR - New/Updated employee trigger (real-time)**: The New/Updated employee real-time trigger in the BambooHR connector fires through a webhook each time an employee is created or updated.
- `docs/connectors/bamboo-hr/updated-employee-trigger.md` — **BambooHR - New/Updated employee trigger**: The New/Updated employee trigger in the BambooHR connector picks up created or updated employees once every poll interval as separate jobs.
- `docs/connectors/baserow.md` — **Baserow**: Connect Baserow to Workato to create, update, search, and delete table rows, and trigger recipes when rows change.
- `docs/connectors/baserow/create-record-action.md` — **Baserow - Create record action**: Add a new row to a table using your table's own column names as the input fields.
- `docs/connectors/baserow/delete-record-action.md` — **Baserow - Delete record action**: Remove one row from a table by its row ID in Baserow.
- `docs/connectors/baserow/get-record-action.md` — **Baserow - Get record details by ID action**: Retrieve one row's current field values using its row ID in a Baserow table.
- `docs/connectors/baserow/list-records-action.md` — **Baserow - List records action**: List the tables in your Baserow workspace or the fields defined on one of your tables.
- `docs/connectors/baserow/new-event-trigger.md` — **Baserow - New event trigger**: Fire a recipe on row changes in a Baserow table using a webhook you register in Baserow.
- `docs/connectors/baserow/search-records-action.md` — **Baserow - Search records action**: Look up rows in a table with pagination, keyword search, sorting, or field-level filters.
- `docs/connectors/baserow/update-record-action.md` — **Baserow - Update record action**: Change one existing row's field values, identified by its row ID, in a Baserow table.
- `docs/connectors/baserow/update-records-action-batch.md` — **Baserow - Update records action (batch)**: Update up to 200 existing rows in a single request instead of calling Update record repeatedly.
- `docs/connectors/bigquery.md` — **Google BigQuery**: The Google BigQuery connector allows you to automate actions on datasets in your Google BigQuery instance, such as inserting rows or performing queries on existing datasets.
- `docs/connectors/bigquery/get-query-result-action.md` — **Google BigQuery - Get query job output action**: This action takes in a specific Job ID and returns the query results of this job.
- `docs/connectors/bigquery/insert.md` — **Google BigQuery - Insert rows into Google BigQuery**: The Insert row actions allow you to insert rows into Google BigQuery.
- `docs/connectors/bigquery/job-completed-trigger.md` — **Google BigQuery - Job completed trigger**: The Job completed trigger polls regularly for jobs completed in Google BigQuery.
- `docs/connectors/bigquery/load_data.md` — **Google BigQuery - Inserting data into Google BigQuery**: The Google BigQuery **Insert** action allows you to insert millions of rows into a BigQuery table efficiently.
- `docs/connectors/bigquery/new-row-trigger.md` — **Google BigQuery - New row trigger**: The **New row** trigger picks up rows that are inserted in the selected table.
- `docs/connectors/bigquery/run-custom-sql.md` — **Google BigQuery - Run custom SQL action**: The **Run custom SQL** action allows you send a SQL string to Google BigQuery to be executed.
- `docs/connectors/bigquery/scheduled-query-trigger.md` — **Google BigQuery - Scheduled query trigger**: The **Scheduled query** trigger executes a query on a regular basis.
- `docs/connectors/bigquery/select-rows-custom-insert.md` — **Google BigQuery - Select rows using custom SQL and insert into table (batch)**: Run a custom SQL query in Google BigQuery and insert the results into a destination table. Use this action to stage or transform data for later use.
- `docs/connectors/bigquery/select-rows-custom.md` — **Google BigQuery - Select rows using custom SQL action (batch)**: Run a custom SQL query in Google BigQuery and retrieve matching rows.
- `docs/connectors/bigquery/select.md` — **Google BigQuery - Select rows action (batch)**: The Select rows batch action retrieves rows from a Google BigQuery table using WHERE predicates.
- `docs/connectors/bill.md` — **BILL**: The BILL connector lets you automate finance workflows: create, update, and search records, build and send invoices, and trigger on new records.
- `docs/connectors/bill/add-line-to-invoice-action.md` — **BILL - Add line to invoice action**: The Add line to invoice action in the BILL connector adds a line item, including item, quantity, and price, to an existing invoice in BILL.
- `docs/connectors/bill/create-record-action.md` — **BILL - Create record action**: The Create record action in the BILL connector creates a new object of the type you select in BILL.
- `docs/connectors/bill/delete-record-action.md` — **BILL - Delete record action**: The Delete record action in the BILL connector deletes an object of the type you select using its internal ID in BILL.
- `docs/connectors/bill/get-disbursement-data-action.md` — **BILL - Get disbursement data action**: The Get disbursement data action in the BILL connector retrieves data about a payment, including its type, disbursement status, and vendor name.
- `docs/connectors/bill/get-record-details-by-ID-action.md` — **BILL - Get record details by ID action**: The Get record details by ID action in the BILL connector retrieves data about an object of the type you select using its internal ID in BILL.
- `docs/connectors/bill/new-or-updated-record-trigger.md` — **BILL - New/updated record trigger**: The New/updated record trigger in the BILL connector monitors a specified object type and fires when records are created or updated in BILL.
- `docs/connectors/bill/new-record-trigger.md` — **BILL - New record trigger**: The New record trigger in the BILL connector monitors a specified object type and fires when new records are created in BILL.
- `docs/connectors/bill/search-record-action.md` — **BILL - Search record action (batch)**: The Search record batch action in the BILL connector searches BILL objects in batches and returns the records that match your filter criteria.
- `docs/connectors/bill/send-invoice-action.md` — **BILL - Send invoice action**: The Send invoice action emails an invoice to a customer in BILL.
- `docs/connectors/bill/update-record-action.md` — **BILL - Update record action**: The Update record action in the BILL connector updates an existing object of the type you select using its internal ID in BILL.
- `docs/connectors/bim360.md` — **BIM 360**: The BIM 360 connector lets you automate construction workflows: manage project objects and issues, handle documents and drawings, and trigger on changes.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/create-issue-in-a-project-v2-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Create issue in a project (V2) action**: The create issue in a project (V2) action creates a new issue in BIM 360.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/create-object-in-a-project-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Create object in a project action**: The create object in a project action creates a new object in BIM 360.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/download-cost-document-in-a-project-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Download cost document in a project action**: The download cost document in a project action retrieves the file content of a BIM 360 cost document you specify.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/download-document-in-a-project-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Download document in a project action**: The download document in a project action retrieves the file content of a BIM 360 document you specify.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/download-drawing-export-in-a-project-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Download drawing export in a project action**: The export drawing in a project action retrieves the file content of an exported drawing you specify from BIM 360.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/export-drawing-in-a-project-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Export drawing in a project action**: The export drawing in a project action exports a page from a PDF in BIM 360's document management system as a new PDF file.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/get-document-in-a-project-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Get document in a project action**: The get document in a project action retrieves data about a BIM 360 document you specify.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/get-drawing-export-status-in-a-project-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Get drawing export status in a project action**: The get drawing export status in a project action retrieves data about a PDF export job in BIM 360.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/get-folder-contents-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Get folder contents action (batch)**: The get folder contents batch action retrieves the contents of a BIM 360 folder you specify.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/get-folder-info-in-a-project-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Get folder info in a project action**: The get folder info in a project action retrieves data about a BIM 360 folder you specify.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/get-issue-in-a-project-v2-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Get issue in a project (V2) action**: The get issue in a project (V2) action retrieves data about a BIM 360 issue you specify.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/get-object-in-a-project-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Get object in a project action**: The get object in a project action retrieves data about a BIM 360 object you specify.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/get-project-details-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Get project details action**: The get object in a project action retrieves data about a BIM 360 object you specify.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/new-or-updated-document-in-a-project-folder-and-subfolders-trigger.md` — **BIM 360 - New or updated document in a project folder and subfolders trigger**: The new or updated document in a project folder and subfolders trigger monitors a BIM 360 project folder and its sub-folders for newly created or updated documents.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/new-or-updated-document-in-a-project-folder-trigger.md` — **BIM 360 - New or updated document in a project folder trigger**: The new or updated document in a project folder trigger monitors a BIM 360 project folder you specify for newly created or updated documents.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/new-or-updated-issue-v2-in-a-project-trigger.md` — **BIM 360 - New or updated issue (V2) in a project trigger**: The new or updated issue (V2) in a project trigger monitors a BIM 360 project folder you specify for newly created or updated issues.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/new-or-updated-object-in-a-project-trigger.md` — **BIM 360 - New or updated object in a project trigger**: The new or updated object in a project trigger monitors a BIM 360 project folder you specify for newly created or updated objects.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/search-issues-in-a-project-v2-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Search issues in a project (V2) action (batch)**: The search issues in a project (V2) batch action retrieves a list of the issues in a BIM 360 project you specify.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/search-objects-in-a-project-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Search objects in a project action**: The search objects in a project action searches a BIM 360 project and returns a list of objects matching the type you specify.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/update-issue-in-a-project-v2-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Update issue in a project (V2) action**: The update issue in a project (V2) action updates an existing issue you specify in BIM 360.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/update-object-in-a-project-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Update object in a project action**: The update object in a project action updates an existing object you specify in BIM 360.
- `docs/connectors/bim360/upload-document-to-a-project-action.md` — **BIM 360 - Upload document to a project action**: The upload document to a project action uploads a file to a project you specify in BIM 360.
- `docs/connectors/bluesky.md` — **Bluesky**: The Bluesky connector lets you post, message, and monitor notifications on the decentralized Bluesky social network in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/bluesky/create-record-action.md` — **Bluesky - Create record action**: The Create record action enables you to create a post in Bluesky.
- `docs/connectors/bluesky/create-thread-action.md` — **Bluesky - Create thread action**: The Create thread action enables you to create a multi-post thread in Bluesky.
- `docs/connectors/bluesky/delete-record-action.md` — **Bluesky - Delete record action**: The Delete record action enables you to delete a post in Bluesky.
- `docs/connectors/bluesky/get-record-action.md` — **Bluesky - Get record action**: The Get record action enables you to retrieve a profile or post engagement by ID in Bluesky.
- `docs/connectors/bluesky/new-notification-trigger.md` — **Bluesky - New notification trigger**: The New notification trigger monitors Bluesky for new notifications, such as likes, mentions, and replies.
- `docs/connectors/bluesky/search-records-action.md` — **Bluesky - Search records action**: The Search records action enables you to search and retrieve a list of likes or followers in Bluesky.
- `docs/connectors/bluesky/send-direct-message-action.md` — **Bluesky - Send direct message action**: The Send direct message action enables you to send a Bluesky direct message.
- `docs/connectors/bluesky/upload-image-action.md` — **Bluesky - Upload image action**: The Upload image action enables you to upload an image blob for use in posts in Bluesky.
- `docs/connectors/box.md` — **Box**: The Box connector lets you automate content workflows: upload, download, and search files, manage shared links, and trigger on new or updated files.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-add-comment-file.md` — **Box - Add comment to file action**: The Add comment to file action adds a comment to a file in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-cancel-sign.md` — **Box - Cancel sign request action**: The Cancel sign request action cancels an existing sign request in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-copy-file-folder.md` — **Box - Copy file or folder action**: The Copy file or folder action copies a file or folder in Box to a target folder.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-create-collaboration.md` — **Box - Create collaboration action**: The Create collaboration action creates a collaboration on a file or folder in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-create-file-metadata.md` — **Box - Create file metadata action**: The Create file metadata action creates standard or custom metadata on a file in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-create-folder.md` — **Box - Create folder action**: The Create folder action creates a new folder in Box within a specified parent folder.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-create-sign.md` — **Box - Create sign request action**: The Create sign request action creates a sign request in Box on one or more files and sends email notifications to signers.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-delete-file-folder.md` — **Box - Delete file or folder action**: The Delete file or folder action deletes a file or folder from Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-delete-metadata.md` — **Box - Delete file metadata action**: The Delete file metadata action deletes metadata from a file in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-download-file.md` — **Box - Download file action**: The Download file action downloads the contents of a file from Box. You can use the file contents in subsequent steps, such as uploading to another application.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-file-folder-shared-link.md` — **Box - Create file shared link action**: The Create file shared link action generates a direct shared link to a file in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-folder-shared-link.md` — **Box - Create folder shared link action**: The Create folder shared link action generates a direct shared link to a folder in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-get-file-comments.md` — **Box - Get file comments (batch) action**: The Get file comments (batch) action retrieves all comments from a file in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-get-file-metadata.md` — **Box - Get file metadata action**: The Get file metadata action retrieves metadata associated with a file in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-get-file-url.md` — **Box - Get file download URL action**: The Get file download URL action retrieves the public URL of a file in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-get-sign.md` — **Box - Get sign request action**: The Get sign request action retrieves details of an existing sign request in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-list-folder-items.md` — **Box - List folder items (batch) action**: The List folder items batch action retrieves the files and folders contained within a specific folder in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-list-sign.md` — **Box - List sign requests action (batch)**: The List sign requests action (Batch) retrieves all sign requests created by a user in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-rename-file.md` — **Box - Rename/move file or folder action**: The Rename/move file or folder action renames a file, moves a file or folder to another folder, or both in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-rename-user.md` — **Box - Rename other user's file or folder action**: The Rename other user's file or folder action renames a file or folder that belongs to another user in your Box organization. Admin privileges are required.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-resend-sign.md` — **Box - Resend sign request action**: The Resend sign request action resends a sign request to all outstanding signers in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-search-files.md` — **Box - Search files or folders action (batch)**: The Search files or folders batch action in the Box connector searches for files or folders by name, description, or contents in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-update-csv-file.md` — **Box - Update CSV file in box action**: The Update CSV file action updates or appends data to an existing CSV file in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-update-file-metadata.md` — **Box - Update file metadata action**: The Update file metadata action updates metadata fields for a file in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-upload-file-contents.md` — **Box - Upload file using file contents action**: The Upload file action uploads a new file to Box or updates an existing file using file contents.
- `docs/connectors/box/action-upload-file-url.md` — **Box - Upload file using file URL action**: The Upload file using file URL action uploads a file to Box from a public URL. You can upload a new file to a folder or a new version of an existing file.
- `docs/connectors/box/trigger-csv-file-folder.md` — **Box - New CSV file in folder trigger (batch)**: The New CSV file trigger (Batch) starts a recipe when Box adds a new CSV file to a specified folder. It processes CSV lines in batches.
- `docs/connectors/box/trigger-new-csv-line.md` — **Box - New line in CSV file trigger**: The New line in CSV file trigger starts a recipe when a new line is added to a specified CSV file in Box.
- `docs/connectors/box/trigger-new-csv.md` — **Box - New/updated CSV file in folder trigger (batch)**: The New/updated CSV file trigger starts a recipe when a CSV file is added or updated in a specified Box folder. It processes CSV rows in batches.
- `docs/connectors/box/trigger-new-event-folder.md` — **Box - New event in folder trigger (real-time)**: The New event in folder real-time trigger starts a recipe when Box detects file or folder events in a specified folder.
- `docs/connectors/box/trigger-new-file.md` — **Box - New/updated file in folder trigger**: “The New/updated file trigger starts a recipe when a file is added or updated in a specified Box folder.”
- `docs/connectors/box/trigger-new-folder-in-folder.md` — **Box - New/updated folder in folder trigger**: The New/updated folder in folder trigger starts a recipe when a new folder is added or an existing folder is updated in a specified Box folder.
- `docs/connectors/box/trigger-new-sign-event.md` — **Box - New/updated sign event in folder trigger**: The New/updated sign event trigger starts a recipe when a new sign request is created or an existing sign request is updated in a specified Box folder.
- `docs/connectors/box/trigger-new-updated-metadata.md` — **Box - New/updated file metadata in folder trigger**: The New/updated file metadata in folder trigger starts a recipe when a file metadata instance is created, updated, or deleted in a specified Box folder.
- `docs/connectors/brevo.md` — **Brevo**: Brevo is a cloud-based communication platform that supports customer messaging across multiple channels.
- `docs/connectors/brevo/create-record-action.md` — **Brevo - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a new record in Brevo.
- `docs/connectors/brevo/delete-record-action.md` — **Brevo - Delete record action**: The Delete record action deletes a record in Brevo.
- `docs/connectors/brevo/send-transactional-email-action.md` — **Brevo - Send transaction email action**: The Send transaction email action sends a transactional email with static or dynamic content in Brevo.
- `docs/connectors/brevo/update-record-action.md` — **Brevo - Update record action**: The Update record action updates a record in Brevo.
- `docs/connectors/bynder.md` — **Bynder**: The Bynder connector lets you automate digital asset workflows: upload, download, search, and update assets and records, and trigger on new or updated assets.
- `docs/connectors/bynder/action-download-asset.md` — **Bynder - Download asset action**: The Download asset action in the Bynder connector downloads the contents of an asset so you can stream the file into other applications in your recipe.
- `docs/connectors/bynder/action-get-record-details-by-id.md` — **Bynder - Get record details by ID action**: The Get record details by ID action in the Bynder connector retrieves an asset, asset usage, or metaproperty record using its unique Bynder ID.
- `docs/connectors/bynder/action-search-records.md` — **Bynder - Search records action**: The Search records action in the Bynder connector searches for assets, collections, tags, metaproperties, and metaproperty options using standard fields.
- `docs/connectors/bynder/action-update-record.md` — **Bynder - Update record action**: The Update record action in the Bynder connector updates the values or content of an existing asset or collection, subject to the required security roles.
- `docs/connectors/bynder/action-upload-asset.md` — **Bynder - Upload asset action**: The Upload asset action in the Bynder connector uploads file contents as a new asset or a new version of an existing asset by its ID.
- `docs/connectors/bynder/trigger-new-asset.md` — **Bynder - New asset trigger**: The New asset trigger in the Bynder connector fires when a new asset is added to the account, checking for new assets every five minutes.
- `docs/connectors/bynder/trigger-new-updated-asset.md` — **Bynder - New/updated asset trigger**: The New/updated asset trigger in the Bynder connector fires when an asset is added or an existing asset is updated, checking every five minutes.
- `docs/connectors/calcom.md` — **Cal.com**: Cal.com is an open-source scheduling platform for booking meetings and appointments.
- `docs/connectors/calcom/cancel-booking-action.md` — **Cal.com - Cancel booking action**: The Cancel booking action cancels an existing booking by its UID in Cal.com.
- `docs/connectors/calcom/create-record-action.md` — **Cal.com - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a new booking in Cal.com.
- `docs/connectors/calcom/get-record-action.md` — **Cal.com - Get record action**: The Get record action retrieves a booking, the available time slots for an event type, or a public booking link from Cal.com.
- `docs/connectors/calcom/new-updated-booking-trigger.md` — **Cal.com - New/updated booking trigger**: The New/updated booking trigger fires when a booking is created, cancelled, or rescheduled in Cal.com.
- `docs/connectors/calcom/reschedule-booking-action.md` — **Cal.com - Reschedule booking action**: The Reschedule booking action moves an existing booking to a new time slot in Cal.com.
- `docs/connectors/calcom/search-records-action.md` — **Cal.com - Search records action**: The Search records action searches for bookings or event types that match the filters you specify in Cal.com.
- `docs/connectors/calendly.md` — **Calendly**: The Calendly connector lets you automate scheduling workflows: trigger recipes on new scheduled events and list your available event types.
- `docs/connectors/calendly/list-event-types-action.md` — **Calendly - List event types action**: The List event types action in the Calendly connector returns a paginated list of event types for an organization or user that match your search criteria.
- `docs/connectors/calendly/new-event-trigger.md` — **Calendly - New event trigger**: The New event trigger activates when trigger events, such as invitee created, occur in Calendly.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect.md` — **Canva Connect**: Canva Connect is an online template editor that helps you create social media graphics, presentations, and other designs, with collaboration features for team workspaces.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect/create-design-autofill-action.md` — **Canva Connect - Create design autofill action**: The Create design autofill action autofills a Canva Connect brand template with your data and returns the created design once the job finishes.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect/create-design-autofill-job-action.md` — **Canva Connect - Create design autofill job action**: The Create design autofill job action starts an asynchronous job that autofills a brand template with data and returns the job ID in Canva.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect/create-design-export-job-action.md` — **Canva Connect - Create design export job action**: The Create design export job action starts an asynchronous export job for a design in Canva and returns the job ID immediately, instead of waiting for the export to finish.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect/create-record-action.md` — **Canva Connect - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a new folder, design, comment, or comment reply in Canva Connect.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect/delete-record-action.md` — **Canva Connect - Delete record action**: The Delete record action deletes a folder or an asset from Canva Connect.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect/export-a-design-action.md` — **Canva Connect - Export a design action**: The Export a design action exports a design from Canva Connect to a file and returns the download URLs.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect/get-record-details-by-id-action.md` — **Canva Connect - Get record details by ID action**: The Get record details by ID action retrieves the details of a single Canva Connect record or job by its ID.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect/import-a-design-action.md` — **Canva Connect - Import a design action**: The Import a design action imports an external file as a new design in Canva Connect and returns the imported design.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect/new-event-trigger.md` — **Canva Connect - New event trigger**: The New event trigger starts a recipe when a selected webhook event occurs in Canva Connect.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect/search-records-action.md` — **Canva Connect - Search records action**: The Search records action returns a list of folder items, designs, or brand templates in Canva Connect that match your search criteria.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect/update-record-action.md` — **Canva Connect - Update record action**: The Update record action updates a folder, moves a folder item, or updates an asset in Canva Connect.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect/upload-asset-action.md` — **Canva Connect - Upload asset action**: The Upload asset action uploads a file to a user's content library in Canva Connect and returns the uploaded asset.
- `docs/connectors/canva-connect/upload-asset-from-url-action.md` — **Canva Connect - Upload asset from URL action**: The Upload asset from URL action starts an asynchronous job to upload an asset from a public URL into Canva Connect and returns the job ID.
- `docs/connectors/celonis.md` — **Celonis**: The Celonis connector lets you automate process mining workflows by triggering recipes on new events and signals from the Celonis process automation engine.
- `docs/connectors/ceridian-dayforce.md` — **Ceridian Dayforce**: Ceridian Dayforce is a global human capital management (HCM) platform that combines payroll, HR, benefits, talent, and workforce management in a single application.
- `docs/connectors/ceridian-dayforce/get-employee-action.md` — **Ceridian Dayforce - Get employee action**: The Get employee action retrieves the HR details of a specified employee in Ceridian Dayforce.
- `docs/connectors/ceridian-dayforce/new-updated-employee-trigger.md` — **Ceridian Dayforce - New/updated employee trigger**: The New/updated employee trigger activates when an employee is created or updated in Ceridian Dayforce.
- `docs/connectors/ceridian-dayforce/search-employees-action.md` — **Ceridian Dayforce - Search employees action**: The Search employees action returns a list of employees that match your search criteria in Ceridian Dayforce.
- `docs/connectors/cisco-webex-teams.md` — **Cisco Webex Teams**: The Cisco Webex Teams connector lets you automate messaging workflows: post messages, create and manage rooms, add people, and trigger on new messages.
- `docs/connectors/cisco-webex-teams/actions/add-person-to-room.md` — **Cisco Webex Teams - Add person to room action**: The Add person to room action in the Cisco Webex Teams connector lets you add a person to a room by email, optionally as a moderator.
- `docs/connectors/cisco-webex-teams/actions/create-room.md` — **Cisco Webex Teams - Create room action**: The Create room action in the Cisco Webex Teams connector lets you create a new room with a specified topic and return its details.
- `docs/connectors/cisco-webex-teams/actions/get-attachment-details.md` — **Cisco Webex Teams - Get attachment details action**: The Get attachment details action in the Cisco Webex Teams connector lets you retrieve details of a specific attachment action by its ID.
- `docs/connectors/cisco-webex-teams/actions/get-message-details.md` — **Cisco Webex Teams - Get message details action**: The Get message details action in the Cisco Webex Teams connector lets you retrieve the details of a specific message by its ID.
- `docs/connectors/cisco-webex-teams/actions/get-person-details.md` — **Cisco Webex Teams - Get person details action**: The Get person details action in the Cisco Webex Teams connector lets you retrieve details of a specific person by their unique ID.
- `docs/connectors/cisco-webex-teams/actions/get-room-details.md` — **Cisco Webex Teams - Get room details action**: The Get room details action in the Cisco Webex Teams connector lets you retrieve details of a specific room, such as its topic and creator.
- `docs/connectors/cisco-webex-teams/actions/post-message.md` — **Cisco Webex Teams - Post message action**: The Post message action in the Cisco Webex Teams connector lets you post a message to a specified room or to a specific person.
- `docs/connectors/cisco-webex-teams/actions/update-room.md` — **Cisco Webex Teams - Update room action**: The Update room action in the Cisco Webex Teams connector lets you change the topic of a specific room and return its updated details.
- `docs/connectors/cisco-webex-teams/triggers/new-button-submission.md` — **Cisco Webex Teams - New button submission trigger (real-time)**: The New button submission trigger in the Cisco Webex Teams connector fires in real time when a button is submitted, processing each as a job.
- `docs/connectors/cisco-webex-teams/triggers/new-message.md` — **Cisco Webex Teams - New message trigger (real-time)**: The New message trigger in the Cisco Webex Teams connector fires in real time when a new message is posted in a selected room.
- `docs/connectors/clarity.md` — **Clarity**: The Clarity connector lets you automate project and portfolio management workflows: search records and trigger recipes on new or updated records.
- `docs/connectors/clarity/actions/search-records.md` — **Clarity - Search records action**: The Search records action in the Clarity connector lets you search records of a selected object type using filter criteria and sorting.
- `docs/connectors/clarity/triggers/new-updated-record.md` — **Clarity - New/updated record trigger**: The New/updated record trigger in the Clarity connector monitors a selected object type and fires when records are created or updated.
- `docs/connectors/clickup.md` — **ClickUp**: The ClickUp connector enables you to automate project, task, and productivity workflows across documents, time tracking, and goals.
- `docs/connectors/clickup/create-record-action.md` — **ClickUp - Create record action**: The **Create record** action creates a record in ClickUp, such as a task. Select the object to create, then provide the data for creating the record.
- `docs/connectors/clickup/delete-record-action.md` — **ClickUp - Delete record action**: The **Delete record** action deletes the specified record from ClickUp.
- `docs/connectors/clickup/get-record-details-action.md` — **ClickUp - Get record details action**: The **Get record details** action retrieves the details of any record, such as a task, via its ID from ClickUp.
- `docs/connectors/clickup/new-event-trigger.md` — **ClickUp - New event trigger**: The **New event** trigger runs immediately as soon as trigger events, such as a task created, occur.
- `docs/connectors/clickup/new-record-trigger.md` — **ClickUp - New record trigger**: The **New record** trigger runs when a new task is created in ClickUp.
- `docs/connectors/clickup/new-updated-record-trigger.md` — **ClickUp - New/updated record trigger**: The **New/updated record** trigger runs when a task is created or updated in ClickUp.
- `docs/connectors/clickup/search-records-action.md` — **ClickUp - Search records action**: The **Search records** action returns results from ClickUp that match all your search criteria.
- `docs/connectors/clickup/update-record-action.md` — **ClickUp - Update record action**: The **Update record** action updates any record in ClickUp, such as task.
- `docs/connectors/clickup/upload-task-attachment-action.md` — **ClickUp - Upload task attachment action**: The **Upload task attachment** action allows you to upload a task attachment in ClickUp.
- `docs/connectors/coingecko.md` — **CoinGecko**: CoinGecko is an independent cryptocurrency data aggregator providing prices, market data, historical charts, and metadata for thousands of coins.
- `docs/connectors/coingecko/get-record-action.md` — **CoinGecko - Get record action**: The Get record action retrieves a single coin detail, coin price, or historical market chart record from CoinGecko.
- `docs/connectors/coingecko/list-records-action.md` — **CoinGecko - List records action**: The List records action lists coins by market data, all supported coins, or trending coins, NFTs, and categories in CoinGecko.
- `docs/connectors/coingecko/search-queries-action.md` — **CoinGecko - Search queries action**: The Search queries action returns coins, exchanges, categories, and NFTs in CoinGecko that match a free-text search term.
- `docs/connectors/concur.md` — **SAP Concur**: Connect to SAP Concur to manage travel and expense reporting, invoice processing, and user provisioning.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions.md` — **SAP Concur - Actions**: Browse the actions available in the SAP Concur connector for Workato to manage lists, list items, expense reports, and users.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/create-list-item.md` — **SAP Concur - Create list item action**: The Create list item action creates a new list item in SAP Concur.

- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/get-all-expense-types-batch.md` — **SAP Concur - Get all expense types action (batch)**: The Get all expense types batch action retrieves all expense types from SAP Concur.

- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/get-all-payment-types-batch.md` — **SAP Concur - Get all payment types action (batch)**: The Get all payment types batch action retrieves all payment types from SAP Concur.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/get-attendee-types-v4.md` — **SAP Concur - Get attendee types action (batch)**: The Get attendee types batch action retrieves attendee types for a specific user from SAP Concur using the v4 API.

- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/get-expense-group-v4.md` — **SAP Concur - Get expense group configuration action (batch)**: The Get expense group configuration batch action retrieves the expense group configuration for a specific user from SAP Concur using the v4 API.

- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/get-expense-types-v4.md` — **SAP Concur - Get expense types action**: The Get expense types action retrieves expense types for a specific user from SAP Concur using the v4 API.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/get-invoice-details.md` — **SAP Concur - Get invoice details action**: The Get invoice details action retrieves detailed information about a specific invoice in SAP Concur.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/get-itemizations-of-specific-expense.md` — **SAP Concur - Get itemizations of specific expense action**: The Get itemizations of specific expense action retrieves a list of itemization summaries for a specific expense in SAP Concur.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/get-payment-types-v4.md` — **SAP Concur - Get payment types batch action**: The Get payment types batch action retrieves payment types for a specific user from SAP Concur using the v4 API.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/get-user-provisioning-status-details.md` — **SAP Concur - Get user provisioning status details action**: The Get user provisioning status details action retrieves a detailed status report for a provisioning request.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/get-user.md` — **SAP Concur - Get user action**: The Get user action retrieves information about a specific user in SAP Concur.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/retrieve-children-of-list-item.md` — **SAP Concur - Retrieve children of list item action**: The Retrieve children of list item action retrieves child items of a specific list item in SAP Concur.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/search-expense-reports-batch.md` — **SAP Concur - Search expense reports action (batch)**: The Search expense reports batch action searches for expense reports in SAP Concur based on specified criteria.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/search-users-batch.md` — **SAP Concur - Search users action (batch)**: The Search users batch action searches for users in SAP Concur based on specified criteria.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/search-vendors-batch.md` — **SAP Concur - Search vendors action (batch)**: The Search vendors batch action searches for vendors in SAP Concur based on specified criteria.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/submit-expense-report-through-workflow.md` — **SAP Concur - Submit expense report through workflow action**: The Submit expense report through workflow action submits an expense report through the workflow in SAP Concur.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/update-user.md` — **SAP Concur - Update user action**: The Update user action updates an existing user in SAP Concur.
- `docs/connectors/concur/actions/update-users-batch.md` — **SAP Concur - Update users action (batch)**: The Update users batch action updates multiple user profiles with identity, spend, and travel attributes.

- `docs/connectors/concur/triggers.md` — **SAP Concur - Triggers**: Browse the triggers available in the SAP Concur connector for Workato to start recipes from expense report and related events.
- `docs/connectors/concur/triggers/new-expense-report-submission.md` — **SAP Concur - New expense report submission trigger**: The New expense report submission trigger activates when an expense report is first submitted or re-submitted in SAP Concur.

- `docs/connectors/concur/triggers/new-updated-expense-report.md` — **SAP Concur - New/updated expense report trigger**: The New/updated expense report trigger activates when an expense report is created or updated in SAP Concur.
- `docs/connectors/concur/triggers/new-updated-invoice.md` — **SAP Concur - New/updated invoice trigger**: The New/updated invoice trigger activates when an invoice is created or updated in SAP Concur.
- `docs/connectors/concur/triggers/new-updated-user.md` — **SAP Concur - New/updated user trigger**: The New/updated user trigger activates when a user is created or updated in SAP Concur.
- `docs/connectors/confluence.md` — **Confluence**: The Confluence connector lets you automate knowledge management workflows: create pages and tasks and search for existing pages.
- `docs/connectors/confluence/create-page.md` — **Confluence - Create page action**: The Create page action in the Confluence connector lets you create a new page in a specified space, returning its ID, status, and content details.
- `docs/connectors/confluence/create-task.md` — **Confluence - Create task action**: The Create task action in the Confluence connector lets you add a new task to a specified page, including assignee, status, and due date.
- `docs/connectors/confluence/search-pages.md` — **Confluence - Search pages action**: The Search pages action in the Confluence connector lets you find pages by keyword, space name, or page title and return matching page details.
- `docs/connectors/confluent-cloud.md` — **Confluent Cloud**: The Confluent Cloud connector lets you automate Kafka streaming workflows: publish messages to a topic and trigger recipes on new messages in a topic.
- `docs/connectors/confluent-cloud/new-message-trigger.md` — **Confluent Cloud - New message in topic trigger**: The New message in topic trigger in the Confluent Cloud connector fires for each new message created in a topic, processing every message as a separate job.
- `docs/connectors/confluent-cloud/new-messages-batch-trigger.md` — **Confluent Cloud - New messages in topic trigger (batch)**: The New messages in topic trigger (batch) in the Confluent Cloud connector picks up new messages from a topic in batches on each poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/confluent-cloud/publish-message-action.md` — **Confluent Cloud - Publish message action**: The Publish message action in the Confluent Cloud connector lets you send a message to a topic you specify, with optional key and partition settings.
- `docs/connectors/conga-composer.md` — **Conga Composer**: Conga Composer is a Salesforce application that allows users to create and deliver pixel-perfect documents, presentations, and reports using data from Salesforce. Conga Composer generates customized documents using templates.
- `docs/connectors/conga-composer/compose-document-action.md` — **Conga Composer - Compose document action**: The Compose document action generates a document by merging one or more templates with data collected from multiple sources in Conga Composer.
- `docs/connectors/conga-composer/download-document-action.md` — **Conga Composer - Download document action**: The Download document action downloads a document that you specify in Conga Composer.
- `docs/connectors/conga.md` — **Conga**: The Conga connector lets you automate document workflows: create, update, search, and query records, generate and download documents, and send email.
- `docs/connectors/conga/create-record-action.md` — **Conga - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a new record in Conga, such as a new contact.
- `docs/connectors/conga/download-document-based-on-document-id-action.md` — **Conga - Download document based on ID action**: The Download document based on ID action downloads a document based on the Conga ID.
- `docs/connectors/conga/generate-a-document-record-action.md` — **Conga - Generate a document record action**: Generate a document record
- `docs/connectors/conga/get-record-by-id-action.md` — **Conga - Get record by ID action**: The Get record by ID action retrieves a record by using the ID in Conga.
- `docs/connectors/conga/new-updated-record-trigger.md` — **Conga - New/updated record trigger**: The New/updated record trigger monitors Conga for new or updated records.
- `docs/connectors/conga/query-records-action.md` — **Conga - Query records action**: The Query records action searches for records in Conga.
- `docs/connectors/conga/search-records-action.md` — **Conga - Search records action**: The Search records action searches for records in Conga.
- `docs/connectors/conga/send-email-action.md` — **Conga - Send email action**: The Send email action sends an email in Conga.
- `docs/connectors/conga/update-record-action.md` — **Conga - Update record action**: The Update record action updates an existing record in Conga.
- `docs/connectors/cornerstone-hr.md` — **Cornerstone HR**: Cornerstone is a cloud-based human capital management (HCM) platform for learning and development, performance management, recruiting, and compliance.
- `docs/connectors/cornerstone-hr/cancel-goal-action.md` — **Cornerstone HR - Cancel goal action**: The Cancel goal action updates a goal's status to cancelled in Cornerstone.
- `docs/connectors/cornerstone-hr/create-record-action.md` — **Cornerstone HR - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a record, such as a user, in Cornerstone.
- `docs/connectors/cornerstone-hr/get-record-details-by-id-action.md` — **Cornerstone HR - Get record details by ID action**: The Get record details by ID action retrieves the details of a record, such as a user, by its ID in Cornerstone.

- `docs/connectors/cornerstone-hr/new-record-batch-trigger.md` — **Cornerstone HR - New record batch trigger**: The New record batch trigger activates when records, such as users, are created in Cornerstone, and returns them in batches.
- `docs/connectors/cornerstone-hr/new-record-trigger.md` — **Cornerstone HR - New record trigger**: The New record trigger activates when a record, such as a user, is created in Cornerstone.
- `docs/connectors/cornerstone-hr/new-updated-record-batch-trigger.md` — **Cornerstone HR - New/updated record batch trigger**: The New/updated record batch trigger activates when records, such as users, are created or updated in Cornerstone, and returns them in batches.
- `docs/connectors/cornerstone-hr/new-updated-record-trigger.md` — **Cornerstone HR - New/updated record trigger**: The New/updated record trigger activates when a record, such as a user, is created or updated in Cornerstone.
- `docs/connectors/cornerstone-hr/new-webhook-event-trigger.md` — **Cornerstone HR - New webhook event (real-time) trigger**: The New webhook event trigger activates when Cornerstone sends a webhook notification for an event type you select, such as training completed or employee created.
- `docs/connectors/cornerstone-hr/search-records-action.md` — **Cornerstone HR - Search records action**: The Search records action searches for records that match your criteria in a Cornerstone object you select.
- `docs/connectors/cornerstone-hr/search-records-using-odata-query-action.md` — **Cornerstone HR - Search records using OData query action**: The Search records using OData query action returns Cornerstone records that match an OData query you provide.
- `docs/connectors/cornerstone-hr/update-record-action.md` — **Cornerstone HR - Update record action**: The Update record action updates a record, such as a user, in Cornerstone.
- `docs/connectors/coupa.md` — **Coupa**: The Coupa connector lets you automate spend management: create and search objects, manage purchase orders and suppliers, and trigger on object changes.
- `docs/connectors/coupa/integration-action.md` — **Coupa - Integration actions**: The Set integration run status action in the Coupa connector lets you update the status of an existing integration run by its run ID.
- `docs/connectors/coupa/object-actions.md` — **Coupa - Object actions**: Use the Coupa connector object actions to create, update, search, and retrieve objects such as invoices, suppliers, and purchase orders in Coupa.
- `docs/connectors/coupa/object-triggers.md` — **Coupa - Object triggers**: The New/updated object trigger in the Coupa connector polls your instance and fires when objects are created or updated, with optional trigger conditions.
- `docs/connectors/coupa/purchase-order-actions.md` — **Coupa - Purchase order actions**: Use the Coupa connector purchase order actions to close or cancel a purchase order in Coupa by its internal Coupa ID.
- `docs/connectors/coupa/supplier-actions.md` — **Coupa - Supplier actions**: The Get supplier sites by supplier action in the Coupa connector lets you retrieve all supplier sites associated with a supplier you specify.
- `docs/connectors/creatio.md` — **Creatio**: The Creatio connector lets you automate CRM workflows: create, update, delete, and search records, manage file attachments, and trigger on new events.
- `docs/connectors/creatio/create-record-action.md` — **Creatio - Create record action**: The Create record action enables you to create a standard record in Creatio.
