- `docs/connectors/creatio/delete-record-action.md` — **Creatio - Delete record action**: The Delete record action enables you to delete a record in Creatio.
- `docs/connectors/creatio/download-file-action.md` — **Creatio - Download file action**: The Download file action enables you to download a file from Creatio.
- `docs/connectors/creatio/get-record-action.md` — **Creatio - Get record action**: The Get record action enables you to retrieve the details of a standard record, for example, Account, using its ID.
- `docs/connectors/creatio/list-attachments-action.md` — **Creatio - List attachments action**: The List attachment action returns all attachments in the specified entity.
- `docs/connectors/creatio/new-event-trigger.md` — **Creatio - New event trigger**: The New event trigger runs when a new event is detected in Creatio.
- `docs/connectors/creatio/search-record-action.md` — **Creatio - Search records action**: The Search records action returns results that match all your search criteria.
- `docs/connectors/creatio/update-record-action.md` — **Creatio - Update record action**: The Update record action enables you to update any standard record, for example, Account using its ID.
- `docs/connectors/creatio/upload-file-action.md` — **Creatio - Upload file action**: The Upload file action enables you to upload a file using file streaming in Creatio.
- `docs/connectors/creatomate.md` — **Creatomate**: The Creatomate connector lets you render videos, images, and GIFs from templates or RenderScript, and manage templates in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/creatomate/create-record-action.md` — **Creatomate - Create record action**: The Create record action enables you to create a template in Creatomate.
- `docs/connectors/creatomate/create-render-action.md` — **Creatomate - Create render action**: The Create render action enables you to create a render from a template or RenderScript and wait for it to complete in Creatomate.
- `docs/connectors/creatomate/delete-record-action.md` — **Creatomate - Delete record action**: The Delete record action enables you to delete a template by ID in Creatomate.
- `docs/connectors/creatomate/get-record-action.md` — **Creatomate - Get record action**: The Get record action enables you to retrieve a template by ID in Creatomate.
- `docs/connectors/creatomate/get-render-status-action.md` — **Creatomate - Get render status action**: The Get render status action enables you to retrieve the status of a render by ID in Creatomate.
- `docs/connectors/creatomate/search-records-action.md` — **Creatomate - Search records action**: The Search records action enables you to search templates in your Creatomate project.
- `docs/connectors/creatomate/update-record-action.md` — **Creatomate - Update record action**: The Update record action enables you to update a template by ID in Creatomate.
- `docs/connectors/csv-by-workato.md` — **CSV tools by Workato**: CSV tools by Workato is a utility connector that parses, transforms, and generates CSV files directly in recipes without external services.
- `docs/connectors/csv-by-workato/compose-csv-action.md` — **CSV tools by Workato - Compose CSV action (batch)**: This action converts a list of objects into a CSV-formatted string for export or storage.
- `docs/connectors/csv-by-workato/parse-csv-action.md` — **CSV tools by Workato - Parse CSV action (batch)**: This action parses a CSV-formatted string into an array of lines for use in recipes.
- `docs/connectors/database-common-use-cases.md` — **Database - Common use cases**: Explore common Workato database use cases, from data exports and replication to building data pipelines into data lakes and warehouses.
- `docs/connectors/databases/best-practices.md` — **Database Connector Best Practices**: Best practices for building Workato recipes with database connectors, covering recipe design patterns, table design, unique keys, and data validation.
- `docs/connectors/databricks.md` — **Databricks**: The Databricks connector provides tools that allow you to maintain data solutions at scale.
- `docs/connectors/databricks/delete-rows.md` — **Databricks – Delete rows in table action**: The Delete rows in table action removes one or more rows from a selected Databricks table.
- `docs/connectors/databricks/export-result.md` — **Databricks – Export query result as CSV file action**: The Export query result as CSV file action executes a SQL query and writes the result to a CSV file.
- `docs/connectors/databricks/insert-row.md` — **Databricks – Insert row in table action**: The Insert row in table action adds a new row to a selected Databricks table.
- `docs/connectors/databricks/new-row-custom-sql-trigger.md` — **Databricks - New rows using custom SQL (batch) trigger**: The New rows using custom SQL (batch) trigger in the Databricks connector picks up rows matching your SQL query as a batch each poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/databricks/new-row-trigger.md` — **Databricks - New rows (batch) trigger**: The New rows (batch) trigger in the Databricks connector picks up rows inserted in a selected table or view, processed as a batch each poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/databricks/new-updated-rows-custom-sql-trigger.md` — **Databricks - New/updated rows via custom SQL (batch) trigger**: The New/updated rows via custom SQL (batch) trigger in the Databricks connector picks up new and updated rows matching your SQL query each poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/databricks/run-custom-sql.md` — **Databricks - Run custom SQL action**: The Run custom SQL action in the Databricks connector lets you run DML and DDL statements such as INSERT, UPDATE, SELECT, DELETE, CREATE, and DROP.
- `docs/connectors/databricks/select-rows-custom-sql-action.md` — **Databricks - Select rows via custom SQL action**: The Select rows via custom SQL action in the Databricks connector lets you return rows from a custom SQL query, with ordering and row quotas.
- `docs/connectors/databricks/select-rows.md` — **Databricks - Select rows action**: The Select rows action in the Databricks connector lets you return rows from a table that match a WHERE condition, with ordering and quotas.
- `docs/connectors/databricks/update-rows.md` — **Databricks – Update rows in table action**: The Update rows in table action modifies one or more rows in a selected Databricks table.
- `docs/connectors/databricks/upload-file-to-internal-stage.md` — **Databricks – Upload file to volume action**: The Upload file to volume action uploads a file to a specified Databricks volume.
- `docs/connectors/datadog.md` — **Datadog**: The Datadog connector connects your recipes with real-time metrics, traces, and logs from Datadog.
- `docs/connectors/datadog/new-event-trigger.md` — **Datadog - New event trigger (real-time)**: The New event trigger uses webhooks to monitor Datadog for events in real-time.
- `docs/connectors/demo-apps.md` — **Demo apps**: Demo apps are simulated Workato connectors that let you learn and test recipe-building concepts without live connections or real data.
- `docs/connectors/deputy.md` — **Deputy**: The Deputy connector lets you automate workforce management: create, update, and search employees, create tasks, and trigger on new leave or timesheets.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/associate-employee-action.md` — **Deputy - Associate employee action**: The Associate employee action in the Deputy connector links an employee to an existing employee workplace and optionally an employee agreement.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/create-employee-action.md` — **Deputy - Create employee action**: The Create employee action in the Deputy connector creates a new employee record with a name, company, role, and contact details.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/create-resource-action.md` — **Deputy - Create resource action**: The Create resource action in the Deputy connector creates a resource such as an employee agreement, employee workplace, or training record.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/create-sales-data-action.md` — **Deputy - Create sales data action**: The Create sales data action in the Deputy connector creates a new sales data record with a transaction date, area, sales reference, and amount.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/create-task-action.md` — **Deputy - Create task action**: The Create task action in the Deputy connector creates a new task record with a question, start and end dates, and a responsible user.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/get-resource-action.md` — **Deputy - Get resource action**: The Get resource action in the Deputy connector retrieves a resource such as an employee, agreement, workplace, or training record.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/new-employee-trigger.md` — **Deputy - New employee trigger**: The New employee trigger in the Deputy connector fires when new employee records are created in your Deputy instance, picking them up on each poll.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/new-leave-trigger.md` — **Deputy - New leave trigger**: The New leave trigger in the Deputy connector fires when new leave records are created in your Deputy instance, picking them up on each poll.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/new-timesheet-trigger.md` — **Deputy - New timesheet trigger**: The New timesheet trigger in the Deputy connector fires when new timesheet records are created in your Deputy instance, picking them up on each poll.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/search-employees-action.md` — **Deputy - Search employees action**: The Search employees action in the Deputy connector finds employees that match a given employee display name.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/search-operational-units-action.md` — **Deputy - Search operational units action**: The Search operational units action in the Deputy connector finds operational units that match a given operational unit name.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/search-resources-action.md` — **Deputy - Search resources action**: The Search resources action in the Deputy connector finds employee agreements, workplaces, or training records matching your search conditions.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/unassociate-employee-action.md` — **Deputy - Unassociate employee action**: The Unassociate employee action in the Deputy connector removes the link between an employee and an existing employee workplace.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/update-employee-action.md` — **Deputy - Update employee action**: The Update employee action in the Deputy connector updates an existing employee record, including name, role, contact details, and date of birth.
- `docs/connectors/deputy/update-resource-action.md` — **Deputy - Update resource action**: The Update resource action in the Deputy connector updates a resource such as an employee agreement, employee workplace, or training record.
- `docs/connectors/dialogflow.md` — **Google Dialogflow**: Connect Google Dialogflow to Workato to power Workbot with natural language understanding for conversational interfaces and chatbots.
- `docs/connectors/discord.md` — **Discord**: Connect Discord to Workato to automate workflows with your communications platform.
- `docs/connectors/discord/add-guild-member-role-action.md` — **Discord - Add Guild member role action**: The Add Guild member role action assigns a role to a guild member in Discord.
- `docs/connectors/discord/create-record-action.md` — **Discord - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a record in Discord.
- `docs/connectors/discord/get-record-details-by-id-action.md` — **Discord - Get record details by ID action**: The Get record details by ID action retrieves a specific record with its ID in Discord.
- `docs/connectors/discord/list-records-action.md` — **Discord - List records action**: The List records action retrieves a list of records in Discord.
- `docs/connectors/docusign.md` — **Docusign**: Connect to Docusign to send, sign, and manage digital documents and eSignature workflows.
- `docs/connectors/docusign/action-create-draft-envelope-from-template.md` — **Docusign - Create draft envelope from template action**: The Create draft envelope from template action in the Docusign connector lets you create a draft envelope based on a template you configure.
- `docs/connectors/docusign/action-create-recipient-view.md` — **Docusign - Create recipient view action**: The Create recipient view action in the Docusign connector generates a URL for embedded signing of a Docusign envelope.
- `docs/connectors/docusign/action-create-send-document.md` — **Docusign - Create/send document action**: The Create/send document action in the Docusign connector lets you create and send a document for signing, with signers, routing, and signature tabs.
- `docs/connectors/docusign/action-create-template.md` — **Docusign - Create template action**: The Create template action creates a new template in your Docusign account.
- `docs/connectors/docusign/action-download-document.md` — **Docusign - Download document action**: The Download document action in the Docusign connector lets you download a document from an envelope to a specified location.
- `docs/connectors/docusign/action-get-envelope-form-data.md` — **Docusign - Get envelope form data action**: The Get envelope form data action returns tab values entered by recipients on a completed envelope.
- `docs/connectors/docusign/action-get-envelope-recipients.md` — **Docusign - Get envelope recipients action**: The Get envelope recipients action in the Docusign connector lets you retrieve the list of recipients for a given envelope.
- `docs/connectors/docusign/action-get-envelope.md` — **Docusign - Get envelope action**: The Get envelope action in the Docusign connector lets you retrieve the details of a single envelope by its ID.

- `docs/connectors/firecrawl/crawl-a-site-action.md` — **Firecrawl - Crawl a site action**: Follow links from a starting URL to scrape an entire site, then check progress with Get crawl job status.
- `docs/connectors/firecrawl/extract-data-action.md` — **Firecrawl - Extract data action**: Send a prompt to Firecrawl's Agent to pull structured data from the web, polling automatically until the job completes.
- `docs/connectors/firecrawl/get-crawl-job-status-action.md` — **Firecrawl - Get crawl job status action**: Check on a crawl you started without waiting for it to finish, and page through large result sets with the Skip cursor.
- `docs/connectors/firecrawl/get-extract-job-status-action.md` — **Firecrawl - Get extract job status action**: Check the status of an Agent extraction job started by the Extract data action, using the job ID it returned.
- `docs/connectors/firecrawl/map-a-site-action.md` — **Firecrawl - Map a site action**: List every URL Firecrawl discovers on a domain, optionally ordered by relevance to a search query.
- `docs/connectors/firecrawl/scrape-a-page-action.md` — **Firecrawl - Scrape a page action**: Fetch a single webpage's content in the format you select, from clean markdown to AI-ready structured JSON.
- `docs/connectors/firecrawl/search-the-web-action.md` — **Firecrawl - Search the web action**: Query the live web the same way a search engine would, and optionally scrape the full content of each result.
- `docs/connectors/firecrawl/track-page-changes-action.md` — **Firecrawl - Track page changes action**: Get an early warning when a monitored page changes, with a status of new, same, changed, or removed.
- `docs/connectors/freshbooks.md` — **FreshBooks**: [FreshBooks](https://www.freshbooks.com/) is a cloud-based accounting software platform designed for small businesses and freelancers. It provides tools for invoicing, expense tracking, time tracking, project management, and financial reporting to help businesses manage their finances efficiently.
- `docs/connectors/freshbooks/create-record-action.md` — **FreshBooks - Create record action**: The Create record action enables you to create a new record in FreshBooks.
- `docs/connectors/freshbooks/get-record-by-id-action.md` — **FreshBooks - Get record by ID action**: The Get record by ID action enables you to retrieve details of a specific record in FreshBooks.
- `docs/connectors/freshbooks/list-records-action.md` — **FreshBooks - List records action**: The List records action enables you to retrieve a list of records in FreshBooks.
- `docs/connectors/freshbooks/new-updated-record-trigger.md` — **FreshBooks - New/updated record trigger**: The New/updated record trigger runs the recipe when there is a new or updated record in FreshBooks.
- `docs/connectors/freshbooks/update-record-action.md` — **FreshBooks - Update record action**: The Update record action enables you to update the details of a specific record in FreshBooks.
- `docs/connectors/freshdesk.md` — **Freshdesk**: Connect Freshdesk to Workato to automate customer support and ticketing workflows, syncing tickets and contacts across your other apps.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice.md` — **Freshservice**: Connect Freshservice to Workato to automate IT service management workflows, streamlining service delivery across your ITIL-based processes.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/create-agent-action.md` — **Freshservice - Create agent action**: This action allows you to create a new agent in Freshservice.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/create-incident-action.md` — **Freshservice - Create incident action**: This action enables you to create tickets of type **Incident**.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/create-onboarding-request-action.md` — **Freshservice - Create onboarding request action**: This action creates employee onboarding request in Freshservice.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/create-requester-action.md` — **Freshservice - Create requester action**: Creates a requester in Freshservice by providing Primary email, Work phone number, or Mobile phone number - one of the three is mandatory to create a requester.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/create-service-request-action.md` — **Freshservice - Create service request action**: Creates a new service request in Freshservice.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/create-task-action.md` — **Freshservice - Create task action**: This action enables you to create a task in a ticket, in Freshservice.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/create-ticket-action.md` — **Freshservice - Create ticket action**: This action allows you to create a ticket in Freshservice.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/delete-task-action.md` — **Freshservice - Delete task action**: Delete task action deletes the tasks in Freshservice.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/get-agent-details-action.md` — **Freshservice - Get agent details action**: This action enables you to retrieve details of an agent based on the agent’s ID.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/get-requester-details-action.md` — **Freshservice - Get requester details action**: The get requester details action allows you to retrieve the requester’s details based on the requester ID.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/get-task-by-id-action.md` — **Freshservice - Get task by ID action**: The action allows you to retrieve the details of a task based on the task ID.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/get-ticket-by-id-action.md` — **Freshservice - Get ticket by ID action**: The action allows you to retrieve the details of a ticket based on the ticket ID.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/list-agent-fields-action.md` — **Freshservice - List agent fields action**: The List agent fields action lists all agent fields in Freshservice.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/list-onboarding-form-fields-action.md` — **Freshservice - List onboarding form fields action**: Lists the onboarding form fields of Freshservice.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/list-requester-fields-action.md` — **Freshservice - List requester fields action**: Lists the requester fields of Freshservice.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/list-service-item-action.md` — **Freshservice - List service items action**: Returns a list of service items based on specified filters.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/list-tasks-action.md` — **Freshservice - List tasks action**: Lists the tasks of a ticket in Freshservice based on ticket ID.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/list-ticket-form-fields-action.md` — **Freshservice - List ticket form fields action**: Returns a list of ticket form fields in Freshservice.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/list-tickets-action.md` — **Freshservice - List tickets action**: Lists the tickets in Freshservice. By default only tickets that have been created within the past 30 days will be returned.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/move-ticket-action.md` — **Freshservice - Move ticket action**: This action allows you to move your ticket to some other workspace while also allowing the assigned group and agent to be modified.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/new-ticket-trigger.md` — **Freshservice - New ticket trigger**: Triggers when a new ticket is created in Freshservice. Checks for the new events every minute.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/new-updated-ticket-trigger.md` — **Freshservice - New/updated ticket trigger**: Triggers when a ticket is created or updated in Freshservice. Checks for the new events every minute.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/restore-ticket-action.md` — **Freshservice - Restore ticket action**: If you deleted some tickets and regret doing so now, this action will help you restore them.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/search-agents-action.md` — **Freshservice - Search agents action**: The Search action will return results that match all your search criteria. Returns a maximum of 30 records.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/search-requester-action.md` — **Freshservice - Search requester action**: The Search action will return results that match all your search criteria. Returns a maximum of 30 records.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/search-tickets-action.md` — **Freshservice - Search tickets action**: The Search action will return results that match all your search criteria. Returns a maximum of 30 records.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/update-requester-action.md` — **Freshservice - Update requester action**: The update action allows you to update the requester information in Freshservice.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/update-task-action.md` — **Freshservice - Update task action**: The update action allows you to update the task in a ticket, in Freshservice.
- `docs/connectors/freshservice/update-ticket-action.md` — **Freshservice - Update ticket action**: The update action allows you to update a ticket in Freshservice.
- `docs/connectors/ftp.md` — **FTP/FTPS**: The FTP/FTPS connector enables you to automate file operations on an FTP or FTPS server, including downloading, uploading, renaming, and removing files.
- `docs/connectors/ftp/download-file-action.md` — **FTP/FTPS - Download file action**: The Download file action retrieves a file from your server.
- `docs/connectors/ftp/download-large-file-action.md` — **FTP/FTPS - Download large file action**: The Download large file action retrieves a large file from your server.
- `docs/connectors/ftp/get-file-information-action.md` — **FTP/FTPS - Get file information action**: The Get file information action retrieves information about a file in the connected server.
- `docs/connectors/ftp/list-files-directories-action.md` — **FTP/FTPS - List files and directories action**: This action retrieves the first level of a directory from the connected server.
- `docs/connectors/ftp/new-csv-file-in-directory-trigger.md` — **FTP/FTPS - New CSV file added in directory on FTP/FTPS server (batch)**: This trigger monitors a server directory for newly added CSV files.
- `docs/connectors/ftp/new-updated-file-in-directory-trigger.md` — **FTP/FTPS - New/updated file in directory trigger**: This trigger monitors a server directory for new or updated files.
- `docs/connectors/ftp/remove-file-action.md` — **FTP/FTPS - Remove file action**: This action removes a file from the connected server.
- `docs/connectors/ftp/rename-file-action.md` — **FTP/FTPS - Rename file action**: This action renames a file on the connected server.
- `docs/connectors/ftp/upload-file-action.md` — **FTP/FTPS - Upload file action**: This action uploads a file to a folder you specify in the connected server.
- `docs/connectors/gainsight-nxt.md` — **Gainsight NXT**: Connect Gainsight NXT to Workato to automate customer success workflows across your company and person records.
- `docs/connectors/gainsight-nxt/create-company-action.md` — **Gainsight NXT - Create company record/s action**: The Create company record/s action creates one or more company records in Gainsight NXT.
- `docs/connectors/gainsight-nxt/get-record-details-action.md` — **Gainsight NXT - Get record details action**: The Get record details action retrieves the details of a record in Gainsight NXT.
- `docs/connectors/gainsight-nxt/get-upsert-request-status-action.md` — **Gainsight NXT - Get upsert request status action**: The Get upsert request status action retrieves the processing status of a bulk person upsert request in Gainsight NXT by status ID.
- `docs/connectors/gainsight-nxt/new-record-trigger.md` — **Gainsight NXT - New record trigger**: The New record trigger monitors Gainsight NXT for new records created within a Gainsight NXT object you select, such as a company or person.
- `docs/connectors/gainsight-nxt/new-records-in-batch-trigger.md` — **Gainsight NXT - New records in batch trigger**: The New records in batch trigger monitors Gainsight NXT for records created since the last time the recipe triggered.
- `docs/connectors/gainsight-nxt/new-updated-record-trigger.md` — **Gainsight NXT - New/updated record trigger**: The New/updated record trigger monitors a Gainsight NXT object you select for new or updated objects, such as a company or person.
- `docs/connectors/gainsight-nxt/new-updated-records-in-batch-trigger.md` — **Gainsight NXT - New/updated records in batch trigger**: The New/updated records in batch trigger monitors Gainsight NXT for records created or updated since the last time the recipe triggered.
- `docs/connectors/gainsight-nxt/search-records-action.md` — **Gainsight NXT - Search records action**: The Search records action fetches one or more records that match specific criteria in Gainsight NXT.
- `docs/connectors/gainsight-nxt/update-company-action.md` — **Gainsight NXT - Update company record/s action**: The Update company record/s action updates up to 50 records in the Company object in Gainsight NXT.
- `docs/connectors/gainsight-nxt/upsert-person-action.md` — **Gainsight NXT - Upsert person action**: The Upsert person action upserts a single person record in Gainsight NXT.
- `docs/connectors/gainsight-nxt/upsert-persons-in-bulk-action.md` — **Gainsight NXT - Upsert persons in bulk action**: The Upsert persons in bulk action upserts up to 50 records in the Person object in Gainsight NXT.
- `docs/connectors/gcp-biglake-iceberg.md` — **GCP BigLake Iceberg**: The GCP BigLake Iceberg connector integrates with Apache Iceberg tables managed by the BigLake Metastore on Google Cloud.
- `docs/connectors/gcp-biglake-iceberg/create-table-action.md` — **GCP BigLake Iceberg - Create table action**: The Create table action registers a new table in a GCP BigLake Iceberg database.
- `docs/connectors/gcp-biglake-iceberg/get-table-definitions-action.md` — **GCP BigLake Iceberg - Get table definitions action**: The Get table definitions action retrieves the definition of a table registered in GCP BigLake Iceberg.
- `docs/connectors/gcp-biglake-iceberg/import-data-action.md` — **GCP BigLake Iceberg - Import data action**: The Import data action loads data from Cloud Storage files into a GCP BigLake Iceberg table using a BigQuery LOAD DATA job.
- `docs/connectors/gcp-biglake-iceberg/list-tables-action.md` — **GCP BigLake Iceberg - List tables action**: The List tables action lists all tables in the specified GCP BigLake Iceberg database.
- `docs/connectors/gcp-biglake-iceberg/query-data-action.md` — **GCP BigLake Iceberg - Query data action**: The Query data action runs a SQL query against GCP BigLake Iceberg tables using a BigQuery query job.
- `docs/connectors/ghost.md` — **Ghost**: The Ghost connector lets you manage posts and members, upload images, and trigger recipes on Ghost webhook events in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/ghost/create-record-action.md` — **Ghost - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a post or member in Ghost.
- `docs/connectors/ghost/get-record-action.md` — **Ghost - Get record action**: The Get record action retrieves a post or member in Ghost.
- `docs/connectors/ghost/new-event-trigger.md` — **Ghost - New event trigger**: The New event trigger monitors a Ghost post or member for webhook events.
- `docs/connectors/ghost/search-records-action.md` — **Ghost - Search records action**: The Search records action searches and retrieves a list of posts or members in Ghost.
- `docs/connectors/ghost/update-record-action.md` — **Ghost - Update record action**: The Update record action updates a post in Ghost.
- `docs/connectors/ghost/upload-image-action.md` — **Ghost - Upload image action**: The Upload image action uploads an image to Ghost and returns a hosted URL.
- `docs/connectors/github.md` — **GitHub**: The GitHub connector enables you to build powerful automation around GitHub to help manage these software development processes and manage your agile sprints.
- `docs/connectors/github/closed-issue-trigger.md` — **GitHub - Closed issue trigger**: The Closed issue trigger monitors a GitHub repository and triggers when an issue is closed.
- `docs/connectors/github/create-comment-action.md` — **GitHub - Create comment in an issue action**: The Create comment in an issue action creates a comment in an issue in GitHub.
- `docs/connectors/github/create-issue-action.md` — **GitHub - Create issue action**: The Create issue action creates a new issue in GitHub.
- `docs/connectors/github/get-issue-pr-details-action.md` — **GitHub - Get issue or PR details action**: The Get issue or PR details action retrieves details of an issue or pull request.
- `docs/connectors/github/list-statuses-for-ref-action.md` — **GitHub - List statuses for a ref (batch) action**: The List statuses for a ref (batch) action lists all statuses associated with a reference (ref) in GitHub. A ref is a commit SHA, branch name, or tag in GitHub.
- `docs/connectors/github/new-issue-trigger.md` — **GitHub - New issue trigger**: The New issue trigger monitors a GitHub repository and triggers when a new issue is created.
- `docs/connectors/github/new-pr-trigger.md` — **GitHub - New PR trigger**: The New PR trigger monitors a GitHub repository and triggers when a new PR is created.
- `docs/connectors/github/new-updated-issue-comment-trigger.md` — **GitHub - New or updated issue comment (real-time) trigger**: The New or updated issue comment (real-time) trigger monitors a GitHub repository and triggers when a comment on an issue is created or updated.
- `docs/connectors/github/new-updated-issue-trigger.md` — **GitHub - New or updated issue (real-time) trigger**: The New or updated issue (real-time) trigger monitors a GitHub repository and triggers when an issue is created or updated.
- `docs/connectors/github/new-updated-milestone-trigger.md` — **GitHub - New or updated milestone (real-time) trigger**: The New or updated milestone (real-time) trigger monitors a GitHub repository and triggers when a milestone is created or updated.
- `docs/connectors/github/new-updated-pr-trigger.md` — **GitHub - New or updated pull request (real-time) trigger**: The New or updated pull request (real-time) trigger monitors a GitHub repository and triggers when a pull request is created or updated.
- `docs/connectors/github/search-issues-pr-action.md` — **GitHub - Search issues and pull requests (batch) action**: The Search issues and pull requests (batch) action searches for a issues or pull requests in a repository.
- `docs/connectors/github/update-issue-action.md` — **GitHub - Update issue action**: The update issue action updates a selected issue in GitHub.
- `docs/connectors/gitlab.md` — **GitLab**: The GitLab connector lets you automate tools and features to manage Git repositories.
- `docs/connectors/gitlab/archive-unarchive-record-action.md` — **GitLab - Archive/Unarchive record action**: The Archive/Unarchive record action archives or unarchives a record in GitLab.
- `docs/connectors/gitlab/assign-issue-to-epic-action.md` — **GitLab - Assign an issue to epic action**: The Assign an issue to epic action assigns an issue to an epic in GitLab.
- `docs/connectors/gitlab/create-record-action.md` — **GitLab - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a record in GitLab.
- `docs/connectors/gitlab/delete-record-action.md` — **GitLab - Delete record action**: The Delete record action deletes a record in GitLab.
- `docs/connectors/gitlab/get-record-details-by-id-action.md` — **GitLab - Get record details by ID action**: The Get record details by ID action retrieves record details using the record ID in GitLab.
- `docs/connectors/gitlab/get-time-logs-action.md` — **GitLab - Get time logs action**: The Get time logs action retrieves the details of time logs in GitLab.
- `docs/connectors/gitlab/new-event-trigger-real-time.md` — **GitLab - New event trigger (real-time)**: The New event trigger immediately detects new events in GitLab.
- `docs/connectors/gitlab/new-updated-pipeline-trigger.md` — **GitLab - New/updated pipeline trigger**: The New/updated pipeline trigger monitors GitLab and triggers when a pipeline is created or updated.
- `docs/connectors/gitlab/search-records-action.md` — **GitLab - Search records action**: The Search records action returns all records that match your search criteria.
- `docs/connectors/gitlab/update-record-action.md` — **GitLab - Update record action**: The Update record action updates a record in GitLab.
- `docs/connectors/glean.md` — **Glean**: The Glean connector lets you automate enterprise search workflows: create, update, delete, search, and get records to power knowledge retrieval.
- `docs/connectors/glean/create-record-action.md` — **Glean - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a new record in Glean.
- `docs/connectors/glean/delete-record-action.md` — **Glean - Delete record action**: The Delete record action deletes a Glean record you specify.
- `docs/connectors/glean/execute-operation-action.md` — **Glean - Execute operation action**: The Execute operation action performs an operation in Glean.
- `docs/connectors/glean/get-record-details-by-id-action.md` — **Glean - Get record details by ID action**: The Get record details by ID action retrieves the details of a Glean record you specify.
- `docs/connectors/glean/search-records-action.md` — **Glean - Search records action**: The Search records action in the Glean connector lets you retrieve a list of records that match the search criteria you specify.
- `docs/connectors/glean/update-record-action.md` — **Glean - Update record action**: The Update record action updates the details of a Glean record you specify.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom.md` — **Gmail (Custom)**: Gmail (Custom) uses a Google service account to manage Gmail settings and messages on behalf of a domain user.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/create-email-delegation-access-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - Create email delegation access action**: The Create email delegation access action adds a delegate to a Gmail mailbox with its verification status set to accepted.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/create-forwarding-address-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - Create forwarding address action**: The Create forwarding address action creates a forwarding address for a Gmail mailbox.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/delete-email-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - Delete email action**: The Delete email action immediately and permanently deletes a message from a Gmail mailbox.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/delete-email-delegation-access-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - Delete email delegation access action**: The Delete email delegation access action removes a delegate from a Gmail mailbox and revokes any verification required for it.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/delete-forwarding-address-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - Delete forwarding address action**: The Delete forwarding address action deletes a forwarding address and revokes any verification required for it.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/get-message-content-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - Get message content action**: The Get message content action gets the full content of a Gmail message, identified by a Pub/Sub notification payload or a message ID.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/list-email-delegates-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - List email delegates action**: The List email delegates action lists the delegates for a Gmail mailbox.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/list-forwarding-address-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - List forwarding address action**: The List forwarding address action lists the forwarding addresses configured for a Gmail mailbox.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/search-messages-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - Search messages action**: The Search messages action lists the messages in a Gmail mailbox.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/set-auto-forwarding-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - Set auto-forwarding action**: The Set auto-forwarding action updates the auto-forwarding setting for a Gmail mailbox.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/setup-notifications-with-pubsub-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - Setup notifications with Pub/Sub action**: The Setup notifications with Pub/Sub action configures or renews Gmail push notifications to a Google Pub/Sub topic.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/stop-notifications-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - Stop notifications action**: The Stop notifications action stops Gmail push notifications for a mailbox.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/turn-off-pop-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - Turn off POP action**: The Turn off POP action updates the POP settings for a Gmail mailbox.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/update-imap-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - Update IMAP action**: The Update IMAP action updates the IMAP settings for a Gmail mailbox.
- `docs/connectors/gmail-custom/update-vacation-settings-action.md` — **Gmail (Custom) - Update vacation settings action**: The Update vacation settings action updates the vacation responder settings for a Gmail mailbox.
- `docs/connectors/gmail.md` — **Gmail**: Gmail is a popular email service used by both consumers and businesses alike.
- `docs/connectors/gmail/download-attachment-action.md` — **Gmail - Download attachment action**: The Download attachment action downloads an email attachment from your Gmail account.
- `docs/connectors/gmail/new-email-trigger.md` — **Gmail - New email trigger**: The New email trigger picks up new emails in your Gmail account.
- `docs/connectors/gmail/send-email-action.md` — **Gmail - Send email action**: The Send email action sends an email from your Gmail account.
- `docs/connectors/go-to-webinar.md` — **Goto Webinar**: The GoTo Webinar connector enables you to automate webinar workflows, scheduling and promoting events, engaging attendees, and analyzing webinar performance.
- `docs/connectors/gong.md` — **Gong**: Connects Workato to Gong to build automations.
- `docs/connectors/gong/add-call-media.md` — **Gong - Add call media action**: The Add call media action in the Gong connector uploads a media file of up to 60 MB to a call you previously added with the Add call action.
- `docs/connectors/gong/add-call.md` — **Gong - Add call action**: The Add call action in the Gong connector lets you add a call by supplying a media download URL or uploading media with the Add call media action.
- `docs/connectors/gong/create-content-share-engagement-event.md` — **Gong - Create content share engagement event action**: The Create content share engagement event action in the Gong connector pushes events into Gong when a user shares content with external participants.
- `docs/connectors/gong/create-content-view-event.md` — **Gong - Create content view event action**: The Create content view event action in the Gong connector pushes content view events to the Gong activity timeline when an external participant views content.
- `docs/connectors/gong/create-custom-action-event.md` — **Gong - Create custom action event action**: The Create custom action event action in the Gong connector pushes engagement events tied to a deal onto the deal's event timeline.
- `docs/connectors/gong/get-call-by-id.md` — **Gong - Get call by ID action**: The Get call by ID action in the Gong connector retrieves data for a specific call using its unique Gong ID.
- `docs/connectors/gong/new-call-realtime.md` — **Gong - New call trigger (real-time)**: The New call trigger in the Gong connector fires in real time when a new call matches the automation rule you set up in Gong.
- `docs/connectors/gong/search-aggregated-user-data.md` — **Gong - Search aggregated user data action**: The Search aggregated user data action in the Gong connector lists activity statistics for multiple users across time periods in a date range.
- `docs/connectors/gong/search-call-scorecards.md` — **Gong - Search call scorecards action**: The Search call scorecards action in the Gong connector retrieves scorecard answers by call date, review date, scorecard, or reviewed user.
- `docs/connectors/gong/search-call-transcripts.md` — **Gong - Search call transcripts action**: The Search call transcripts action in the Gong connector lists transcripts of calls within a specified date range and matching specific call IDs.
- `docs/connectors/gong/search-calls.md` — **Gong - Search calls action**: The Search calls action in the Gong connector lists detailed call data for calls that match a date range, specific call IDs, or both.
- `docs/connectors/gong/search-users.md` — **Gong - Search users action**: The Search users action in the Gong connector looks up users by their Gong internal IDs and returns their profile details.
- `docs/connectors/google-analytics.md` — **Google Analytics**: The Google Analytics connector lets you automate web analytics workflows: pull reports on demand and trigger recipes on new rows of analytics data.
- `docs/connectors/google-analytics/get-report-action.md` — **Google Analytics - Get report action**: The Get report action returns a customized report of Google Analytics event data. Reports contain statistics derived from data collected by the Google Analytics tracking code.
- `docs/connectors/google-analytics/new-rows-in-batch-trigger.md` — **Google Analytics - New rows in batch trigger (batch)**: The New rows in batch trigger activates when a new batch of rows is created in a Google Analytics report.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar.md` — **Google Calendar**: Google Calendar is a cloud-based calendar application that enables scheduling, event tracking, reminders, and more. You can use Workato to synchronize data with Google Calendar automatically as part of a workflow.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/action-add-guest.md` — **Google Calendar - Add attendees to an event action (batch)**: The Add attendees to an event batch action adds participants to an existing event in a calendar you specify.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/action-all-day-event.md` — **Google Calendar - Create all day event action**: The Create all day event action schedules an all-day event in a calendar you specify.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/action-create-calendar.md` — **Google Calendar - Create calendar action**: The Create calendar action creates a new calendar with the name, description, and time zone you specify.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/action-create-event.md` — **Google Calendar - Create event action**: The Create event action adds a new event to a calendar you specify.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/action-create-task.md` — **Google Calendar - Create task action**: The Create task action creates a new task in a task list you specify.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/action-delete-event.md` — **Google Calendar - Delete event action**: The Delete event action removes an existing event from a calendar you specify.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/action-get-calendar-by-id.md` — **Google Calendar - Get calendar by ID action**: The Get calendar by ID action retrieves a calendar you specify by its Google Calendar ID.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/action-get-id.md` — **Google Calendar - Get event by ID action**: The Get event by ID action retrieves information about an event you specify by its Google Calendar ID.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/action-list-calendars.md` — **Google Calendar - List calendars action**: The List calendars action retrieves all calendars from your connected Google Calendar account.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/action-remove-guest.md` — **Google Calendar - Delete attendees from an event action (batch)**: The Delete attendees from an event batch action removes participants you specify from an existing event.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/action-search-events.md` — **Google Calendar - Search events action (batch)**: The Search events batch action searches a calendar for events matching the search terms you specify.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/action-update-event.md` — **Google Calendar - Update event action**: The Update event action updates an existing Google Calendar event you specify.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/action-update-task.md` — **Google Calendar - Update task action**: The Update task action updates an existing task in a Google Calendar task list you specify.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/trigger-event-end.md` — **Google Calendar - Event end trigger**: The Event end trigger monitors a calendar you specify for ending events.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/trigger-event-start.md` — **Google Calendar - Event start trigger**: The Event start trigger monitors events in a calendar you specify and triggers jobs at the start time of an event or at a specified length before the start time.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/trigger-event-updated.md` — **Google Calendar - New/updated event trigger**: The New/updated event monitors a calendar you specify for newly created or updated events.
- `docs/connectors/google-calendar/trigger-new-event.md` — **Google Calendar - New event trigger**: The New event trigger monitors a calendar you specify for newly created events.
- `docs/connectors/google-docs.md` — **Google Docs**: The Google Docs connector enables you to automate document workflows, creating, editing, and collaborating on cloud-based documents in real time.
- `docs/connectors/google-docs/create-document-action.md` — **Google Docs - Create document action**: Creates a new document with the provided text in a simple paragraph format.
- `docs/connectors/google-docs/create-document-from-template-action.md` — **Google Docs - Create document from template action**: Creates a new document from an existing template.
- `docs/connectors/google-docs/get-document-action.md` — **Google Docs - Get document action**: Retrieves the document based on specified document ID.
- `docs/connectors/google-docs/update-document-action.md` — **Google Docs - Update document action**: This action enables you to update the selected Google document.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive.md` — **Google Drive**: Connects Workato to Google Drive to build automations.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/action-add-permission.md` — **Google Drive - Add file permission action**: Adds a permission to a file in Google Drive.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/action-copy-file.md` — **Google Drive - Copy file action**: Copies a file from a source folder to a destination folder in Google Drive.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/action-create-folder.md` — **Google Drive - Create folder action**: Creates a new folder in Google Drive.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/action-delete-file.md` — **Google Drive - Delete file action**: Deletes a file from Google Drive
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/action-download-file.md` — **Google Drive - Download file action**: Downloads the content of a file in Google Drive.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/action-export-file.md` — **Google Drive - Export file action**: Exports Google files to other MIME types.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/action-get-permission.md` — **Google Drive - Get file permission action**: Retrieves details for a permission associated with a file you specify in Google Drive.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/action-list-permissions.md` — **Google Drive - List file permissions action (batch)**: Lists all permissions associated with a file you specify in Google Drive.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/action-remove-permissions.md` — **Google Drive - Remove file permission action**: Removes a permission associated with a file you specify in Google Drive.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/action-rename-file.md` — **Google Drive - Rename or move file/folder action**: Renames or moves a file or folder in Google Drive.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/action-search-files.md` — **Google Drive - Search files or folders action**: Retrieves a list of files or folders that match your search criteria
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/action-update-permission.md` — **Google Drive - Update file permission action**: Updates a permission associated with a file you specify in Google Drive.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/action-upload-file.md` — **Google Drive - Upload file action**: Uploads a file to Google Drive using multipart streaming uploads.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/trigger-new-activity.md` — **Google Drive - New activity trigger**: The New activity trigger in the Google Drive connector fires when selected activities, such as edits or moves, occur in a parent folder and its subfolders.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/trigger-new-csv-file.md` — **Google Drive - New CSV file trigger (batch)**: The New CSV file trigger in the Google Drive connector checks a folder every 5 minutes for new CSV files and processes their lines in batches.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/trigger-new-file-hierarchy.md` — **Google Drive - New file or folder in folder hierarchy trigger**: The New file or folder in folder hierarchy trigger in the Google Drive connector picks up new files or folders created in a folder and its subfolders.
- `docs/connectors/google-drive/trigger-new-file.md` — **Google Drive - New file or folder trigger**: The New file or folder trigger in the Google Drive connector fires in real time when a new file or folder is created in a specified folder.
- `docs/connectors/google-forms.md` — **Google Forms**: The Google Forms connector enables you to automate workflows for forms, surveys, and quizzes, collecting responses and routing the data to other apps.
- `docs/connectors/google-forms/get-record-details-action.md` — **Google Forms - Get record details action**: The Get record details action retrieves the details of any record, such as a form response, using the record's ID.
- `docs/connectors/google-forms/new-response-trigger.md` — **Google Forms - New response trigger**: The New response trigger activates when a response is submitted to a Google Form.
- `docs/connectors/google-forms/search-records-action.md` — **Google Forms - Search records action**: The Search records action returns results that match your search criteria.
- `docs/connectors/google-gemini.md` — **Google Gemini**: The Google Gemini connector enables you to automate AI workflows with a multimodal LLM that interprets and generates text, images, audio, and code.
- `docs/connectors/google-gemini/analyze-image-action.md` — **Google Gemini - Analyze image action**: The Analyze image action analyses an image and answers a related question.
- `docs/connectors/google-gemini/analyze-text-action.md` — **Google Gemini - Analyze text action**: The Analyze text action performs a contextual analysis of a text to answer user-provided questions.
- `docs/connectors/google-gemini/categorize-text-action.md` — **Google Gemini - Categorize text action**: The Categorize text action sorts text into categories.
- `docs/connectors/google-gemini/draft-email-action.md` — **Google Gemini - Draft email action**: The Draft email action generates an email from a prompt.
- `docs/connectors/google-gemini/generate-text-embedding-action.md` — **Google Gemini - Generate text embedding action**: The Generate text embedding action generates an embedding from the provided text.
- `docs/connectors/google-gemini/parse-text-action.md` — **Google Gemini - Parse text action**: The Parse text action extracts data you specify from a provided text.
- `docs/connectors/google-gemini/send-messages-to-gemini-models-action.md` — **Google Gemini - Send messages to Gemini models action**: The Send messages to Gemini models action sends a message to and receives a response from a Gemini model you specify.
- `docs/connectors/google-gemini/summarize-text-action.md` — **Google Gemini - Summarize text action**: The Summarize text action summarizes a provided text.
- `docs/connectors/google-gemini/translate-text-action.md` — **Google Gemini - Translate text action**: The Translate text action translates a provided text into a different language.
- `docs/connectors/google-sheets.md` — **Google Sheets**: The Google Sheets connector enables you to automate spreadsheet workflows, writing and reading rows to move data between Google Sheets and other apps.
- `docs/connectors/google-sheets/action-add-row.md` — **Google Sheets - Add row action**: The Add row action in the Google Sheets connector lets you add a new row to a spreadsheet sheet you select.
- `docs/connectors/google-sheets/action-add-rows.md` — **Google Sheets - Add rows in bulk action**: The Add rows in bulk action in the Google Sheets connector lets you add multiple rows at the bottom of a selected sheet from a list datapill.
- `docs/connectors/google-sheets/action-get-rows.md` — **Google Sheets - Get rows action**: The Get rows action in the Google Sheets connector lets you retrieve rows from a spreadsheet, returning the first 10,000 rows or a range you specify.
- `docs/connectors/google-sheets/action-search-rows-v4.md` — **Google Sheets - Search rows action**: The Search rows action in the Google Sheets connector lets you find rows in a sheet by matching exact search values in each column.
- `docs/connectors/google-sheets/action-update-row-v4.md` — **Google Sheets - Update row action**: The Update row action in the Google Sheets connector lets you update a row in a sheet, often paired with the Search rows action.
- `docs/connectors/google-sheets/action-update-rows-in-bulk.md` — **Google Sheets - Update rows in bulk action**: The Update rows in bulk action in the Google Sheets connector lets you update up to 50,000 rows of a sheet in batches of 500.
- `docs/connectors/google-sheets/trigger-new-row-my-drive.md` — **Google Sheets - New row in sheet in My Drive trigger**: The New row in sheet in My Drive trigger in the Google Sheets connector picks up new rows added at the bottom of a sheet stored in My Drive.
- `docs/connectors/google-sheets/trigger-new-row-team-drive.md` — **Google Sheets - New row in sheet in Team Drive trigger**: The New row in sheet in Team Drive trigger in the Google Sheets connector picks up new rows added at the bottom of a sheet stored in a Team Drive.
- `docs/connectors/google-sheets/trigger-new-row.md` — **Google Sheets - New row in sheet in My Drive trigger (real-time)**: The New row in sheet in My Drive (real-time) trigger in the Google Sheets connector picks up new rows added at the bottom of a sheet in real time.
- `docs/connectors/google-sheets/trigger-new-updated-row-my-drive.md` — **Google Sheets - New/updated row in sheet in My Drive trigger**: The New/updated row in sheet in My Drive trigger in the Google Sheets connector picks up changed rows and new rows in a My Drive sheet, up to 10,000 rows.
- `docs/connectors/google-sheets/trigger-new-updated-row-team-drive.md` — **Google Sheets - New/updated row in sheet in Team Drive trigger**: The New/updated row in sheet in Team Drive trigger in the Google Sheets connector picks up changed rows and new rows in a Team Drive sheet.
- `docs/connectors/google-sheets/trigger-new-updated-row.md` — **Google Sheets - New/updated row in sheet in My Drive trigger (real-time)**: The New/updated row in sheet in My Drive (real-time) trigger in the Google Sheets connector picks up changed and new rows in a sheet in real time.
- `docs/connectors/google-slides.md` — **Google Slides**: The Google Slides connector lets you automate updates to presentations by retrieving and modifying slide content in real time.
- `docs/connectors/google-slides/get-presentaton-action.md` — **Google Slides - Get presentation details action**: The Get presentation details action retrieves metadata and structural information for a specific Google Slides presentation.
- `docs/connectors/google-slides/update-presentation-action.md` — **Google Slides - Update presentation action**: The Update presentation action updates the content of a specific Google Slides presentation by replacing text, inserting rows, adding text, or replacing images.
- `docs/connectors/google-speech-to-text.md` — **Google Speech to Text**: Google Speech to Text allows converting audio to text by applying neural network models.
- `docs/connectors/google-speech-to-text/action-short-speech.md` — **Google Speech to Text - Convert short speech to text action**: Converts a short audio file into a text transcript.
- `docs/connectors/google-text-to-speech.md` — **Google Text to Speech**: Learn how to connect to the Google Text to Speech connector in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/google-text-to-speech/action-synthesize-text.md` — **Google Text to Speech - Convert text to speech action**: The Convert text to speech action converts text into synthetic voice audio, supporting 30 voices and multiple languages.
- `docs/connectors/google-translate.md` — **Google Translate**: Translates an arbitrary text into any supported language using state-of-the-art Neural Machine Translation.
- `docs/connectors/google-translate/action-translate-text.md` — **Google Translate - Translate text action**: Allows you to translate any text into another language.
- `docs/connectors/google-vault.md` — **Google Vault**: The Google Vault connector enables you to automate information governance and eDiscovery workflows, retaining, holding, searching, and exporting Workspace data.
- `docs/connectors/google-vault/add-accounts-to-hold-action.md` — **Google Vault - Add accounts to hold action**: The add accounts to hold action enables you to add accounts to an accounts-based hold in Google Vault.
- `docs/connectors/google-vault/close-matter-action.md` — **Google Vault - Close matter action**: The close matter action enables you to close a matter in Google Vault.
- `docs/connectors/google-vault/create-record-action.md` — **Google Vault - Create record action**: The Create record action enables you to create a record in Google Vault.
- `docs/connectors/google-vault/delete-record-action.md` — **Google Vault - Delete record action**: The delete record action enables you to delete a record in Google Vault.
- `docs/connectors/google-vault/get-record-by-id-action.md` — **Google Vault - Get record by ID action**: The get record by ID action enables you to get a record in Google Vault.
- `docs/connectors/google-vault/remove-accounts-from-hold-action.md` — **Google Vault - Remove accounts from hold action**: The remove accounts from hold action enables you to remove accounts from an accounts-based hold in Google Vault.
- `docs/connectors/google-vault/reopen-matter-action.md` — **Google Vault - Reopen matter action**: The reopen matter action enables you to reopen a matter in Google Vault.
- `docs/connectors/google-vault/search-records-action.md` — **Google Vault - Search records action**: The search records action enables you to search records in Google Vault.
- `docs/connectors/google-vault/update-record-action.md` — **Google Vault - Update record action**: The Update record action enables you to update a record in Google Vault.
- `docs/connectors/google-vision.md` — **Google Vision**: The Google Vision connector lets you automate image analysis: read and extract printed text from images in your recipes.
- `docs/connectors/google-vision/action-read-text.md` — **Google Vision - Read text from image action**: You can read text from an image with Google Vision.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace-custom.md` — **Google Workspace (Custom)**: The Google Workspace (Custom) connector uses Google batch APIs to automate user and group management, including creation, updates, aliases, and membership.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace-custom/create-records-action.md` — **Google Workspace (Custom) - Create records action**: The Create records action enables you to create multiple records in Google Workspace.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace-custom/delete-records-action.md` — **Google Workspace (Custom) - Delete records action**: The Delete records action enables you to delete multiple records in Google Workspace.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace-custom/get-records-action.md` — **Google Workspace (Custom) - Get records action**: The Update record action enables you to retrieve multiple records in Google Workspace.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace-custom/update-records-action.md` — **Google Workspace (Custom) - Update records action**: The Update records action enables you to update the details of multiple records in Google Workspace.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace.md` — **Google Workspace**: Google Workspace is a collection of cloud computing, productivity, and collaboration tools.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace/action-add-record.md` — **Google Workspace - Add record action**: The Add record action allows you to add an object into the Google Workspace.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace/action-delete-record.md` — **Google Workspace - Delete record action**: The Delete record action allows you to delete an object in the Google Workspace.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace/action-get-record.md` — **Google Workspace - Get record action**: The Get record action allows you to get details of an object in the Google Workspace.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace/action-mobile-device.md` — **Google Workspace - Mobile device action**: The Mobile device action performs certain action on the mobile device resource enlisted in the Google Workspace.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace/action-search-record.md` — **Google Workspace - Search record action (batch)**: Search record action allows you to search for specific objects in the Google Workspace.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace/action-transfer-data.md` — **Google Workspace - Transfer data action**: Transfer data action allows you to transfer data from one user ID to another in the Google Workspace.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace/action-update-record.md` — **Google Workspace - Update record action**: Update record action allows you to update an object in the Google Workspace.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace/trigger-new-admin-activity-event.md` — **Google Workspace - New admin activity event trigger**: This trigger runs the recipe every time a Google admin performs an activity in the Google Workspace.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace/trigger-new-app-activity-event.md` — **Google Workspace - New application event trigger**: The New application event trigger runs the recipe every time a specific event occurs in the selected Google Workspace application.
- `docs/connectors/google-workspace/trigger-new-user-event.md` — **Google Workspace - New user event trigger**: The **New user event** trigger listens to new user related events that are created in your Google Workspace instance.
- `docs/connectors/google_cloud_storage.md` — **Google Cloud Storage**: The Google Cloud Storage connector lets you manage object storage: create and list buckets, and upload, download, and delete objects.
- `docs/connectors/google_cloud_storage/create-bucket.md` — **Google Cloud Storage - Create bucket action**: The Create bucket action in the Google Cloud Storage connector lets you create a new bucket with a unique name within a specified project.
- `docs/connectors/google_cloud_storage/delete-bucket.md` — **Google Cloud Storage - Delete bucket action**: The Delete bucket action in the Google Cloud Storage connector lets you delete a bucket from a specified project by its bucket name.
- `docs/connectors/google_cloud_storage/delete-object.md` — **Google Cloud Storage - Delete object action**: The Delete object action in the Google Cloud Storage connector lets you delete an object and its metadata from a bucket, with generation conditions.
- `docs/connectors/google_cloud_storage/download-object.md` — **Google Cloud Storage - Download object action**: The Download object action in the Google Cloud Storage connector lets you download an object and its metadata from a bucket in a chosen data type.
- `docs/connectors/google_cloud_storage/get-bucket.md` — **Google Cloud Storage - Get bucket action**: The Get bucket action in the Google Cloud Storage connector lets you retrieve the details of a single bucket by its bucket name.
- `docs/connectors/google_cloud_storage/list-buckets.md` — **Google Cloud Storage - List buckets action**: The List buckets action in the Google Cloud Storage connector lets you list all buckets within a project, with optional prefix and pagination filters.
- `docs/connectors/google_cloud_storage/list-objects.md` — **Google Cloud Storage - List objects action**: The List objects action in the Google Cloud Storage connector lets you list the details of all objects within a bucket, with folder and offset filters.
- `docs/connectors/google_cloud_storage/update-bucket.md` — **Google Cloud Storage - Update bucket action**: The Update bucket action in the Google Cloud Storage connector lets you update the settings of an existing bucket by bucket name.
- `docs/connectors/google_cloud_storage/update-object-metadata.md` — **Google Cloud Storage - Update object metadata action**: The Update object metadata action in the Google Cloud Storage connector lets you update an object's metadata and access controls within a bucket.
- `docs/connectors/google_cloud_storage/upload-object-with-file-streaming.md` — **Google Cloud Storage - Upload object with file streaming action**: The Upload object with file streaming action in the Google Cloud Storage connector uploads an object to a bucket using parallel uploads for any file size.
- `docs/connectors/gotowebinar/get-attendees-from-session.md` — **GoTo Webinar - Get attendees from session action (batch)**: The Get attendees from session action in the GoTo Webinar connector lets you retrieve attendees from a webinar session using a webinar and session key.
- `docs/connectors/gotowebinar/get-webinar-details.md` — **GoTo Webinar - Get webinar details action**: The Get webinar details action in the GoTo Webinar connector lets you retrieve webinar details such as times, registrants, and subject by webinar key.
- `docs/connectors/gotowebinar/new-webinar-session.md` — **GoTo Webinar - New webinar session trigger**: The New webinar session trigger in the GoTo Webinar connector picks up new webinar session events from a specified time range on a polling interval.
- `docs/connectors/graphql.md` — **GraphQL**: The GraphQL connector enables you to query and mutate data from any application with a GraphQL API, building operations from its schema without code.
- `docs/connectors/graphql/custom-action.md` — **GraphQL - Custom action**: The Custom action in the GraphQL connector lets you run any custom GraphQL query or mutation, including variables, fragments, directives, and aliases.
- `docs/connectors/graphql/mutate-records-action.md` — **GraphQL - Mutate records action**: The Mutate records action in the GraphQL connector lets you create, update, or delete records using mutation arguments, fields, and sub-queries.
- `docs/connectors/graphql/query-records-action.md` — **GraphQL - Query records action**: The Query records action in the GraphQL connector lets you get a single record or search for records using query arguments, fields, and sub-queries.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse.md` — **Greenhouse**: Use the Greenhouse connector to automate workflow for applicant tracking and recruiting.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/advance-application-action.md` — **Greenhouse - Advance application action**: The Advance application action in the Greenhouse connector lets you move an active application to the next stage in the hiring pipeline.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/create-attachment-v3.md` — **Greenhouse - Create attachment (v3) action**: The Create attachment (v3) action creates an attachment for an application in Greenhouse using the v3 API.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/create-object-action.md` — **Greenhouse - Create object action**: The Create object action in the Greenhouse connector lets you create new candidates, jobs, users, and candidate records in your Greenhouse instance.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/create-object-v3.md` — **Greenhouse - Create object (v3) action**: The Create object (v3) action creates objects in Greenhouse using the v3 API.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/get-object-by-id-action.md` — **Greenhouse - Get object by ID action**: The Get object by ID action in the Greenhouse connector lets you retrieve a candidate, application, job, offer, or user by its unique Greenhouse ID.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/mark-candidate-as-hired-action.md` — **Greenhouse - Mark candidate as hired action**: The Mark candidate as hired action in the Greenhouse connector lets you set an active application's status to hired and set the start date.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/mark-candidate-v3.md` — **Greenhouse - Mark candidate as hired (v3) action**: The Mark candidate as hired (v3) action marks an application as hired in Greenhouse using the v3 API.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/migration.md` — **Migrate your Greenhouse connection to v3**: Migrate Greenhouse recipes from the v1 and v2 APIs to v3, including creating an OAuth 2.0 connection and replacing legacy actions and triggers.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/move-application-v3.md` — **Greenhouse - Move application (v3) action**: The Move application (v3) action moves an application to a different stage or job in Greenhouse using the v3 API.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/new-event-trigger.md` — **Greenhouse - New event trigger (real-time)**: The New event trigger in the Greenhouse connector fires in real time when a configured candidate, application, job, or offer event arrives via webhook.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/new-object-trigger.md` — **Greenhouse - New object trigger**: The New object trigger in the Greenhouse connector picks up newly created candidates, applications, and scheduled interviews, processing each as a job.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/new-object-v3.md` — **Greenhouse - New object (v3) trigger**: The New object (v3) trigger triggers when a new object is created in Greenhouse using the v3 API.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/new-updated-v3.md` — **Greenhouse - New/updated object (v3) batch trigger**: The New/updated object (v3) trigger triggers when an object is created or updated in Greenhouse using the v3 API.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/object-coverage.md` — **Greenhouse v3 object coverage**: Reference for the objects supported by Greenhouse v3 actions and triggers, including per-object fields and differences from v1 object coverage.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/object-types.md` — **Greenhouse - Object types**: Reference for Greenhouse object types exposed as Workato datapills, including the candidate, user, application, job, and offer objects and their fields.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/reject-application-action.md` — **Greenhouse - Reject application action**: The Reject application action in the Greenhouse connector lets you reject an active application and set its status to rejected, with an optional email.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/reject-application-v3.md` — **Greenhouse - Reject application (v3) action**: The Reject application (v3) action rejects an application in Greenhouse using the v3 API.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/search-objects-action.md` — **Greenhouse - Search action**: The Search action in the Greenhouse connector lets you search your Greenhouse instance for candidates, applications, jobs, offers, and users by criteria.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/search-objects-v3.md` — **Greenhouse - Search objects (v3) batch action**: The Search objects (v3) action retrieves records from Greenhouse using the v3 API.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/update-object-action.md` — **Greenhouse - Update object action**: The Update object action in the Greenhouse connector lets you update a candidate, application, or job identified by its unique Greenhouse ID.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/update-object-v3.md` — **Greenhouse - Update object (v3) action**: The Update object (v3) action updates objects in Greenhouse using the v3 API.
- `docs/connectors/greenhouse/upload-attachment-action.md` — **Greenhouse - Upload attachment action**: The Upload attachment action in the Greenhouse connector lets you attach a file to a candidate or application identified by its Greenhouse ID.
- `docs/connectors/gumroad.md` — **Gumroad**: Connect Gumroad to Workato to manage sales, subscribers, refunds, content access, and license keys.
- `docs/connectors/gumroad/enable-or-disable-license-action.md` — **Gumroad - Enable or disable license action**: Enable or disable a license key for a product in Gumroad. A disabled license key fails verification.
- `docs/connectors/gumroad/get-record-details-by-id-action.md` — **Gumroad - Get record details by ID action**: Retrieve the details of a Gumroad record, such as a sale or subscriber, by its unique ID.
- `docs/connectors/gumroad/new-event-trigger.md` — **Gumroad - New event trigger**: Invoke a recipe in real time when a Gumroad sale, refund, dispute, cancellation, or subscription event occurs.
- `docs/connectors/gumroad/refund-sale-action.md` — **Gumroad - Refund sale action**: Issue a full or partial refund for a sale in Gumroad. Requires a sufficient seller balance.
- `docs/connectors/gumroad/revoke-or-restore-access-action.md` — **Gumroad - Revoke or restore access action**: Revoke or restore a buyer's access to the downloaded content for a sale in Gumroad.
- `docs/connectors/gumroad/search-records-action.md` — **Gumroad - Search records action**: Search for Gumroad records, such as sales or subscribers, that match optional filters.
- `docs/connectors/gumroad/verify-license-action.md` — **Gumroad - Verify license action**: Verify a license key for a product in Gumroad and optionally avoid consuming a use.
- `docs/connectors/hibob.md` — **HiBob**: The HiBob connector lets you automate HR workflows: create, update, search, and delete records, manage employees, and trigger on new events.
- `docs/connectors/hibob/create-object-action.md` — **HiBob - Create object action**: **Create object** action enables you to create any standard or custom record, such as employee, in HiBob.
- `docs/connectors/hibob/delete-object-action.md` — **HiBob - Delete object action**: **Delete object** action enables you to delete any standard or custom record such as employee, through its HiBob ID. First select the object, then specify the HiBob ID of the record to be deleted.

- `docs/connectors/hibob/upload-document-action.md` — **HiBob - Upload document action**: The **Upload document** action uploads a document to the specific folder in HiBob. The maximum file size allowed to upload is 1 MB.

- `docs/connectors/hive.md` — **Hive**: The Hive connector lets you automate project management: create, update, search, and delete records, apply templates, and trigger on new records.

- `docs/connectors/hl7-http.md` — **HL7 HTTP**: HL7 HTTP, or Health Level Seven HTTP, is a set of international standards for the exchange, integration, sharing, and retrieval of electronic health information. This connector is the HTTP-based variant of the HL7 connector. This connector sends HL7 messages using HTTP.
- `docs/connectors/hl7-http/new-message-http-trigger.md` — **HL7 HTTP - New message trigger (real-time)**: The New message trigger (real-time) monitors HL7 HTTP for new messages.
- `docs/connectors/hl7-http/parse-message-header-http-action.md` — **HL7 HTTP - Parse message header action**: The Parse message header action parses a message header in HL7 HTTP.
- `docs/connectors/hl7-http/parse-message-http-action.md` — **HL7 HTTP - Parse message action**: The Parse message action parses a message in HL7 HTTP.
- `docs/connectors/hl7-http/send-message-http-action.md` — **HL7 HTTP - Send message action**: The Send message action sends a message in HL7 HTTP.
- `docs/connectors/hl7-http/send-raw-message-http-action.md` — **HL7 HTTP - Send raw message action**: The Send raw message action sends a raw message in HL7 HTTP.
- `docs/connectors/hl7.md` — **HL7**: The HL7 connector enables you to send and receive HL7 messages to automate healthcare data exchange between clinical and administrative systems.

- `docs/connectors/hl7/send-message-action.md` — **HL7 - Send message action**: The Send message action in the HL7 connector lets you send an HL7 message using the model version and message type you specify.
