- `docs/connectors/intercom/search-conversations.md` — **Intercom - Search conversations by user action**: The Search conversations by user action in the Intercom connector lets you retrieve the conversations associated with a specific user by their ID.
- `docs/connectors/intercom/search-notes.md` — **Intercom - Search notes by user action**: The Search notes by user action in the Intercom connector lets you retrieve the list of notes associated with a specific user by their unique ID.
- `docs/connectors/intercom/search-segments.md` — **Intercom - Search segments by user action**: The Search segments by user action in the Intercom connector lets you retrieve the list of segments associated with a specific user by their ID.
- `docs/connectors/intercom/search-tags.md` — **Intercom - Search tags by user action**: The Search tags by user action in the Intercom connector lets you retrieve the list of tags associated with a specific user by their unique ID.
- `docs/connectors/intercom/search-user.md` — **Intercom - Search user action**: The Search user action in the Intercom connector lets you find a specific user record by their email, user ID, or internal Intercom ID.
- `docs/connectors/intercom/update-user.md` — **Intercom - Update users action**: The Update users action in the Intercom connector lets you update an existing user record by the user ID or internal Intercom ID you specify.
- `docs/connectors/intercom/updated-contact.md` — **Intercom - Updated contact trigger**: The Updated contact trigger in the Intercom connector fires when a contact's details are updated, checking for changes on the trigger poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/intercom/updated-conversation.md` — **Intercom - Updated conversation trigger**: The Updated conversation trigger in the Intercom connector fires when a conversation is updated, checking for changes on the trigger poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/intercom/updated-user.md` — **Intercom - Updated user trigger**: The Updated user trigger in the Intercom connector fires when a user is updated, checking for changes on the trigger poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/ironclad.md` — **Ironclad**: The Ironclad connector lets you automate contract lifecycle workflows: create, update, list, search, and delete records and trigger recipes on new events.
- `docs/connectors/ironclad/create-record-action.md` — **Ironclad - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a new record in Ironclad.
- `docs/connectors/ironclad/delete-record-action.md` — **Ironclad - Delete record action**: The Delete record action deletes a record in Ironclad.
- `docs/connectors/ironclad/get-record-details-action.md` — **Ironclad - Get record details action**: The Get record details action retrieves the details of a record in Ironclad.
- `docs/connectors/ironclad/list-records-action.md` — **Ironclad - List records action**: The List records action lists all records in your Ironclad instance.
- `docs/connectors/ironclad/new-event-trigger.md` — **Ironclad - New event trigger**: The New event trigger monitors Ironclad for events.
- `docs/connectors/ironclad/search-records-action.md` — **Ironclad - Search records action**: The Search records action searches for records in your Ironclad instance.
- `docs/connectors/ironclad/update-record-action.md` — **Ironclad - Update record action**: The Update record action updates a record in Ironclad.
- `docs/connectors/iterable.md` — **Iterable**: The Iterable connector lets you automate marketing workflows: upsert users, manage lists and subscriptions, send SMS, and track events.
- `docs/connectors/jamf.md` — **JAMF**: Use the JAMF connector to manage Apple devices, computers, account users, and departments in JAMF Pro from Workato recipes.
- `docs/connectors/jamf/create-record-action.md` — **JAMF - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a new account user, command, computer, or computer invitation in JAMF Pro.
- `docs/connectors/jamf/delete-record-action.md` — **JAMF - Delete record action**: The Delete record action deletes an account user or computer from JAMF Pro.
- `docs/connectors/jamf/get-record-action.md` — **JAMF - Get record action**: The Get record action retrieves the details of a single JAMF object by ID, name, serial number, or UDID.
- `docs/connectors/jamf/new-event-trigger.md` — **JAMF - New event trigger**: The New event trigger fires when a JAMF Pro webhook event occurs, including computer enrollment, check-in, inventory, and policy events.
- `docs/connectors/jamf/search-records-action.md` — **JAMF - Search records action**: The Search records action returns a list of JAMF objects that match your criteria, including computers, account users, departments, and Mac applications.
- `docs/connectors/jamf/update-record-action.md` — **JAMF - Update record action**: The Update record action updates an existing account user in JAMF Pro.
- `docs/connectors/javascript.md` — **JavaScript**: The JavaScript connector is a utility connector that lets you run custom JavaScript code in a recipe to transform data or invoke external services.
- `docs/connectors/javascript/javascript-faqs.md` — **JavaScript FAQs**: Answers to frequent questions about the Workato JavaScript connector, including its Node.js runtime, script size quotas, and defining input fields.
- `docs/connectors/jdbc.md` — **JDBC**: The JDBC connector lets you work with any ANSI-compliant database: insert, update, delete, and select rows, run custom SQL, and trigger on new rows.
- `docs/connectors/jdbc/delete.md` — **JDBC - Delete rows action**: The Delete rows action in the JDBC connector lets you delete rows from a table that match the WHERE condition you define.
- `docs/connectors/jdbc/export-query-result.md` — **JDBC - Export query result action**: The Export query result action in the JDBC connector lets you export the data from a SQL query to a CSV or delimited file at an upload URL.
- `docs/connectors/jdbc/insert.md` — **JDBC - Insert rows (batch) action**: Insert multiple rows into a table using the JDBC connector.
- `docs/connectors/jdbc/new-row-trigger.md` — **JDBC - New row triggers**: The JDBC new row triggers pick up rows inserted in a selected table or view, processing them individually or in batches each poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/jdbc/run_sql.md` — **JDBC - Run custom SQL action (batch)**: The Run custom SQL (batch) action in the JDBC connector lets you run DML and DDL statements such as INSERT, UPDATE, SELECT, CREATE, and DROP.
- `docs/connectors/jdbc/scheduled-query-trigger.md` — **JDBC - Scheduled query trigger**: The JDBC scheduled query trigger runs custom SQL on a schedule you set and picks up new or updated rows as batches each poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/jdbc/select.md` — **JDBC - Select actions**: Use the JDBC Select rows and Select rows using custom SQL actions to return rows from a table or view that match a WHERE condition or query.
- `docs/connectors/jdbc/update.md` — **JDBC - Update rows action**: The Update rows action in the JDBC connector lets you update one or more rows that match a WHERE condition with a single set of values.
- `docs/connectors/jina.md` — **Jina**: Prepare clean, LLM-ready content from the web with Jina's Search Foundation APIs, then embed, rerank, or classify the results in your recipes.
- `docs/connectors/jina/classify-text-action.md` — **Jina - Classify text action**: Assign labels to text automatically, using either zero-shot candidate labels or a classifier you've trained in Jina.
- `docs/connectors/jina/deep-research-action.md` — **Jina - Deep research action**: Get a structured research answer from Jina DeepSearch, with support for JSON schema output and hostname allow and block lists.
- `docs/connectors/jina/generate-embeddings-action.md` — **Jina - Generate embeddings action**: Turn text into numeric vectors for search, retrieval, or classification pipelines, with configurable dimensions and encoding.
- `docs/connectors/jina/read-url-action.md` — **Jina - Read URL action**: Turn any URL into LLM-ready markdown, HTML, or a screenshot using Jina Reader.
- `docs/connectors/jina/rerank-documents-action.md` — **Jina - Rerank documents action**: Improve search and RAG pipelines by ranking a document list against a query, with optional embeddings for each result.
- `docs/connectors/jina/search-web-action.md` — **Jina - Search web action**: Run a Google, Bing, or Jina-native web search and return clean, LLM-ready results, at a 10,000-token minimum per call.
- `docs/connectors/jina/segment-text-action.md` — **Jina - Segment text action**: Chunk long text for downstream LLM steps, free of charge, up to 64,000 characters per call.
- `docs/connectors/jira.md` — **Jira**: The Jira connector lets you automate issue tracking: create, update, and search issues, manage comments and attachments, and trigger on new or updated issues.

- `docs/connectors/jira/assign-user-to-issue-action.md` — **Jira - Assign user to issue action**: This action assigns an issue to a Jira user.
- `docs/connectors/jira/create-comment-action.md` — **Jira - Create comment action**: This action creates a comment on an existing issue.
- `docs/connectors/jira/create-issue-action.md` — **Jira - Create issue action**: This action creates a new issue in Jira.
- `docs/connectors/jira/create-user-action.md` — **Jira - Create user action**: This action creates a new user in your Jira instance.
- `docs/connectors/jira/deleted-object-trigger.md` — **Jira - Deleted object trigger (real-time)**: This trigger monitors Jira for deleted objects in real-time.
- `docs/connectors/jira/download-attachment-action.md` — **Jira - Download attachment action**: This action downloads an attachment from a Jira issue.
- `docs/connectors/jira/export-new-issues-trigger.md` — **Jira - Export new issues in Jira trigger**: The Export new issues in Jira trigger retrieves new issues from Jira on a schedule you specify.

- `docs/connectors/jira/get-changelog-of-an-issue-action.md` — **Jira - Get changelog of an issue action**: This action retrieves an issue's changelog from Jira.
- `docs/connectors/jira/get-issue-action.md` — **Jira - Get issue action**: This action retrieves an issue from Jira.
- `docs/connectors/jira/get-issue-comments-batch-action.md` — **Jira - Get issue comments action (batch)**: This action retrieves the comments belonging to an existing issue in batches.
- `docs/connectors/jira/get-issue-schema-action.md` — **Jira - Get issue schema action**: This action retrieves the name and schema of all fields in a Jira object.
- `docs/connectors/jira/get-user-details-action.md` — **Jira - Get user details action**: This action retrieves a user from Jira. This action fails if the user doesn't exist.
- `docs/connectors/jira/new-event-trigger.md` — **Jira - New event trigger (real-time)**: This trigger monitors Jira for new events in real-time.
- `docs/connectors/jira/new-issue-batch-trigger.md` — **Jira - New issue trigger (batch)**: This trigger retrieves new issues from Jira in batches at an interval you specify.
- `docs/connectors/jira/new-issue-trigger.md` — **Jira - New issue trigger**: This trigger checks Jira for new issues at an interval you specify.
- `docs/connectors/jira/new-updated-comment-trigger.md` — **Jira - New/updated comment trigger (real-time)**: This trigger monitors Jira for new or updated comments in real-time.
- `docs/connectors/jira/new-updated-issue-trigger.md` — **Jira - New/updated issue trigger (real-time)**: This trigger monitors Jira for new or updated issues in real-time.
- `docs/connectors/jira/new-updated-worklog-trigger.md` — **Jira - New/updated worklog trigger (real-time)**: This trigger monitors Jira for new or updated worklogs in real-time.
- `docs/connectors/jira/search-assignable-users-batch-action.md` — **Jira - Search assignable users action (batch)**: This action retrieves a list of all users that can be assigned to a specific issue.
- `docs/connectors/jira/search-issues-batch-action.md` — **Jira - Search issues action (batch)**: This action searches Jira for issues that exactly match the criteria you specify and retrieves the results in batches.
- `docs/connectors/jira/search-jql-action.md` — **Jira - Search issues by JQL action (batch)**: This action searches Jira for issues that match the JQL query string you provide and retrieves the results in batches.

- `docs/connectors/jira/update-comment-action.md` — **Jira - Update comment action**: This action updates a comment on an existing issue.
- `docs/connectors/jira/update-issue-action.md` — **Jira - Update issue action**: This action updates an issue in Jira.
- `docs/connectors/jira/update-issue-status-action.md` — **Jira - Update issue status action**: This action updates the status of an issue in Jira.
- `docs/connectors/jira/updated-issue-batch-trigger.md` — **Jira - Updated issue trigger (batch)**: This trigger retrieves updated issues from Jira in batches at an interval you specify.

- `docs/connectors/jsd/create-customer-request-action.md` — **Jira Service Desk - Create customer request action**: The Create customer request action in the Jira Service Desk connector lets you raise a request of a chosen type in a service desk instance.
- `docs/connectors/jsd/get-comment-by-id-action.md` — **Jira Service Desk - Get comment by ID action**: The Get comment by ID action in the Jira Service Desk connector lets you retrieve a single comment on an issue in your service desk instance.

- `docs/connectors/jumpcloud/lock-user-action.md` — **JumpCloud - Lock user action**: The Lock user action in the JumpCloud connector lets you lock a user account you specify, preventing that user from signing in.
- `docs/connectors/jumpcloud/new-object-trigger.md` — **JumpCloud - New object trigger**: The New object trigger in the JumpCloud connector monitors JumpCloud for newly created objects of the type you specify and picks them up.

- `docs/connectors/jumpcloud/search-objects-action.md` — **JumpCloud - Search objects action (batch)**: The Search objects batch action in the JumpCloud connector searches for objects in batches and returns up to 100 results matching your criteria.
- `docs/connectors/jumpcloud/search-user-by-employee-id-action.md` — **JumpCloud - Search user by employee ID action**: The Search user by employee ID action in the JumpCloud connector retrieves data about a user based on the employee ID you provide.
- `docs/connectors/jumpcloud/unlock-user-action.md` — **JumpCloud - Unlock user action**: The Unlock user action in the JumpCloud connector lets you unlock a user account locked by the Lock user action or by failed sign-in attempts.

- `docs/connectors/jumpcloud/update-user-on-system-action.md` — **JumpCloud - Update user on system action**: The Update user on system action in the JumpCloud connector lets you update a user's settings on a system you specify.
- `docs/connectors/jwt-tools/decode-jwt.md` — **JWT tools by Workato - Decode JWT action**: The Decode JWT action in the JWT tools by Workato connector decodes a JSON Web Token and validates its iat, exp, and nbf claims when present.
- `docs/connectors/jwt-tools/generate-jwt.md` — **JWT tools by Workato - Generate JWT action**: The Generate JWT action in the JWT tools by Workato connector lets you create a JSON Web Token by specifying the payload and header details.
- `docs/connectors/jwt.md` — **JWT tools by Workato**: The JWT tools by Workato connector is a utility connector that generates and decodes JSON Web Tokens for secure authentication and information exchange.

- `docs/connectors/kafka/new-message-trigger.md` — **Apache Kafka - New message in topic trigger**: The New message trigger picks up new Kafka messages for a selected Kafka topic.

- `docs/connectors/kafka/publish-messages-batch.md` — **Apache Kafka - Publish messages action (batch)**: The Publish messages batch action publishes multiple messages to a selected Kafka topic in a single batch operation.
- `docs/connectors/kickflow.md` — **Kickflow**: Use the Kickflow connector to create, retrieve, update, delete, and search Kickflow records, and run ticket operations, from Workato recipes.

- `docs/connectors/kickflow/update-record-action.md` — **Kickflow - Update record action**: The Update record action updates an existing record in Kickflow.

- `docs/connectors/linear/new-or-updated-records-trigger.md` — **Linear - New/updated records trigger**: This trigger runs the recipe on Linear whenever a new record is created or updated, such as an issue or project.
- `docs/connectors/linear/new-record-trigger.md` — **Linear - New record trigger**: This trigger runs the recipe when a new record, such as an issue or project, is created in Linear.

- `docs/connectors/linear/update-record-action.md` — **Linear - Update record action**: The Update record action enables you to update the details of a specific record in Linear.
- `docs/connectors/linkedin.md` — **LinkedIn**: The LinkedIn connector enables you to automate B2B marketing workflows, syncing campaign performance and leads from LinkedIn Campaign Manager to your CRM.
- `docs/connectors/linkedin/campaign-actions.md` — **LinkedIn - Campaign actions**: Reference for LinkedIn campaign actions in Workato, including retrieving a list of campaigns by status and getting campaign details by ID.
- `docs/connectors/linkedin/get-advertising-form-response-by-id.md` — **LinkedIn - Get lead gen form response by ID action**: The Get lead gen form response by ID action in the LinkedIn connector lets you retrieve a specific lead gen form submission, with its questions and answers.
- `docs/connectors/linkedin/new-advertising-form.md` — **LinkedIn - New lead gen form submitted trigger**: The New lead gen form submitted trigger in the LinkedIn connector fires when someone completes a lead gen form in any of your LinkedIn campaigns.
- `docs/connectors/linkedin/search-advertising-form-responses.md` — **LinkedIn - Search lead gen form responses action**: The Search lead gen form responses action in the LinkedIn connector lets you search responses, filtered by campaign, form, and creative.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp.md` — **MailChimp**: The MailChimp connector enables you to automate email and marketing campaign workflows, managing subscribers and integrated campaigns across email and ads.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/add-subscriber-action.md` — **MailChimp - Add subscriber action**: The Add subscriber action in the MailChimp connector lets you add a subscriber to a list you specify, with an optional subscriber status.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/add-subscriber-tags-action.md` — **MailChimp - Add subscriber tags action**: The Add subscriber tags action in the MailChimp connector lets you add a tag to a subscriber you specify on a given list.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/campaign-created-trigger.md` — **MailChimp - Campaign created trigger**: The Campaign created trigger in the MailChimp connector fires when a new campaign is created, returning details about that campaign.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/campaign-opened-trigger.md` — **MailChimp - Campaign opened trigger**: The Campaign opened trigger in the MailChimp connector fires when a contact in your audience opens a campaign communication sent to them.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/campaign-sent-trigger.md` — **MailChimp - Campaign sent trigger**: The Campaign sent trigger in the MailChimp connector fires when a campaign is sent, returning information about the MailChimp object.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/get-subscriber-activity-action.md` — **MailChimp - Get subscriber activity action**: The Get subscriber activity action in the MailChimp connector lets you fetch a subscriber's activity on a list, including opens, clicks, and unsubscribes.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/get-subscriber-tags-action.md` — **MailChimp - Get subscriber tags action**: The Get subscriber tags action in the MailChimp connector lets you fetch the tags assigned to a subscriber on a list you specify.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/new-list-trigger.md` — **MailChimp - New list trigger**: The New list trigger in the MailChimp connector fires when a new list is detected, checking for new lists every five minutes by default.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/new-subscriber-trigger.md` — **MailChimp - New subscriber trigger**: The New subscriber trigger in the MailChimp connector fires when a new subscriber is added, checking the list you monitor every five minutes by default.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/new-updated-subscriber-trigger.md` — **MailChimp - New or updated subscriber trigger**: The New or updated subscriber trigger in the MailChimp connector fires when a subscriber is added or updated, checking every five minutes by default.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/remove-subscriber-action.md` — **MailChimp - Remove subscriber action**: The Remove subscriber action in the MailChimp connector lets you remove a subscriber that you specify from a list by email address.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/search-campaigns-action.md` — **MailChimp - Search campaigns action**: The Search campaigns action in the MailChimp connector lets you fetch a list of campaigns that match your search criteria, such as name or status.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/search-subscribers-action.md` — **MailChimp - Search subscribers action**: The Search subscribers action in the MailChimp connector lets you find subscribers whose email addresses at least partially match your search text.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/search-tags-action.md` — **MailChimp - Search tags action**: The Search tags action in the MailChimp connector lets you search for tags contained within a list that you specify.
- `docs/connectors/mailchimp/update-subscriber-action.md` — **MailChimp - Update subscriber action**: The Update subscriber action in the MailChimp connector lets you update a subscriber that you specify on a given list by email address.
- `docs/connectors/mapper.md` — **Mapper by Workato**: Mapper is a utility connector that lets you map data to a predefined schema before publishing or moving it between applications.
- `docs/connectors/marketo.md` — **Marketo**: The Marketo connector helps to automate marketing and provides tools for email marketing and lead management.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/activate-smart-campaign-action.md` — **Marketo - Activate smart campaign action**: The Activate smart campaign action in the Marketo connector lets you activate a smart campaign by its ID in your instance.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/add-custom-activity-action.md` — **Marketo - Add custom activity action (batch)**: The Add custom activity action in the Marketo connector lets you add custom activities to lead records in batches of up to 300 records at once.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/add-leads-to-list-action.md` — **Marketo - Add leads to lead list action (batch)**: The Add leads to lead list action in the Marketo connector lets you add a batch of leads to a list by list ID or name in your instance.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/bulk-export-objects-action.md` — **Marketo - Bulk export objects to file action (bulk)**: The Bulk export objects to file action in the Marketo connector lets you export activities, program members, leads, or custom objects to a CSV file.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/bulk-import-objects-action.md` — **Marketo - Bulk import objects from file action (bulk)**: The Bulk import objects from file action in the Marketo connector lets you import program members, leads, or custom objects in bulk.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/change-lead-program-status-action.md` — **Marketo - Change lead program status action (batch)**: The Change lead program status action in the Marketo connector lets you change the program status of a batch of leads in your instance.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/clone-object-action.md` — **Marketo - Clone object action**: The Clone object action in the Marketo connector lets you clone a program or smart campaign into a destination folder you specify.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/create-object-action.md` — **Marketo - Create object action**: The Create object action in the Marketo connector lets you create opportunities, leads, programs, smart campaigns, and other objects in your instance.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/create-update-upsert-leads-action.md` — **Marketo - Create/update/upsert leads action (batch)**: The Create/update/upsert leads action in the Marketo connector lets you create, update, or upsert up to 300 leads at once by a lookup field.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/export-new-leads-trigger.md` — **Marketo - Export new leads in Marketo trigger (bulk)**: The Export new leads trigger (bulk) in the Marketo connector runs on a schedule and returns new leads from your instance as a CSV stream.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/export-new-updated-leads-trigger.md` — **Marketo - Export new/updated leads in Marketo trigger (bulk)**: The Export new/updated leads trigger (bulk) in the Marketo connector runs on a schedule and returns new or updated leads as a CSV stream.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/get-objects-action.md` — **Marketo - Get objects action**: The Get objects action in the Marketo connector lets you retrieve object schemas, channels, programs, lead activities, folders, and smart campaigns.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/monitor-leads-added-to-list-trigger.md` — **Marketo - Monitor leads added to list trigger (batch)**: The Monitor leads added to list trigger (batch) in the Marketo connector picks up leads added to a specified list each poll interval, in batches.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/new-lead-activity-batch-trigger.md` — **Marketo - New lead activity trigger (batch)**: The New lead activity trigger (batch) in the Marketo connector detects new lead activities each poll interval, processed as separate jobs.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/new-lead-trigger.md` — **Marketo - New lead in list trigger**: The New lead in list trigger in the Marketo connector picks up new leads added to a specified list each poll interval, processed as separate jobs.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/new-marketo-self-service-flow-step-trigger.md` — **Marketo - New Marketo Self Service Flow Step trigger (real-time)**: The New Marketo Self Service Flow Step trigger picks up new self-service flow steps in real time, processing each as a separate job in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/new-updated-lead-batch-trigger.md` — **Marketo - New/updated lead trigger (batch)**: The New/updated lead trigger (batch) in the Marketo connector picks up new or updated leads in batches each poll interval, processed as separate jobs.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/remove-leads-from-list-action.md` — **Marketo - Remove leads from lead list action**: The Remove leads from lead list action in the Marketo connector lets you remove selected leads or lead IDs from a list in your instance.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/return-data-to-marketo-self-service-flow-step-action.md` — **Marketo - Return data to Marketo Self Service Flow Step action**: The Return data to Self Service Flow Step action in the Marketo connector lets you send lead updates back to Marketo from a flow step invocation.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/schedule-campaign-action.md` — **Marketo - Schedule campaign or smart campaign action**: The Schedule campaign or smart campaign action in the Marketo connector lets you schedule a campaign in a program to run at a time you specify.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/search-objects-action.md` — **Marketo - Search objects action (batch)**: The Search objects action in the Marketo connector lets you find opportunities, programs, leads, campaigns, and smart campaigns by your criteria.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/self-service-flow-steps.md` — **Marketo - Self-service Flow Steps**: Learn how Marketo self-service flow steps work with Workato to send lead data in bulk from smart campaign flows, plus setup steps and FAQs.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/submit-form-action.md` — **Marketo - Submit form action**: The Submit form action in the Marketo connector upserts a lead and generates a Fill out Form activity tied to a program or campaign.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/trigger-campaign-action.md` — **Marketo - Trigger campaign or smart campaign for specific leads action**: The Trigger campaign or smart campaign action in the Marketo connector lets you run a campaign for specific leads, with optional tokens passed in.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/update-object-action.md` — **Marketo - Update object action**: The Update object action in the Marketo connector lets you update opportunities, leads, smart campaigns, and custom objects in your instance.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/updated-lead-trigger.md` — **Marketo - New/updated lead trigger**: The New/updated lead trigger in the Marketo connector picks up new or updated leads each poll interval, processed as separate jobs.
- `docs/connectors/marketo/upsert-custom-objects-action.md` — **Marketo - Upsert custom objects action**: The Upsert custom objects action in the Marketo connector lets you create or update up to 300 custom object records by a lookup field you specify.

- `docs/connectors/marketo/upsert-tokens-action.md` — **Marketo - Upsert tokens action**: The Upsert tokens action in the Marketo connector lets you create or update tokens in a folder or program by name, type, and value.

- `docs/connectors/mastercard/enroll-or-unenroll-merchants.md` — **Mastercard - Enroll/unenroll merchants action**: The Enroll/unenroll merchants action enables you to add or delete merchants in Mastercard.
- `docs/connectors/mastercard/get-status-of-merchant-enrollment-action.md` — **Mastercard - Get status of merchant enrollment action**: The Get status of merchant enrollment action enables you to fetch the status of enrolled merchants.
- `docs/connectors/message-template-by-workato.md` — **Message template by Workato**: Message template by Workato is a utility connector that lets you create reusable Mustache templates to generate HTML, text, JSON, and XML messages in recipes.

- `docs/connectors/microsoft-dynamics-365/new-object-trigger-real-time.md` — **Microsoft Dynamics 365 - New object trigger (real-time)**: The New object real-time trigger in the Microsoft Dynamics 365 connector fires immediately when a record is created in Microsoft Dynamics 365.

- `docs/connectors/microsoft-dynamics-365/new-updated-object-trigger-batch.md` — **Microsoft Dynamics 365 - New or updated object trigger (batch)**: The New or updated object batch trigger in the Microsoft Dynamics 365 connector picks up newly created or updated records in batches each interval.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-dynamics-365/new-updated-object-trigger-real-time.md` — **Microsoft Dynamics 365 - New or updated object trigger (real-time)**: The New or updated object real-time trigger in the Microsoft Dynamics 365 connector runs immediately when a record is created or updated.

Triggers when records are created or updated.

- `docs/connectors/microsoft-dynamics-great-plains/update-record-action.md` — **Microsoft Dynamics Great Plains - Update record action**: The Update record action updates a new record in Microsoft Dynamics GP.

- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api.md` — **Microsoft Graph API**: Connect to the Microsoft Graph API connector using authorization code grant or client credentials authentication.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/create-record-action.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - Create record action**: The Create record action enables you to create a new record in Microsoft Graph API, such as a user.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/create-shareable-link-action.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - Create shareable link action**: The Create shareable link action creates a shareable link in Microsoft Graph API. The existing link is returned if a shareable link already exists.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/delete-record-action.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - Delete record action**: The Delete record action enables you to delete a record using its object ID.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/download-file-action.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - Download file action**: The Download file action downloads the contents of a file using its object ID.

- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/list-records-action.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - List records action**: The List records action lists records based on the object you select in Microsoft Graph API.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/move-mail-message-action.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - Move mail message action**: The Move mail message action moves a message to another folder within the specified user's mailbox. This creates a new copy of the message in the destination folder and removes the original message.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/new-email-trigger.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - New email trigger**: The New email trigger activates when a new email is received.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/new-mail-message-trigger.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - New mail message trigger**: The New mail message trigger checks for new events on a polling interval you specify.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/new-presence-trigger.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - New presence trigger**: The New presence trigger activates when there is a new presence change.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/new-row-in-sheet-trigger.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - New row in sheet trigger**: The New row in sheet trigger activates when a new Excel sheet row is created in Microsoft Graph API.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/search-records-action.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - Search records action**: The Search records action returns all records matching your search criteria.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/send-mail-message-action.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - Send mail message action**: The Send mail message action sends an existing draft message. Use the Create mail message action to send a new mail message.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/update-record-action.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - Update record action**: The Update record action enables you to update a record, such as a list, using its object ID.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-graph-api/upload-file-action.md` — **Microsoft Graph API - Upload file action**: The Upload file action enables you to upload a file by providing the file contents. If a file with the same name already exists in the folder, this action overwrites the file contents.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-onelake.md` — **Microsoft OneLake**: The Microsoft OneLake connector lets you automate data lake workflows by uploading and downloading files across your Microsoft Fabric tenant.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-onelake/download-file-action.md` — **Microsoft OneLake - Download file action**: The Download file action downloads the contents of an existing file from Microsoft OneLake storage.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-onelake/upload-file-action.md` — **Microsoft OneLake - Upload file action**: The Upload file action uploads a new file to Microsoft OneLake storage.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-teams.md` — **Microsoft Teams**: Microsoft Teams integrates group chat, video conferencing, secure file storage, and business apps in one hub.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word.md` — **Microsoft Word**: Set up and connect the Microsoft Word connector in Workato using OAuth 2.0 authorization code or client credentials authentication.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word/accept-tracked-change-action.md` — **Microsoft Word - Accept tracked change action**: The Accept tracked change action accepts a specific tracked change in a Word document by its ID.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word/add-comment-action.md` — **Microsoft Word - Add comment action**: The Add comment action adds a comment to a Word document, optionally anchored to a specific block.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word/create-document-action.md` — **Microsoft Word - Create document action**: The Create document action creates a new Word document, optionally from a template, XML content, or a binary upload.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word/download-document-action.md` — **Microsoft Word - Download document action**: The Download document action downloads a Word document as XML, binary (.docx), or PDF.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word/get-document-content-action.md` — **Microsoft Word - Get document content action**: The Get document content action returns structured content blocks from a Word document, with optional filtering and pagination.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word/get-document-info-action.md` — **Microsoft Word - Get document info action**: The Get document info action retrieves metadata and properties of a Word document without returning its content.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word/list-comments-action-batch.md` — **Microsoft Word - List comments action (batch)**: The List comments action lists comment threads in a Word document with filtering and pagination support.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word/list-tracked-changes-action-batch.md` — **Microsoft Word - List tracked changes action (batch)**: The List tracked changes action lists pending tracked changes in a Word document with authorship and location information.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word/reject-tracked-change-action.md` — **Microsoft Word - Reject tracked change action**: The Reject tracked change action rejects a specific tracked change in a Word document by its ID.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word/reopen-comment-action.md` — **Microsoft Word - Reopen comment action**: The Reopen comment action marks a resolved comment thread as unresolved in a Word document.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word/reply-to-comment-action.md` — **Microsoft Word - Reply to comment action**: The Reply to comment action adds a reply to an existing comment thread in a Word document.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word/resolve-comment-action.md` — **Microsoft Word - Resolve comment action**: The Resolve comment action marks an existing comment thread as resolved in a Word document.
- `docs/connectors/microsoft-word/update-document-content-action.md` — **Microsoft Word - Update document content action**: The Update document content action applies block-level updates or a full content replacement to a Word document, with revision safety enforcement.

- `docs/connectors/monday/delete-record-action.md` — **Monday - Delete record action**: The Delete record action enables you to delete a record on Monday.
- `docs/connectors/monday/get-record-action.md` — **Monday - Get record action**: The Get record action enables you to retrieve details of a specific record on Monday.

- `docs/connectors/monday/new-activity-log-trigger.md` — **Monday - New activity log trigger**: The New activity log trigger monitors Monday for new activity logs.
- `docs/connectors/monday/new-item-real-time-trigger.md` — **Monday - New item trigger (real-time)**: The New item (real-time) trigger runs the recipe instantly when a new item is created in Monday.

- `docs/connectors/monday/new-moved-item-to-group-real-time-trigger.md` — **Monday - New moved item to group trigger (real-time)**: The New moved item to group (real-time) trigger runs the recipe instantly when an item is moved to a group in Monday.
- `docs/connectors/monday/new-moved-item-to-group-trigger.md` — **Monday - New moved item to group trigger**: The New Moved Item to Group trigger runs the recipe when an item is moved to a group on Monday.
- `docs/connectors/monday/search-record-action.md` — **Monday - Search record action**: The Search record action enables you to retrieve a list of records that matches your search criteria on Monday.
- `docs/connectors/monday/update-any-column-value-real-time-trigger.md` — **Monday - Update any column value trigger (real-time)**: The Updated any column value (real-time) trigger runs the recipe instantly when an item is moved to any group in Monday.
- `docs/connectors/monday/update-any-column-value-trigger.md` — **Monday - Update any column value trigger**: The Update Any Column Value trigger runs the recipe when any column value is updated on Monday.
- `docs/connectors/monday/update-record-action.md` — **Monday - Update record action**: The Update record action enables you to update the details of a specific record on Monday.
- `docs/connectors/monday/update-specific-column-value-real-time-trigger.md` — **Monday - Updated specific column value trigger (real-time)**: The Updated specific column value (real-time) trigger runs the recipe instantly when a specific column value is updated in Monday.

- `docs/connectors/monday/upload-file-action.md` — **Monday - Upload file action**: The Upload File action enables you to upload the file on Monday.
- `docs/connectors/mongodb-atlas.md` — **MongoDB Atlas**: The MongoDB Atlas connector enables you to automate cloud database workflows, inserting, updating, searching, and replicating documents at scale.
- `docs/connectors/mongodb-atlas/actions/delete-documents.md` — **MongoDB Atlas - Delete documents action**: The Delete documents action in the MongoDB Atlas connector deletes documents from a collection that match an optional custom filter query.
- `docs/connectors/mongodb-atlas/actions/insert-documents.md` — **MongoDB Atlas - Insert documents action**: The Insert documents action in the MongoDB Atlas connector inserts new documents into a collection based on a defined schema and field mapping.
- `docs/connectors/mongodb-atlas/actions/replicate-documents.md` — **MongoDB Atlas - Replicate documents action**: The Replicate documents action in the MongoDB Atlas connector inserts or updates documents in a collection, de-duping records by unique fields.
- `docs/connectors/mongodb-atlas/actions/search-documents.md` — **MongoDB Atlas - Search documents action**: The Search documents action in the MongoDB Atlas connector retrieves documents from a collection, with optional custom filter query, quota, and offset.
- `docs/connectors/mongodb-atlas/actions/update-documents.md` — **MongoDB Atlas - Update documents action**: The Update documents action in the MongoDB Atlas connector updates documents in a collection that match an optional custom filter query.

- `docs/connectors/mongodb-atlas/guides/using-custom-filter-queries.md` — **Using Custom Filter Queries In MongoDB Actions**: Use custom filter queries to filter documents in MongoDB Atlas delete, search, and update actions with Extended JSON and BSON types.

- `docs/connectors/mssql.md` — **SQL Server**: The SQL Server connector automates relational database workflows including insert, update, upsert, delete, select, replicate, and custom SQL actions.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/bulk-load-from-on-prem-file.md` — **SQL Server - Bulk load from on-prem file action**: The Bulk load from on-prem file action in the SQL Server connector loads data from a CSV file on the on-prem agent into a target table.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/delete.md` — **SQL Server - Delete actions**: The Delete rows action in the SQL Server connector lets you delete one or more rows from a table that match a WHERE condition.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/export-query-result.md` — **SQL Server - Export query result action**: The Export query result action in the SQL Server connector runs a SQL query and exports the results as a CSV file over cloud or on-prem connections.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/insert.md` — **SQL Server - Insert actions**: Use the SQL Server insert actions in Workato to add a single row or a batch of rows to a selected table for higher-throughput data loads.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/introduction.md` — **SQL Server**: Connect Microsoft SQL Server to Workato using cloud or on-prem connections to migrate data, build pipelines, and automate database workflows.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/new-row-trigger.md` — **SQL Server - New row triggers**: The New row trigger in the SQL Server connector processes rows inserted into a selected table or view, with optional WHERE condition filtering.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/replicate.md` — **SQL Server - Replicate action**: The Replicate action in the SQL Server connector inserts or updates rows from a list datapill, creating new columns when the input schema changes.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/run_sql.md` — **SQL Server - Run custom SQL action**: The Run custom SQL action in the SQL Server connector lets you execute DML and DDL statements such as INSERT, UPDATE, CREATE, and DROP.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/scheduled-query-trigger.md` — **SQL Server - Scheduled query trigger**: The Scheduled query trigger in the SQL Server connector runs a SQL query on a set schedule and processes the returned rows in batches.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/select.md` — **SQL Server - Select actions**: Use the SQL Server select actions in Workato to return rows from a table or view that match a WHERE condition, including custom SQL queries.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/stored-procedure.md` — **SQL Server - Execute stored procedure action**: The Execute stored procedure action in the SQL Server connector lets you run stored procedures or functions and pass dynamic input parameters.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/trouble-shooting.md` — **SQL Server - Troubleshooting**: Troubleshoot common SQL Server recipe errors in Workato, including timeouts, connection failures, and other database design-time issues.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/update.md` — **SQL Server - Update actions**: Use the SQL Server update actions in Workato to update one or more rows with a set of values selected by a WHERE condition, in single or batch mode.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/updated-row-trigger.md` — **SQL Server - New/updated row triggers**: The New/updated row trigger in the SQL Server connector processes rows inserted or updated in a table or view, with optional WHERE condition filtering.
- `docs/connectors/mssql/upsert.md` — **SQL Server - Upsert actions**: Use the SQL Server upsert actions in Workato to insert or update a single row or a batch of rows in a table based on a primary key.
- `docs/connectors/mufg.md` — **MUFG**: The MUFG connector enables you to automate banking operations including account transaction inquiries, incoming transfer tracking, and money transfer requests through the MUFG corporate banking API.

You can filter transactions by date range and navigate through large result sets using pagination.

- `docs/connectors/mysql.md` — **MySQL**: The MySQL connector automates relational database workflows: insert, update, upsert, select, and delete rows, run custom SQL, and trigger on new rows.

- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-get-case-comments-action.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - Get case comments action**: The Get case comments action in the NetSuite SOAP connector retrieves the case comments of a specific case in NetSuite by its case ID.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-get-standard-objects-action.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - Get all standard objects action**: The Get all standard objects action in the NetSuite SOAP connector returns the entire list of records for a selected standard record type in your NetSuite app.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-new-records-batch-trigger.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - New/updated records trigger (batch)**: The New/updated records (batch) trigger in the NetSuite SOAP connector retrieves records created or updated since the last run, every five minutes in batches.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-new-records-trigger.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - New standard object and new custom object trigger**: The new standard object and new custom object triggers in the NetSuite SOAP connector pick up newly created records of the selected object type.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-new-saved-search-trigger.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - New saved search trigger**: The New saved search trigger in the NetSuite SOAP connector retrieves new records that meet the criteria of a saved search.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-recipe-migration.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - Migrating NetSuite recipes across environments**: Learn how to migrate NetSuite recipes across environments, including how custom field mappings are preserved when switching connections.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-search-custom-object-action.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - Search custom objects action**: The Search custom objects action in the NetSuite SOAP connector returns a list of records that match your search criteria, or an empty array if none match.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-search-standard-object-action.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - Search standard objects action**: The Search standard objects action in the NetSuite SOAP connector returns a list of records that match your search criteria, or an empty array if none match.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-unsupported-records.md` — **Unsupported records in NetSuite**: Reference for NetSuite records that triggers and search actions do not support because they cannot be queried through the NetSuite API.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-update-batch-action.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - Update records in bulk actions**: The Update standard and custom records in bulk actions in the NetSuite SOAP connector let you update standard or custom records in bulk.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-update-object-action.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - Update standard object and update custom object actions**: The Update standard object and update custom object actions in the NetSuite SOAP connector let you update existing standard or custom records in NetSuite.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-update-saved-search-trigger.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - New/updated saved search trigger**: The New/updated saved search trigger in the NetSuite SOAP connector retrieves new and updated records that meet the criteria of a saved search.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-updated-records-trigger.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - New/Updated standard object and new/updated custom object triggers**: The new/updated standard object and custom object triggers in the NetSuite SOAP connector pick up records created or updated after a set date and time.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/netsuite-upsert-batch-action.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - Upsert records in bulk actions**: The Upsert standard and custom records in bulk actions in the NetSuite SOAP connector let you upsert standard or custom records in bulk.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/new-custom-records-saved-search-batch.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - New custom records in a saved search trigger (batch)**: The New custom records in a saved search trigger (batch) retrieves batches of new custom records from a saved search in NetSuite.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/new-standard-records-saved-search-batch.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - New standard records in a saved search trigger (batch)**: The New standard records in a saved search trigger (batch) retrieves batches of new standard records from a saved search in NetSuite.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/new-updated-custom-records-saved-search-batch.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - New/updated custom records in a saved search trigger (batch)**: The New/updated custom records in a saved search trigger (batch) retrieves batches of new or updated custom records from a saved search in NetSuite.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/new-updated-standard-records-saved-search-batch.md` — **NetSuite SOAP - New/updated standard records in a saved search trigger (batch)**: The New/updated custom records in a saved search trigger (batch) retrieves batches of new or updated standard records from a saved search in NetSuite.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/troubleshooting/troubleshoot-netsuite-connection.md` — **Troubleshoot NetSuite connection errors**: Use this guide to troubleshoot errors that occur when establishing a NetSuite connection in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/netsuite/troubleshooting/troubleshoot-netsuite-runtime.md` — **Troubleshoot NetSuite runtime errors**: Use this guide to troubleshoot errors that occur at runtime when you use the Netsuite connector in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/nocodb.md` — **NocoDB**: Connect Workato to NocoDB Cloud or self-hosted instances to search, create, update, and delete table rows, and trigger recipes when records change.
- `docs/connectors/nocodb/count-records-action.md` — **NocoDB - Count records action**: Count the rows in a NocoDB table, optionally filtered with a where clause.
- `docs/connectors/nocodb/create-records-action.md` — **NocoDB - Create records (batch) action**: Create one or more rows in a NocoDB table with a single request.
- `docs/connectors/nocodb/delete-records-action.md` — **NocoDB - Delete records (batch) action**: Permanently delete one or more rows from a NocoDB table by primary key ID.
- `docs/connectors/nocodb/get-record-action.md` — **NocoDB - Get record action**: Retrieve a single NocoDB row by primary key ID.
- `docs/connectors/nocodb/manage-linked-records-action.md` — **NocoDB - Manage linked records (batch) action**: List, link, or unlink related rows for a LinkToAnotherRecord field in a NocoDB table.
- `docs/connectors/nocodb/new-updated-deleted-record-trigger.md` — **NocoDB - New/updated/deleted record (real-time) trigger**: Start a recipe in real time when rows in a NocoDB table are inserted, updated, or deleted, using a webhook.
- `docs/connectors/nocodb/new-updated-record-trigger.md` — **NocoDB - New/updated record trigger**: Start a recipe when rows in a NocoDB table are created or updated, using polling ordered by UpdatedAt.
- `docs/connectors/nocodb/search-records-action.md` — **NocoDB - Search records action**: List rows in a NocoDB table with filter, sort, and paging options, or list the tables in a base.
- `docs/connectors/nocodb/update-records-action.md` — **NocoDB - Update records (batch) action**: Update one or more rows in a NocoDB table by primary key ID with a single request.
- `docs/connectors/notion.md` — **Notion**: The Notion connector lets you automate workflows: create, update, search, and get records, query databases, and trigger on new or updated database records.
- `docs/connectors/notion/create-record-action.md` — **Notion - Create record action**: The Create record action in the Notion connector lets you create a new object inside a page or database in Notion.
- `docs/connectors/notion/get-record-details-by-id-action.md` — **Notion - Get record details by ID action**: The Get record details by ID action in the Notion connector retrieves data about a page, database, or block object in Notion.
- `docs/connectors/notion/new-or-updated-database-record-trigger.md` — **Notion - New/updated database record trigger**: The New/updated database record trigger in the Notion connector fires when a record is created or updated in the database you monitor.
- `docs/connectors/notion/query-database-action.md` — **Notion - Query database action**: The Query database action in the Notion connector searches a database and returns the records that match the filter and sort criteria you specify.
- `docs/connectors/notion/search-records-action.md` — **Notion - Search records action**: The Search records action in the Notion connector retrieves a list of Notion objects matching the object type you specify.
- `docs/connectors/notion/update-record-action.md` — **Notion - Update record action**: The Update record action in the Notion connector lets you update an existing object, such as a page, database, or block, in Notion.
- `docs/connectors/odata.md` — **OData**: The OData connector lets you automate workflows with any OData API: create, update, delete, search, and get records, and trigger on new or updated records.
- `docs/connectors/odata/batch-requests-action.md` — **OData - Batch requests action**: The Batch requests action sends batch requests for multiple objects using Odata with Workato.
- `docs/connectors/odata/create-record-action.md` — **OData - Create record action**: The Create record action enables you to create a record in OData.
- `docs/connectors/odata/delete-record-action.md` — **OData - Delete record action**: The Delete record action enables you to delete a record in OData.
- `docs/connectors/odata/execute-operation-action.md` — **OData - Execute operation action**: The Execute operation action enables you to execute an operation in OData.
- `docs/connectors/odata/get-record-details-by-id-action.md` — **OData - Get record details by ID action**: The Get record details by ID action enables you to get record details by searching by ID.
- `docs/connectors/odata/new-record-trigger.md` — **OData - New record trigger**: The New record trigger in the OData connector fires when a new record is created for the object you select.
- `docs/connectors/odata/new-updated-record-trigger.md` — **OData - New/updated record trigger**: The New/updated record trigger in the OData connector fires when a record is created or updated in the object you select.
- `docs/connectors/odata/search-records-action.md` — **OData - Search records action**: The Search records action enables you to search records in OData.
- `docs/connectors/odata/update-record-action.md` — **OData - Update record action**: The Update record action allows you to update a record in OData.
- `docs/connectors/odoo-json2.md` — **Odoo (JSON-2)**: Connect Workato to Odoo's JSON-2 API to create, update, search, and sync records across any Odoo object, run object functions, and complete guided wizards.
- `docs/connectors/odoo-json2/create-record-action.md` — **Odoo (JSON-2) - Create record action**: Create a new record of any Odoo object in Odoo.
- `docs/connectors/odoo-json2/get-record-by-id-action.md` — **Odoo (JSON-2) - Get record by ID action**: Get a record of any Odoo object from Odoo based on its ID.
- `docs/connectors/odoo-json2/new-updated-object-trigger.md` — **Odoo (JSON-2) - New/updated object trigger**: Triggers when a record of the selected Odoo object is created or updated in Odoo.
- `docs/connectors/odoo-json2/quotations-by-validity-date-action.md` — **Odoo (JSON-2) - Quotations by validity date action**: Retrieve quotations (sales orders) from Odoo filtered by their validity date.
- `docs/connectors/odoo-json2/run-object-function-action.md` — **Odoo (JSON-2) - Run object function action**: The Run object function action performs a function on a record in Odoo.
- `docs/connectors/odoo-json2/run-wizard-action.md` — **Odoo (JSON-2) - Run wizard action**: Complete a guided process in Odoo, such as registering a payment or creating a credit note.
- `docs/connectors/odoo-json2/search-records-action.md` — **Odoo (JSON-2) - Search records action**: Search for records of any Odoo object based on specified criteria.
- `docs/connectors/odoo-json2/update-record-action.md` — **Odoo (JSON-2) - Update record action**: Update an existing record of any Odoo object in Odoo.
- `docs/connectors/okta.md` — **Okta**: The Okta connector lets you automate identity workflows: create, update, delete, activate, and suspend users, manage group membership, and trigger on events.
- `docs/connectors/okta/activate-user-action.md` — **Okta - Activate user action**: The Activate user action in the Okta connector activates a staged user, with an option to send an activation email or return activation credentials.
- `docs/connectors/okta/add-user-to-group-action.md` — **Okta - Add user to group action**: The Add user to group action in the Okta connector adds a user to a group, with both identified by their unique Okta ID.
- `docs/connectors/okta/create-user-action.md` — **Okta - Create user action**: The Create user action in the Okta connector creates a new user, with profile, credentials, and an option to activate the user on creation.
- `docs/connectors/okta/deactivate-user-action.md` — **Okta - Deactivate user action**: The Deactivate user action in the Okta connector deactivates a user and sets the status to deprovisioned, a destructive, unrecoverable operation.
- `docs/connectors/okta/delete-user-action.md` — **Okta - Delete user action**: The Delete user action in the Okta connector permanently deletes a deprovisioned user, an operation that cannot be recovered.
- `docs/connectors/okta/events-output-datatree.md` — **Okta - Output datatree for events**: Reference for the Okta events output datatree, covering actor, client, authentication, security, transaction, and target fields for log events.
- `docs/connectors/okta/expire-an-existing-user-password.md` — **Okta - Expire an existing user password action**: The Expire an existing user password action in the Okta connector expires a user password and can optionally return a temporary password.
- `docs/connectors/okta/get-group-members-action.md` — **Okta - Get user group members action**: The Get user group members action in the Okta connector retrieves the list of users belonging to a specified group, with status and profile data.
- `docs/connectors/okta/get-groups-action.md` — **Okta - Get groups by name action**: The Get groups by name action in the Okta connector retrieves all groups whose names start with a given value, with their profile details.
- `docs/connectors/okta/get-recent-events-by-ip-action.md` — **Okta - Get recent log on events by IP address action**: The Get recent log on events by IP address action in the Okta connector lets you retrieve up to 100 log events from the last 12 hours for an IP.
- `docs/connectors/okta/get-recent-events-by-user-action.md` — **Okta - Get recent log on events by user action**: The Get recent log on events by user action in the Okta connector lets you retrieve up to 100 log events from the last 12 hours for a specific user.
- `docs/connectors/okta/get-user-by-id-action.md` — **Okta - Get user by ID action**: The Get user by ID action in the Okta connector retrieves the account details of a specific user, including status, profile, and credentials.
- `docs/connectors/okta/get-user-groups-action.md` — **Okta - Get user groups action**: The Get user groups action in the Okta connector retrieves the group memberships of a specific user, including group ID and profile details.
- `docs/connectors/okta/list-applications-assigned-to-user.md` — **Okta - List applications assigned to user action**: The List applications assigned to user action in the Okta connector returns the applications assigned to a user with their metadata.
- `docs/connectors/okta/new-events-trigger.md` — **Okta - New events trigger**: The New events trigger in the Okta connector fires when new events are created in your instance, with both polling and real-time options.
- `docs/connectors/okta/remove-user-from-group-action.md` — **Okta - Remove user from group action**: The Remove user from group action in the Okta connector removes a user from a group, with both identified by their unique Okta ID.
- `docs/connectors/okta/reset-user-password.md` — **Okta - Reset an existing user's forgotten password action**: The Okta connector action resets an active user's forgotten password and can optionally email a one-time token link to the user.
- `docs/connectors/okta/scheduled-search-trigger.md` — **Okta - Scheduled event search trigger**: The Scheduled event search trigger in the Okta connector runs a search of log events on a set schedule and returns matching events in batches.
- `docs/connectors/okta/suspend-user.md` — **Okta - Suspend an existing user action**: The Suspend an existing user action in the Okta connector sets an active user to suspended status, and requires an administrator role to use.
- `docs/connectors/okta/troubleshooting.md` — **Okta troubleshooting guide**: Troubleshoot common Okta connection errors in Workato, including 302 Found, 400 Bad Request, and 403 Forbidden OAuth 2.0 setup issues.
- `docs/connectors/okta/unsuspend-user.md` — **Okta - Un-suspend an existing user action**: The Un-suspend an existing user action in the Okta connector returns a suspended user to active status, and requires an administrator role.
- `docs/connectors/okta/update-user-action.md` — **Okta - Update user action**: The Update user action in the Okta connector updates an existing user identified by user ID, including profile fields like name, email, and title.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-command-line-scripts.md` — **On-prem command-line scripts**: The On-prem command-line scripts connector is a utility connector that runs allowlisted command-line scripts in your private network through the on-prem agent.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files.md` — **On-prem files**: The On-prem files connector lets you upload, download, move, rename, and delete files, manage folders and CSV files, and trigger on new files in your network.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/append-csv-line-action.md` — **On-prem files - Append line to CSV file action**: The Append line to CSV file action in the On-prem files connector lets you add a new line to an existing on-prem CSV file.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/create-folder.md` — **On-prem files - Create folder action**: The Create folder action in the On-prem files connector lets you create a new folder in your on-prem directory at a path you specify.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/delete-file-action.md` — **On-prem files - Delete file action**: The Delete file action in the On-prem files connector lets you delete an existing on-prem file at the relative path you specify.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/delete-folder-action.md` — **On-prem files - Delete folder action**: The Delete folder action in the On-prem files connector lets you delete an existing on-prem folder, with an option to skip folders that aren't empty.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/download-file-action.md` — **On-prem files - Download file action**: The Download file action in the On-prem files connector lets you download an existing on-prem file and return its raw contents, with file streaming support.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/generate-on-prem-file-url.md` — **On-prem files - Generate on-prem file URL action**: The Generate on-prem file URL action in the On-prem files connector lets you generate a URL that points to a file in your on-prem directory.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/list-files-action.md` — **On-prem files - List files in folder action (Batch)**: The List files in folder batch action in the On-prem files connector lets you list all files in an on-prem folder, sorted alphabetically by path.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/move-file-action.md` — **On-prem files - Move file action**: The Move file action in the On-prem files connector lets you move an existing on-prem file to a destination folder you specify.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/new-line-trigger.md` — **On-prem files - New line in CSV file trigger**: The New line in CSV file trigger in the On-prem files connector picks up newly added lines from an on-prem CSV file each poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/new-lines-trigger.md` — **On-prem files - New lines in CSV file trigger (Batch)**: The New lines in CSV file batch trigger in the On-prem files connector picks up newly added lines from an on-prem CSV file each poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/rename-file-action.md` — **On-prem files - Rename file action**: The Rename file action in the On-prem files connector lets you rename an existing on-prem file to a new name you specify.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/trigger-new-csv.md` — **On-prem file trigger - New CSV file in folder trigger (Batch)**: The New CSV file in folder batch trigger in the On-prem files connector picks up new CSV files added to an on-prem folder on a schedule you define.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/trigger-new-file.md` — **On-prem files - New file in folder trigger**: The New file in folder trigger monitors an on-prem folder for new files on a schedule you define.
- `docs/connectors/on-prem-files/upload-file-action.md` — **On-prem files - Upload file action**: The Upload file action in the On-prem files connector lets you upload a file to an existing on-prem folder, with support for large file sizes.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive.md` — **OneDrive**: The OneDrive connector enables you to integrate and automate workflows to support file processing use cases.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/add-permission-action.md` — **OneDrive - Add permission action**: The Add permission action in the OneDrive connector grants read or write access to a file or folder for specified recipients in Microsoft OneDrive.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/create-folder-action.md` — **OneDrive - Create folder action**: The Create folder action in the OneDrive connector creates a new folder under a specified parent path in Microsoft OneDrive.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/delete-file-folder-action.md` — **OneDrive - Delete file or folder action**: The Delete file or folder action in the OneDrive connector moves a specified file or folder to the recycle bin in Microsoft OneDrive.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/download-file-action.md` — **OneDrive - Download file or folder action (file)**: The Download file or folder action in the OneDrive connector downloads the contents of a file from Microsoft OneDrive for use in later recipe steps.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/list-files-folder-action.md` — **OneDrive - List files and folders action (batch)**: The List files and folders batch action in the OneDrive connector lists files and folders in a selected parent folder in Microsoft OneDrive.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/list-permissions-action.md` — **OneDrive - List permissions action (batch)**: The List permissions batch action in the OneDrive connector lists the permissions assigned to a file or folder in Microsoft OneDrive.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/new-event-trigger.md` — **OneDrive - New event trigger (real-time)**: The New event real-time trigger in the OneDrive connector monitors created, updated, or deleted files and folders in a specified Microsoft OneDrive folder.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/new-file-trigger.md` — **OneDrive - New file trigger**: The New file trigger in the OneDrive connector picks up new files created in a specified Microsoft OneDrive folder on each poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/new-folder-trigger.md` — **OneDrive - New folder trigger**: The New folder trigger in the OneDrive connector picks up new folders created in a specified Microsoft OneDrive folder on each poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/new-line-csv-file-trigger.md` — **OneDrive - New line in CSV file trigger**: The New line in CSV file trigger in the OneDrive connector picks up new lines added to a specified CSV file in Microsoft OneDrive as separate events.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/new-updated-file-trigger.md` — **OneDrive - New/updated file trigger**: The New/updated file trigger in the OneDrive connector picks up new or updated files in a specified Microsoft OneDrive folder on each poll interval.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/remove-permission-action.md` — **OneDrive - Remove permission action**: The Remove permission action in the OneDrive connector removes a specified permission from a file or folder in Microsoft OneDrive.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/search-files-action.md` — **OneDrive - Search files action (batch)**: The Search files action in the OneDrive connector searches a folder and its subfolders in Microsoft OneDrive by file name, metadata, or content.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/upload-file-file-content-action.md` — **OneDrive - Upload file using file content action**: The Upload file using file content action in the OneDrive connector uploads a file up to 4MB to Microsoft OneDrive from its text or binary content.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/upload-file-url-action.md` — **OneDrive - Upload file from URL action (file)**: The Upload file from URL action in the OneDrive connector uploads a file from a public URL to Microsoft OneDrive, overwriting any file with the same name.
- `docs/connectors/onedrive/upload-large-file-session-action.md` — **OneDrive - Upload large file using session action (file)**: The Upload large file using session action in the OneDrive connector uploads large files to Microsoft OneDrive in chunks using a file session.
- `docs/connectors/onetrust.md` — **OneTrust**: The OneTrust connector lets you create, retrieve, search, update, and reassign assessments, and retrieve users in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/onetrust/create-record-action.md` — **OneTrust - Create record action**: The Create record action creates an assessment in OneTrust.
- `docs/connectors/onetrust/get-record-action.md` — **OneTrust - Get record action**: The Get record action retrieves an assessment, user, or assessment workflow stage from OneTrust.
- `docs/connectors/onetrust/reassign-record-action.md` — **OneTrust - Reassign record action**: The Reassign record action reassigns an assessment in OneTrust.
- `docs/connectors/onetrust/search-record-action.md` — **OneTrust - Search record action**: The Search record action retrieves a list of assessments or users from OneTrust.
- `docs/connectors/onetrust/update-record-action.md` — **OneTrust - Update record action**: The Update record action updates an assessment in OneTrust.
- `docs/connectors/opa-webhook.md` — **OPA Webhook**: OPA Webhook is a webhook HTTP request forwarder. OPA Webhook runs on a Workato On‑Premise Agent (OPA). This connector lets internal services send webhook events to Workato.
- `docs/connectors/opa-webhook/new-webhook-event-trigger.md` — **OPA Webhook - New webhook event trigger**: The New webhook event trigger monitors OPA Webhook for new webhook requests.
- `docs/connectors/openai.md` — **OpenAI**: The OpenAI connector enables you to automate AI workflows, generating text and images, classifying text, and running models through the OpenAI API in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/openai/business-actions.md` — **OpenAI - Business actions**: Use the pre-configured OpenAI business actions in Workato to analyze, categorize, parse, translate, summarize text, and draft email without writing prompts.
- `docs/connectors/openai/chatgpt-conversation.md` — **OpenAI - Send messages to ChatGPT action**: The Send messages to ChatGPT action in the OpenAI connector lets you send questions or build a chat experience with ChatGPT models in your recipes.
- `docs/connectors/openai/edit-text.md` — **OpenAI - Edit text action**: The Edit text action in the OpenAI connector revises input text based on your instructions to improve clarity, grammar, and conciseness.
- `docs/connectors/openai/generate-images.md` — **OpenAI - Generate images action**: The Generate images action in the OpenAI connector creates images from text prompts, edits images, or makes variations using Dall-E models.
- `docs/connectors/openai/generate-text-embedding.md` — **OpenAI - Generate text embedding action**: The Generate text embedding action in the OpenAI connector turns text into numerical vectors for classification, similarity, and other NLP tasks.
- `docs/connectors/openai/moderate-text.md` — **OpenAI - Moderate text action**: The Moderate text action in the OpenAI connector checks whether input text violates content policy so you can filter, classify, and route content.
- `docs/connectors/openai/send-text-prompt.md` — **OpenAI - Send text prompt action**: The Send text prompt action in the OpenAI connector generates text completions for a prompt to autocomplete text, answer questions, or create content.
- `docs/connectors/openai/transcribe-recording.md` — **OpenAI - Transcribe recording action**: The Transcribe recording action in the OpenAI connector converts audio files such as mp3, mp4, wav, or webm into text you can use in further steps.
- `docs/connectors/openai/translate-recording.md` — **OpenAI - Translate recording action**: The Translate recording action in the OpenAI connector translates audio files such as mp3, mp4, wav, or webm into text you can use in further steps.
- `docs/connectors/openapi.md` — **OpenAPI**: Use the OpenAPI connector in Workato to connect any application with an OpenAPI-compliant API, with actions and triggers generated from a Swagger file.
- `docs/connectors/openapi/create-record-action.md` — **OpenAPI - Create record action**: Create a record via OpenAPI.
- `docs/connectors/openapi/custom-action.md` — **OpenAPI - Custom action**: The Custom action allows you to build and execute an HTTP request with your OpenAPI connection.
- `docs/connectors/openapi/delete-record-action.md` — **OpenAPI - Delete record action**: Delete a record via OpenAPI.
- `docs/connectors/openapi/execute-operation-action.md` — **OpenAPI - Execute operation action**: Execute an operation via OpenAPI.
- `docs/connectors/openapi/get-record-details-by-id-action.md` — **OpenAPI - Get record details by ID action**: Retrieve the details of a record by ID via OpenAPI.
- `docs/connectors/openapi/guides/authentication.md` — **OpenAPI Connector Authentication**: Reference for the authentication methods supported by the OpenAPI connector, including None, Basic, Header, Query params, and OAuth 2.0 grants.

- `docs/connectors/openapi/new-record-trigger.md` — **OpenAPI - New/updated record trigger**: The New/updated record trigger in the OpenAPI connector runs when a record of the selected object is created or updated in the connected API.
