- `docs/connectors/oracle-ebs.md` — **Oracle E-Business Suite**: Connect to Oracle E-Business Suite to manage enterprise resources, automate business processes, and integrate ERP, CRM, and supply chain operations.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-ebs/actions.md` — **Oracle E-Business Suite - Actions**: Browse the actions supported by the Oracle E-Business Suite connector in Workato to read and write data in your recipes.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-ebs/actions/execute-pl-sql-operation.md` — **Oracle E-Business Suite - Execute PL/SQL operation action**: The Execute PL/SQL operation action executes  PL/SQL-based REST services deployed in your Oracle E-Business Suite system.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-ebs/triggers.md` — **Oracle E-Business Suite - Triggers**: Browse the triggers supported by the Oracle E-Business Suite connector in Workato to start recipes from Oracle business events.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-ebs/triggers/new-business-event.md` — **Oracle E-Business Suite - New business event trigger**: The New business event trigger monitors business events in Oracle E-Business Suite such as invoice creation, order status changes, or employee updates.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-ebs/triggers/new-custom-business-event.md` — **Oracle E-Business Suite - New custom business event trigger**: The New custom business event trigger monitors custom business events in Oracle E-Business Suite.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud**: The Oracle Fusion Cloud connector lets you create, update, get, and search records, run bulk imports and ESS jobs, and trigger on new or updated records.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Actions**: Browse the actions available in the Oracle Fusion Cloud connector for Workato to create and manage records on Fusion Cloud objects.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/append-file-comment.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Append file comment action**: The Append file comment action adds a comment to a document in Oracle Fusion Cloud's Universal Content Management (UCM) system.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/confirm-extract-consumption.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Confirm extract consumption action**: The Confirm extract consumption action acknowledges and confirms that the download for a specific extract has completed.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/create-record.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a new record for a specified object in Oracle Fusion Cloud
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/create-records-batch.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Create records action (batch)**: The Create records batch action creates new records for a specified object in Oracle Fusion Cloud in batches.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/create-user.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Create user action**: The Create user action creates a user in Oracle Fusion Cloud using HCM Data Loader.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/delete-record.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Delete record action**: The Delete record action deletes an existing record.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/download-ess-job-execution-details.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Download ESS job execution details action**: The Download ESS job execution details action downloads the Enterprise Scheduling Service (ESS) job output and logs as a ZIP file for specified ESS request ID.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/download-export-output.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Download export output action**: The Download export output action downloads the Enterprise Scheduling Service (ESS) job output and logs as a ZIP file for the specified ESS request ID.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/export-bulk-data.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Export bulk data action**: The Export bulk data action submits an ESS job request to export large datasets from Oracle Fusion Cloud.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/extract-and-purge.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Extract and purge action**: The Extract and purge action extracts data from Oracle Fusion Cloud and removes the extracted records from the source system.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/fetch-extract-output.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Fetch extract output action**: The Fetch extract output action retrieves the archived XML output produced by an extract process.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/get-flow-task-instance-status.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Get flow task instance status action**: The Get flow task instance status action retrieves the current execution status of a specific task within a flow instance in Oracle Fusion Cloud's Human Capital Management (HCM) system.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/get-record.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Get record action**: The Get record action retrieves a specific record by its internal ID.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/import-bulk-data.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Import bulk data action**: The Import bulk data action uploads a file to the Oracle Universal Content Management (UCM) server based on the document details specified and submits an ESS job to load and import the uploaded files to an application table.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/list-entities.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - List entities action**: The List entities action retrieves the list of service data objects associated with the ERP Integration Service.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/load-and-import-data.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Load and import data action**: The Load and import data action uploads a file to the Oracle Universal Content Management (UCM) server based on the document details specified and submits an ESS job(s) to load and import the uploaded files to an application table.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/search-documents-by-file-prefix.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Search documents by file prefix action**: The Search documents by file prefix action searches and retrieves document information and content from Oracle Fusion Cloud based on a file prefix pattern.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/search-records-batch.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Search records action**: The Search records batch action allows searching by specifying attribute values directly.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/search-records-using-advanced-query-batch.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Search records using advanced query action (batch)**: The Search records using advanced query batch action searches for records using flexible query options.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/submit-and-get-flow-instance-id.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Submit and get flow instance ID action**: The Submit and get flow instance ID action creates and submits a new flow instance in Oracle Fusion Cloud's Human Capital Management (HCM) system and returns the unique instance identifier.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/submit-ess-job-request.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Submit ESS job request action**: The Submit ESS job request action submits an Enterprise Scheduling Service (ESS) job request for the specified job definition.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/submit-job-with-output.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Submit job with output action**: The Submit job with output action submits an Enterprise Scheduling Service (ESS) job request for a specified job definition and sends a callback message to the specified callback URL when the job completes.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/update-interface-data.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Update interface data action**: The Update interface data action updates error data in interface tables pertaining to the specified import process and load request ID.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/update-record.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Update record action**: The Update record action updates an existing record.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/update-records-batch.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Update records action (batch)**: The Update records batch action updates existing records in batches.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/upload-to-ucm.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Upload to UCM action**: The Upload to UCM action uploads a document to the Universal Content Management (UCM) server based on provided document details.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/actions/upsert-record.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Upsert record action**: The Upsert record action creates or updates a record.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/general-setup.md` — **General setup**: Set up the metadata-based Oracle Fusion Cloud connector in Workato, including object selection and mapping main and child object fields at design time.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/triggers.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - Triggers**: Browse the triggers available in the Oracle Fusion Cloud connector for Workato to start recipes from events on Fusion Cloud objects.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/triggers/new-business-event.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - New business event trigger**: The New business event trigger monitors Oracle Fusion Cloud for new business events and triggers immediately when they occur.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/triggers/new-employee-atom-feed-entry.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - New employee atom feed entry trigger**: The New employee atom feed entry trigger detects and retrieves new employee-related events from Oracle Fusion Cloud's atom feed.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/triggers/new-organization-atom-feed-entry.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - New organization atom feed entry trigger**: The New organization atom feed entry trigger detects and retrieves new organization-related events from Oracle Fusion Cloud's atom feed.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/triggers/new-record-batch.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - New record trigger (batch)**: The New record batch trigger detects when new records are created in Oracle Fusion Cloud, processing multiple records at once for any supported object type.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/triggers/new-record.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - New record trigger**: The New record trigger detects when a new record is created in Oracle Fusion Cloud and can be configured to monitor any supported object type.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/triggers/new-updated-record-batch.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - New/updated record trigger (batch)**: The New/updated record batch trigger detects when new records are created or existing records are updated in Oracle Fusion Cloud, processing multiple records at once for any supported object type.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-fusion-cloud/triggers/new-updated-record.md` — **Oracle Fusion Cloud - New/updated record trigger**: The New/updated record trigger detects when a new record is created or an existing record is updated in Oracle Fusion Cloud and can be configured to monitor any supported object type.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-object-storage.md` — **Oracle Object Storage**: The Oracle Object Storage connector lets you upload and download objects, including streams, and create, get, search, and delete stored records.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-object-storage/create-record-action.md` — **Oracle Object Storage - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a new record in Oracle Object Storage.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-object-storage/delete-record-action.md` — **Oracle Object Storage - Delete record action**: The Delete record action deletes a record from Oracle Object Storage.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-object-storage/download-object-action.md` — **Oracle Object Storage - Download object action**: The Download object action downloads an object from Oracle Object Storage.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-object-storage/download-object-stream-action.md` — **Oracle Object Storage - Download object (stream) action**: The Download object (stream) action downloads an object from Oracle Object Storage.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-object-storage/get-record-action.md` — **Oracle Object Storage - Get record action**: The Get record action retrieves details about a record from Oracle Object Storage.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-object-storage/search-records-action.md` — **Oracle Object Storage - Search records action**: The Search records action retrieves a list of records for an object in Oracle Object Storage that match your search criteria.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-object-storage/upload-object-action.md` — **Oracle Object Storage - Upload object action**: The Upload object action uploads an object to Oracle Object Storage.
- `docs/connectors/oracle-object-storage/upload-object-stream-action.md` — **Oracle Object Storage - Upload object (stream) action**: The Upload object (stream) action uploads an object to Oracle Object Storage.
- `docs/connectors/oracle.md` — **Oracle**: The Oracle connector enables you to automate database workflows in Workato by selecting, inserting, upserting, and deleting rows or running custom SQL.
- `docs/connectors/oracle/delete.md` — **Oracle - Delete actions**: The Delete rows action in the Oracle connector lets you delete rows from a table based on a WHERE condition you define.
- `docs/connectors/oracle/export-query-result.md` — **Oracle - Export query result action**: The Export query result action in the Oracle connector runs a SQL query and exports the results as a CSV file to a local directory or cloud storage.
- `docs/connectors/oracle/insert.md` — **Oracle - Insert actions**: The Insert actions in the Oracle connector let you add a single row or a batch of rows to a selected table in your Oracle database.

- `docs/connectors/oracle/new-row-trigger.md` — **Oracle - New row triggers**: The New row trigger in the Oracle connector picks up rows inserted in a selected table or view, processing each new row as a separate job.
- `docs/connectors/oracle/run_sql.md` — **Oracle - Run custom SQL action**: The Run custom SQL action in the Oracle connector lets you send a SQL string to your database to run DML statements and DDL commands.
- `docs/connectors/oracle/select.md` — **Oracle - Select actions**: The Select actions in the Oracle connector let you retrieve rows from a table or view that match a WHERE condition you define.
- `docs/connectors/oracle/stored-procedure.md` — **Oracle - Execute stored procedure action**: The Execute stored procedure action in the Oracle connector lets you run a stored procedure or function in your database with dynamic input parameters.

- `docs/connectors/oracle/update.md` — **Oracle - Update actions**: The Update actions in the Oracle connector let you update one or more rows with a set of values, using a WHERE condition to select the rows.
- `docs/connectors/oracle/updated-row-trigger.md` — **Oracle - New/updated row triggers**: The New/updated row trigger in the Oracle connector picks up rows inserted or updated in a selected table or view, processing each as a separate job.
- `docs/connectors/oracle/upsert.md` — **Oracle - Upsert actions**: The Upsert actions in the Oracle connector let you insert or update a single row or a batch of rows in a table based on the primary key value.
- `docs/connectors/oracle/working-with-the-oracle-connector.md` — **Working with the Oracle connector**: Best practices for working with the Oracle connector in Workato, including tables, views, stored procedures, and WHERE conditions.

- `docs/connectors/outlook/delete-contacts-action.md` — **Outlook - Delete contact action**: The Delete contact action in the Outlook connector lets you delete an existing contact from the contact folder you specify by its ID.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/deleted-event-trigger.md` — **Outlook - Deleted event trigger (real-time)**: The Deleted event trigger in the Outlook connector monitors your calendar or a shared calendar and fires instantly when an event is deleted.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/download-email-attachments-action.md` — **Outlook - Download email attachments action**: The Download email attachments action in the Outlook connector lets you download attachments from a specific email in Outlook Mail.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/get-calendar-action.md` — **Outlook - Get calendar by ID action**: The Get calendar by ID action in the Outlook connector lets you retrieve the details of a specific calendar using its calendar ID.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/get-calendar-event-action.md` — **Outlook - Get calendar event by ID action**: The Get calendar event by ID action in the Outlook connector lets you retrieve an event from a specific calendar using its event ID.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/get-contact-action.md` — **Outlook - Get contact action**: The Get contact action in the Outlook connector lets you retrieve an existing contact from the contact folder you specify by its ID.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/list-calendars-action.md` — **Outlook - List calendars action**: The List calendars action in the Outlook connector lets you retrieve all calendars from your account, with paging for additional records.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/list-contacts-action.md` — **Outlook - List contacts action (batch)**: The List contacts action in the Outlook connector lets you retrieve contacts from a specific folder in batches, with paging beyond the first 200.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/list-events-action.md` — **Outlook - List all instances of an event action (batch)**: The List all instances of an event action in the Outlook connector lets you list every occurrence of a recurring calendar event series.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/new-contact-trigger.md` — **Outlook - New contact trigger**: The New contact trigger in the Outlook connector picks up new contacts added to the Outlook contact folder you monitor.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/new-event-trigger.md` — **Outlook - New event trigger (real-time)**: The New event trigger in the Outlook connector picks up new calendar events in real time and processes each event as a separate job.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/new-updated-contact-trigger.md` — **Outlook - New/updated contact trigger**: The New/updated contact trigger in the Outlook connector picks up new or updated contacts in the Outlook contact folder you monitor.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/new-updated-event-trigger.md` — **Outlook - New/updated event trigger (real-time)**: The New/updated event trigger in the Outlook connector picks up new or updated calendar events in real time and processes each as a separate job.

- `docs/connectors/outlook/search-calendar-action.md` — **Outlook - Search calendar action (batch)**: The Search calendar action in the Outlook connector lets you retrieve calendars that match your criteria, with batches of up to 1000 results.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/search-calendar-events-action.md` — **Outlook - Search calendar events action (batch)**: The Search calendar events action in the Outlook connector lets you search a specific calendar for events within a date range, in batches.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/search-contacts-action.md` — **Outlook - Search contacts action (batch)**: The Search contacts action in the Outlook connector lets you find contacts in a folder that match your criteria, returning 200 per batch.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/send-email-action.md` — **Outlook - Send email action**: The Send email action in the Outlook connector lets you send a plain text or HTML email to specified recipients from Outlook Mail.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/trigger-new-email.md` — **Outlook - New email trigger (real-time)**: The New email trigger in the Outlook connector monitors an inbox or shared mailbox and fires in real time when new emails arrive.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/update-calendar-event-action.md` — **Outlook - Update calendar event action**: The Update calendar event action in the Outlook connector lets you update an existing calendar event identified by its event ID.
- `docs/connectors/outlook/update-contacts-action.md` — **Outlook - Update contact action**: The Update contact action in the Outlook connector lets you update an existing contact in the contact folder you specify by its ID.
- `docs/connectors/outreach.md` — **Outreach**: The Outreach connector lets you create, update, and search records, add prospects to sequences, and trigger on new or updated objects and completed tasks.
- `docs/connectors/outreach/action.md` — **Outreach - Actions**: Actions in the Outreach connector let you create, update, and retrieve records like prospects, accounts, sequences, and tasks, or add prospects to sequences.
- `docs/connectors/outreach/trigger.md` — **Outreach - Triggers**: Triggers in the Outreach connector start recipes when a sequence task is completed or when objects such as prospects are created or updated in Outreach.
- `docs/connectors/outsystems.md` — **OutSystems**: The OutSystems connector lets you create, update, search, and get records, including in batches, and trigger on new or updated records.
- `docs/connectors/outsystems/create-record-action.md` — **OutSystems - Create record action**: The Create record action in the OutSystems connector creates a single record in the module you select using the label you provide.
- `docs/connectors/outsystems/create-records-batch-action.md` — **OutSystems - Create record batch action**: The Create record batch action in the OutSystems connector creates multiple records at once in the module you select.
- `docs/connectors/outsystems/get-entity-by-id-action.md` — **OutSystems - Get entity by ID action**: The Get entity by ID action in the OutSystems connector fetches a record from a module using the entity ID and record ID you provide.
- `docs/connectors/outsystems/new-record-batch-trigger.md` — **OutSystems - New record batch trigger**: The New record batch trigger in the OutSystems connector runs when new records are detected in a module, returning them in configurable batches.
- `docs/connectors/outsystems/new-record-trigger.md` — **OutSystems - New record trigger**: The New record trigger in the OutSystems connector runs when a new record is detected in a module you select, checking every five minutes by default.
- `docs/connectors/outsystems/new-updated-record-batch-trigger.md` — **OutSystems - New or updated record batch trigger**: The New or updated record batch trigger in the OutSystems connector runs when records are created or updated in a module, returning them in batches.
- `docs/connectors/outsystems/new-updated-record-trigger.md` — **OutSystems - New or updated record trigger**: The New or updated record trigger in the OutSystems connector runs when a new record is detected or an existing record is updated in a module you select.
- `docs/connectors/outsystems/search-records-action.md` — **OutSystems - Search records action**: The Search records action in the OutSystems connector searches a module for records that match the values you configure and returns the matching results.
- `docs/connectors/outsystems/update-record-action.md` — **OutSystems - Update record action**: The Update record action in the OutSystems connector updates a single record you specify by its ID in the selected module.
- `docs/connectors/outsystems/update-records-batch-action.md` — **OutSystems - Update records batch action**: The Update records batch action in the OutSystems connector updates multiple records you specify by their IDs in the selected module.
- `docs/connectors/pagerduty.md` — **PagerDuty**: The PagerDuty connector lets you search, get, update, and annotate incidents, send events, and trigger recipes on new incidents and notifications.

- `docs/connectors/pagerduty/search-incident.md` — **PagerDuty - Search incident action**: The Search incident action in the PagerDuty connector lets you find existing incidents by date range, incident key, statuses, or urgencies.

- `docs/connectors/recipe-functions/triggers/new-function-call.md` — **Recipe function by Workato - New call for function trigger**: The New call for function trigger in the Recipe functions connector lets you build a reusable recipe that other recipes can call.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops.md` — **RecipeOps by Workato**: Use RecipeOps by Workato to monitor and manage active recipes, automating responses to stopped recipes and failed jobs.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/actions/get-account-details.md` — **RecipeOps - Get account details action**: The Get account details action in the RecipeOps connector retrieves Workato account info such as plan type and remaining transaction and recipe counts.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/actions/get-recipe-details.md` — **RecipeOps - Get recipe details action**: The Get recipe details action in the RecipeOps connector retrieves key information about a recipe, such as status, version number, and lifetime task count.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/actions/list-connections.md` — **RecipeOps - List connections action**: The List connections action in the RecipeOps connector retrieves connection details in the account, filtered by name, recipe, and application type.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/actions/list-recipes.md` — **RecipeOps - List recipes action**: The List recipes action in the RecipeOps connector retrieves a list of recipes with details such as ID, name, job count, and lifetime task count.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/actions/rerun-jobs.md` — **RecipeOps - Rerun jobs action**: The Rerun jobs action in the RecipeOps connector lets you re-run one or more specified recipe jobs by their job IDs.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/actions/search-job-history.md` — **RecipeOps - Search job history action**: The Search job history action in the RecipeOps connector lists the job history for a recipe, with filters for job status and start time.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/actions/search-recipes.md` — **RecipeOps - Search recipes action**: The Search recipes action in the RecipeOps connector retrieves recently updated recipes in the account, filtered by status and connection.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/actions/start-recipe.md` — **RecipeOps - Start recipe action**: The Start recipe action in the RecipeOps connector lets you start a single recipe specified by its ID or name.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/actions/stop-recipe.md` — **RecipeOps - Stop recipe action**: The Stop recipe action in the RecipeOps connector lets you stop a single recipe by its ID or name, and choose whether to force the stop.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/account-connected.md` — **RecipeOps - Account connected trigger**: The Account connected trigger in the RecipeOps connector monitors selected connections and fires when those connections connect successfully.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/account-credentials-refresh-failed.md` — **RecipeOps - Account credentials refresh failed trigger**: The Account credentials refresh failed trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when OAuth 2.0 or Multi-digest connections fail to refresh access credentials.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/account-disconnected.md` — **RecipeOps - Account disconnected trigger**: The Account disconnected trigger in the RecipeOps connector monitors selected connections and fires when those connections are disconnected.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/api-policy-violation.md` — **RecipeOps - API policy quota violation trigger**: The API policy quota violation trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when an access profile exceeds a policy quota threshold of 80, 90, or 100 percent.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/api-request-timeout.md` — **RecipeOps - API request timeout trigger**: The API request timeout trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when an API request to any workspace endpoint exceeds its configured timeout limit.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/concurrency-exceeded.md` — **RecipeOps - API concurrency threshold exceeded**: The API concurrency threshold exceeded trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when workspace API requests exceed 80% of the concurrency quota.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/customer-usage-threshold-reached.md` — **RecipeOps - Usage threshold reached trigger**: Use the Usage threshold reached trigger in Embedded partner workspaces to send alerts when a workspace reaches a task usage threshold according to its plan.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/deployment-approved.md` — **RecipeOps - Deployment approved trigger**: The Deployment approved trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when a project deployment is approved by reviewers in real time.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/deployment-complete.md` — **RecipeOps - Deployment complete trigger**: The Deployment complete trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when a project deployment is successfully pushed to assigned environments in real time.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/deployment-failed.md` — **RecipeOps - Deployment failed trigger**: The Deployment failed trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when a project deployment fails to push to assigned environments in real time.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/deployment-re-opened-for-review.md` — **RecipeOps - Deployment re-opened for review trigger**: The Deployment re-opened for review trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when a collaborator re-opens a project deployment for review in real time.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/deployment-rejected.md` — **RecipeOps - Deployment rejected trigger**: The Deployment rejected trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when a project deployment is rejected by reviewers in real time.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/member-invitation-accepted.md` — **RecipeOps - Member invitation accepted trigger**: The Member invitation accepted trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when a member invitation is accepted in the workspace.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/new-deployment-submitted-for-review.md` — **RecipeOps - New deployment submitted for review trigger**: The New deployment submitted for review trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when a new project deployment is submitted for review in real time.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/new-failed-job.md` — **RecipeOps - Job failed trigger**: The Job failed trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when a monitored recipe has a failed job, so you can automate corrective actions in real time.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/opa-disconnected.md` — **RecipeOps - On-prem agent disconnected trigger**: The On-prem agent disconnected trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when an agent in the selected group disconnects from Workato and fails to reconnect.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/package-deployed.md` — **RecipeOps - Package deployed trigger**: The Package deployed trigger in the RecipeOps connector monitors selected connections and fires when a package is deployed.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/rate-limit-violation.md` — **RecipeOps - API policy rate limit violation trigger**: The API policy rate limit violation trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when an access profile exceeds its assigned API rate limits.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/recipe-started.md` — **RecipeOps - Recipe started trigger**: The Recipe started trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when a user starts a monitored recipe, in real time.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/recipe-stopped-by-user.md` — **RecipeOps - Recipe stopped by user trigger**: The Recipe stopped by user trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when a user manually stops a monitored recipe, in real time.
- `docs/connectors/recipeops/triggers/recipe-stopped-by-workato.md` — **RecipeOps - Recipe stopped by Workato trigger**: The Recipe stopped by Workato trigger in the RecipeOps connector fires when Workato stops a monitored recipe due to non-recoverable errors, in real time.
- `docs/connectors/reckon.md` — **Reckon**: The Reckon connector enables you to automate workflows using your Reckon One account. Create and update records, retrieve accounting data, and trigger recipes on record changes.
- `docs/connectors/reckon/create-record-action.md` — **Reckon - Create record action**: The Create record action adds a new record to Reckon based on the selected object type.
- `docs/connectors/reckon/get-record-action.md` — **Reckon - Get record by ID action**: The Get record by ID action retrieves a specific record from Reckon using its unique ID.
- `docs/connectors/reckon/new-updated-record-trigger.md` — **Reckon - New/updated record trigger**: The New/updated record trigger initiates a recipe when a record is created or updated in Reckon.
- `docs/connectors/reckon/search-records-action.md` — **Reckon - Search records action**: The Search records action retrieves records from Reckon using filters and selected fields.
- `docs/connectors/reckon/update-record-action.md` — **Reckon - Update record action**: The Update record action updates an existing record in Reckon.
- `docs/connectors/redshift.md` — **Redshift**: The Redshift connector lets you run SQL on your data warehouse: select, insert, update, upsert, and delete rows, and trigger on new rows.
- `docs/connectors/redshift/custom-sql-action.md` — **Redshift - Run custom SQL action**: The Run custom SQL action in the Redshift connector executes a single DML or DDL SQL statement, returning up to 50,000 rows in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/redshift/delete.md` — **Redshift - Delete actions**: The Delete rows action in the Redshift connector lets you delete rows from a selected table based on a WHERE condition in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/redshift/export-query-result.md` — **Redshift - Export query result action**: The Export query result action in the Redshift connector runs a SQL query and exports the results as a CSV file in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/redshift/insert.md` — **Redshift - Insert actions**: Use the Redshift insert actions in Workato to add a single row or a batch of rows to a selected table for high-throughput data loads.
- `docs/connectors/redshift/long-query-custom-sql.md` — **Redshift - Run long query custom SQL action**: The Run long query custom SQL action in the Redshift connector runs DML and DDL queries asynchronously within a timeout you configure.
- `docs/connectors/redshift/new-row-trigger.md` — **Redshift - New row triggers**: The New row trigger in the Redshift connector picks up rows inserted in a selected table or view, processing each row as a separate job.
- `docs/connectors/redshift/select.md` — **Redshift - Select actions**: Use the Redshift select actions in Workato to retrieve rows from a table using a WHERE condition, order by, and limit options.
- `docs/connectors/redshift/update.md` — **Redshift - Update actions**: The Update rows action in the Redshift connector updates one or more rows with a single set of values using a WHERE condition in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/redshift/updated-row-trigger.md` — **Redshift - New/updated row triggers**: The New/updated row trigger in the Redshift connector picks up rows inserted or updated in a selected table or view, one job per row.
- `docs/connectors/redshift/upsert.md` — **Redshift - Upsert actions**: Use the Redshift upsert actions in Workato to insert or update a single row or a batch of rows in a table based on a unique key.
- `docs/connectors/replicon.md` — **Replicon**: The Replicon connector lets you automate time tracking: trigger on new projects, users, and clients, ready-to-sync invoices, and updated timesheets.
- `docs/connectors/replicon/new-client-trigger.md` — **Replicon - New client trigger**: The New client trigger in the Replicon connector picks up new clients created in your Replicon instance and processes each as an individual job.
- `docs/connectors/replicon/new-or-updated-invoice-ready-to-sync-trigger.md` — **Replicon - New/updated ready to sync invoice trigger**: The New/updated ready to sync invoice trigger in the Replicon connector picks up new or updated invoices in your Replicon instance, one job per invoice.
- `docs/connectors/replicon/new-project-trigger.md` — **Replicon - New project trigger**: The New project trigger in the Replicon connector picks up new projects created in your Replicon instance and processes each as an individual job.
- `docs/connectors/replicon/new-user-trigger.md` — **Replicon - New user trigger**: The New user trigger in the Replicon connector picks up new users created in your Replicon instance and processes each as an individual job.
- `docs/connectors/replicon/updated-timesheet-trigger.md` — **Replicon - Updated timesheet trigger**: The deprecated Updated timesheet trigger in the Replicon connector picks up timesheets updated in your Replicon instance, processing each as a job.
- `docs/connectors/richervalues.md` — **RicherValues**: The RicherValues connector lets you automate property valuation workflows by placing valuation and inspection orders for real estate and lending teams.
- `docs/connectors/richervalues/place-order-action.md` — **RicherValues - Place order action**: The Place order places a property evaluation, AVM, or inspection order in RicherValues.
- `docs/connectors/ringcentral.md` — **RingCentral**: Learn how to connect to RingCentral on Workato.
- `docs/connectors/ringcentral/actions/ring-out.md` — **RingCentral - Ring out action**: Use the Ring out action to make a two-legged RingOut call using RingCentral.
- `docs/connectors/ringcentral/actions/send-pager-message.md` — **RingCentral - Send pager message action**: Use the Send pager message action to send a pager message to specified extension numbers within RingCentral.
- `docs/connectors/ringcentral/actions/send-sms.md` — **RingCentral - Send SMS action**: Use the Send SMS action to send an SMS message to a specified phone number using RingCentral.
- `docs/connectors/ringcentral/triggers/call-ended.md` — **RingCentral - Call ended trigger**: Use the Call ended trigger to retrieve calls that have ended for the connected RingCentral extension.
- `docs/connectors/ringcentral/triggers/new-call-recording.md` — **RingCentral - New call recording trigger**: Use the New call recording trigger to retrieve calls with recordings made by the connected RingCentral extension.
- `docs/connectors/ringcentral/triggers/new-call.md` — **RingCentral - New call trigger**: Use the New call trigger to retrieve new calls made by the connected RingCentral extension.
- `docs/connectors/ringcentral/triggers/new-company-level-call.md` — **RingCentral - New company level call trigger**: Use the New company level call trigger to retrieve new company level calls made by the connected RingCentral account.
- `docs/connectors/ringcentral/triggers/new-event.md` — **RingCentral - New event trigger (real-time)**: Use the New event trigger to monitor new events in your RingCentral account in real-time.
- `docs/connectors/ringcentral/triggers/new-sms.md` — **RingCentral - New SMS trigger**: Use the New SMS trigger to monitor when your connected RingCentral extension receives new SMS messages.
- `docs/connectors/rpa-by-workato.md` — **RPA by Workato**: The RPA by Workato connector lets you start automation jobs, get job and process details, retrieve job logs, and list departments and processes.
- `docs/connectors/rpa-by-workato/get-job-detail.md` — **RPA by Workato - Get job detail action**: The Get job detail action retrieves information from an RPA job you specify.
- `docs/connectors/rpa-by-workato/get-job-logs-batch.md` — **RPA by Workato - Get job logs action (batch)**: The Get job logs batch action retrieves the logs of an RPA job you specify.
- `docs/connectors/rpa-by-workato/get-process-details.md` — **RPA by Workato - Get process details action**: The Get process details action retrieves information from a process you specify.
- `docs/connectors/rpa-by-workato/list-departments-batch.md` — **RPA by Workato - List departments action (batch)**: The List departments batch action retrieves a list of all departments in your RPA instance.
- `docs/connectors/rpa-by-workato/list-processes-by-department-batch.md` — **RPA by Workato - List processes by department action (batch)**: The List processes by department batch action retrieves all processes from a department and stage you specify.
- `docs/connectors/rpa-by-workato/start-job.md` — **RPA by Workato - Start job action**: The Start job action starts an RPA job in the process you specify.
- `docs/connectors/ruby-snippets-by-workato.md` — **Ruby snippets by Workato**: The Ruby snippets by Workato connector enables you to write custom Ruby code and run it in your recipes to perform actions such as invoking external services.
- `docs/connectors/s3-lake.md` — **S3 Data Lake**: S3 Data Lake is a centralized AWS service that stores and manages structured and unstructured data at scale using Amazon S3 and AWS Glue.
- `docs/connectors/s3-lake/create-table-action.md` — **S3 Data Lake - Create table action**: The Create table action in the S3 Data Lake connector lets you create a new Iceberg table and register its metadata in the AWS Glue data catalog.
- `docs/connectors/s3-lake/import-data-action.md` — **S3 Data Lake - Import data action**: The Import data action in the S3 Data Lake connector lets you load data from an S3 file into an Iceberg table using an AWS Glue batch job.
- `docs/connectors/s3-lake/list-tables-action.md` — **S3 Data Lake - List tables action**: The List tables action in the S3 Data Lake connector lets you retrieve all tables from a specified database in your AWS Glue data catalog.
- `docs/connectors/s3-lake/query-data-action.md` — **S3 Data Lake - Query data action**: The Query data action in the S3 Data Lake connector lets you run a SQL query on a Glue Iceberg table and write the results to S3 in JSON format.
- `docs/connectors/s3-lake/s3-custom-action.md` — **S3 Data Lake - Custom action**: The Custom action in the S3 Data Lake connector lets you make authorized HTTP requests to AWS Glue or S3 Data Lake APIs for advanced use cases.
- `docs/connectors/s3-lake/table-definition-action.md` — **S3 Data Lake - Get table definitions action**: The Get table definitions action in the S3 Data Lake connector lets you retrieve metadata for a table you specify in the AWS Glue data catalog.
- `docs/connectors/s3.md` — **Amazon S3**: Amazon S3 is a web service offered by Amazon Web Services that provides scalable and highly flexible cloud storage through web services interfaces.
- `docs/connectors/s3/action-copy-file.md` — **Amazon S3 - Copy file action**: Copy a file between Amazon S3 buckets with the Copy file action, optionally into a different Region and with a canned ACL.
- `docs/connectors/s3/action-create-bucket.md` — **Amazon S3 - Create bucket action**: The Create bucket action creates a new bucket in Amazon S3.
- `docs/connectors/s3/action-delete-bucket.md` — **Amazon S3 - Delete bucket action**: Permanently delete an empty Amazon S3 bucket with the Delete bucket action, optionally specifying the bucket's Region.
- `docs/connectors/s3/action-delete-file.md` — **Amazon S3 - Delete file action**: The Delete file action in the Amazon S3 connector lets you delete a file from a specified bucket and region by its object name.
- `docs/connectors/s3/action-download-file.md` — **Amazon S3 - Download file action**: The Download file action downloads contents of a file in Amazon S3.
- `docs/connectors/s3/action-list-files.md` — **Amazon S3 - List files in bucket action**: The List files in bucket action lists all files in a bucket in Amazon S3.
- `docs/connectors/s3/action-presign-url.md` — **Amazon S3 - Generate presigned URL action**: The Generate presigned URL action creates a temporary URL to a specific object in your Amazon S3 bucket. This URL can be passed to third-party users to upload, download, or modify an existing object.
- `docs/connectors/s3/action-rename-file.md` — **Amazon S3 - Rename file action**: The Rename file action in the Amazon S3 connector lets you rename an existing file in a bucket and returns the original and new file path and name.
- `docs/connectors/s3/action-upload-file-streaming.md` — **Amazon S3 - Upload file action (streaming)**: The Upload file action (streaming) uploads a file to a bucket or folder in Amazon S3.
- `docs/connectors/s3/action-upload-file.md` — **Amazon S3 - Upload file action (non-streaming)**: The Upload file action (non-streaming) uploads a file to a bucket or folder in Amazon S3.
- `docs/connectors/s3/trigger-csv-file.md` — **Amazon S3 - New CSV file trigger**: The New CSV file trigger in the Amazon S3 connector fires when a CSV file is added to a selected bucket or folder, returning file metadata and rows.
- `docs/connectors/s3/trigger-new-file-slice.md` — **Amazon S3 - New file slice trigger**: The New file slice trigger monitors when a new file is uploaded to a selected bucket or folder in Amazon S3. This trigger splits the uploaded file into slices of a specified size and creates a job for each individual slice.
- `docs/connectors/s3/trigger-new-file.md` — **Amazon S3 - New file trigger**: The New file trigger monitors when a new file is added in a selected bucket or folder in Amazon S3.
- `docs/connectors/sage-intacct.md` — **Sage Intacct (Custom)**: The Sage Intacct (Custom) connector lets you automate accounting workflows: create, update, search, and delete records like AP bills, AR payments, and orders.
- `docs/connectors/sage-intacct/actions.md` — **Sage Intacct (Custom) - Actions**: Explore actions in the Sage Intacct community connector for Workato, including creating AP bills, AR payments, contracts, invoices, journal entries, and more.
- `docs/connectors/sage-intacct/apply-ar-payment-action.md` — **Sage Intacct (Custom) - Apply AR payment action**: The Apply AR payment action applies the AR payment in Sage Intacct.
- `docs/connectors/sage-intacct/create-contract-mea-allocation-action.md` — **Sage Intacct (Custom) - Create contract MEA allocation action**: The Create contract MEA allocation action creates the contract MEA allocation in Sage Intacct.
- `docs/connectors/sage-intacct/delete-ap-bill-action.md` — **Sage Intacct (Custom) - Delete AP bill action**: The Delete AP bill action deletes the AP bill entry from Sage Intacct.
- `docs/connectors/sage-intacct/get-ap-bill-by-record-number-action.md` — **Sage Intacct (Custom) - Get AP bill by record number action**: The Get AP bill by record number action retrieves AP bill details based on the provided record number from Sage Intacct.
- `docs/connectors/sage-intacct/search-bank-feed-entries-action.md` — **Sage Intacct (Custom) - Search bank feed entries action**: The Search bank feed entries action retrieves the bank feed entries matching the search criteria from Sage Intacct.
- `docs/connectors/sage-intacct/triggers.md` — **Sage Intacct (Custom) - Triggers**: Explore triggers in the Sage Intacct community connector for Workato, including new and updated AP bills, contracts, customers, invoices, and vendors.
- `docs/connectors/sage-intacct/update-sales-purchase-order-custom-fields-action.md` — **Sage Intacct (Custom) - Update sales/purchase order custom fields action**: The Update sales/purchase order custom fields action updates the custom fields of a sales order/purchase order document in Sage Intacct.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce.md` — **Salesforce**: The Salesforce connector lets you automate CRM workflows: create, update, search, and delete records, manage files and approvals, and trigger on changes.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/approve-record.md` — **Salesforce - Approve record in approval process action**: This action approves a Salesforce record and triggers all steps in the approval process.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/create-record.md` — **Salesforce - Create record action**: This action creates a record in Salesforce.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/create-records-from-csv.md` — **Salesforce - Create records in bulk from CSV file action**: This action creates Salesforce records in bulk from a CSV file.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/create-records-in-batches.md` — **Salesforce - Create records in batches action (batch)**: This action creates Salesforce records in batches.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/delete-record.md` — **Salesforce - Delete record action**: This action deletes a Salesforce record by its ID.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/download-attachment.md` — **Salesforce - Download attachment action**: This action retrieves the contents of an attachment in Salesforce.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/download-file.md` — **Salesforce - Download file action**: This action retrieves the contents of a file in Salesforce.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/get-object-schema.md` — **Salesforce - Get object schema action**: This action retrieves the schema of a standard or custom object in Salesforce.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/get-record-details.md` — **Salesforce - Get record details by ID action**: This action retrieves the details of a Salesforce record by its ID.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/get-related-list.md` — **Salesforce - Get related list by parent record ID action (batch)**: This action retrieves a related list of a Salesforce record in batches using a parent record's ID.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/get-report.md` — **Salesforce - Get report by ID action**: This action retrieves report details from Salesforce.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/list-category-groups.md` — **Salesforce - List data category groups action (batch)**: This action retrieves all data category groups from Salesforce in batches.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/publish-platform-event.md` — **Salesforce - Publish platform event action**: This action publishes a platform event message in Salesforce.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/reject-record.md` — **Salesforce - Reject record in approval process action**: This action rejects a Salesforce record and triggers all steps in the rejection process.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/retrieve-category-group-hierarchy.md` — **Salesforce - Retrieve data category group hierarchy action (batch)**: This action retrieves a data category group hierarchy from Salesforce using a source list.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/retry-job-csv.md` — **Salesforce - Retry bulk job for failed records from CSV file action**: This action retries failed Salesforce jobs in bulk from a CSV file.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/search-records-soql-batch.md` — **Salesforce - Search records using SOQL query (batch)**: This action retrieves a list of Salesforce records that match the SOQL query you provide.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/search-records-soql-where.md` — **Salesforce - Search records using SOQL query WHERE clause action (batch)**: This action retrieves a CSV stream of Salesforce records that match the SOQL WHERE clause you provide.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/search-records-soql.md` — **Salesforce - Search records in bulk using SOQL query action**: This action retrieves a CSV stream of Salesforce records that match the SOQL query you provide.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/search-records.md` — **Salesforce - Search records action (batch)**: This action retrieves a list of Salesforce records that match the search criteria you provide.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/submit-record.md` — **Salesforce - Submit record for approval action**: This action submits a record for approval in Salesforce.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/update-record.md` — **Salesforce - Update record action**: This action updates a record in Salesforce by its ID.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/update-records-csv.md` — **Salesforce - Update records in bulk from CSV file action**: This action updates Salesforce records in bulk from a CSV file using the Salesforce bulk API.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/update-records-in-batches.md` — **Salesforce - Update records in batches action (batch)**: This action updates Salesforce records in batches.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/upload-file.md` — **Salesforce - Upload file action**: This action uploads a file to Salesforce.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/upsert-record.md` — **Salesforce - Upsert record action**: This action upserts a record in Salesforce.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/upsert-records-batch.md` — **Salesforce - Upsert records in batches action (batch)**: This action upserts Salesforce records in batches.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/actions/upsert-records-in-bulk-from-csv.md` — **Salesforce - Upsert records in bulk from CSV file action**: This action upserts Salesforce records in bulk from a CSV file and a primary key.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/approval-processes.md` — **Salesforce approval process**: Learn how the Salesforce connector for Workato supports approval processes, including approving, rejecting, and submitting records for approval.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/batch-operations.md` — **Salesforce batch operations**: Learn how Salesforce batch operations in Workato process up to 2,000 records at a time using the sObject Collections API for smaller batches.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/change-data-capture-trigger.md` — **Salesforce - Monitor changes in a record trigger (real-time)**: This trigger monitors Salesforce Change Data Capture events in real-time for changes to object fields you specify.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/custom-oauth.md` — **Create a custom OAuth profile for Salesforce**: Learn how to create a custom OAuth profile and external client app for Salesforce.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/database-syncs.md` — **Sync Salesforce with databases/data warehouses**: Learn how to sync Salesforce to databases and data warehouses using Workato, with the Get object schema action and detection of new or updated custom data.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/real-time-triggers.md` — **Salesforce Real-time triggers**: Explore real-time triggers in the Salesforce connector for Workato, which use Salesforce flows to notify recipes immediately when records change.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/related-objects.md` — **Salesforce object relationships**: Learn how Salesforce object relationships work in Workato and how to use the Related objects and Fields inputs to retrieve data from related objects.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/salesforce-bulk.md` — **Salesforce bulk operations**: Learn how the Salesforce connector for Workato supports bulk operations to load large datasets from CSV files efficiently using the Bulk API.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/salesforce-connector-faqs.md` — **Salesforce connector FAQs**: Answers to frequently asked questions about the Salesforce connector in Workato, covering OAuth 2.0 and JWT authentication and connection setup.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/salesforce-generic.md` — **Salesforce actions**: Explore actions in the Salesforce connector for Workato, including uploading files and creating, updating, upserting, and deleting records.

- `docs/connectors/salesforce/triggers/new-message.md` — **Salesforce - New Outbound message trigger (real-time)**: This trigger monitors Salesforce in real-time for new Outbound messages.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/triggers/new-platform-event.md` — **Salesforce - New platform event trigger (real-time)**: This trigger monitors Salesforce in real-time for new platform events.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/triggers/new-record-real-time.md` — **Salesforce - New record trigger (real-time)**: This trigger monitors Salesforce in real-time for newly created objects.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/triggers/new-record.md` — **Salesforce - New record trigger**: This trigger checks Salesforce for newly created objects every five minutes.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/triggers/new-records-batch.md` — **Salesforce - New records trigger (batch)**: This trigger monitors Salesforce for newly created records and retrieves them in batches.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/triggers/new-updated-record-real-time.md` — **Salesforce - New/updated record trigger (real-time)**: This trigger monitors Salesforce in real-time for newly created or updated objects.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/triggers/new-updated-record.md` — **Salesforce - New/updated record trigger**: This trigger checks Salesforce for newly created or updated objects at an interval you specify.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/triggers/new-updated-records-batch.md` — **Salesforce - New/updated records trigger (batch)**: This trigger monitors Salesforce for newly created or updated objects and retrieves them in batches.
- `docs/connectors/salesforce/triggers/scheduled-search-soql-where.md` — **Salesforce - Scheduled record search using SOQL query WHERE clause trigger (batch)**: This trigger searches Salesforce using a SOQL query WHERE clause on a schedule you specify.

- `docs/connectors/salesforce/triggers/threshold-met-batch.md` — **Salesforce - Threshold met for new records created trigger (batch)**: This trigger monitors Salesforce for when the number of newly created objects reaches a threshold you specify.

- `docs/connectors/sftp/updated-file-trigger.md` — **SFTP - New/updated file in directory trigger**: The New/updated file in directory trigger in the SFTP connector fires when a file is created or updated in the specified first-level directory.

- `docs/connectors/sharepoint/move-file-in-library-action.md` — **SharePoint - Move file in SharePoint library action**: The move file action in the SharePoint connector lets you move a file to a specific folder in a library.
- `docs/connectors/sharepoint/new-row-in-list-trigger.md` — **SharePoint - New row in SharePoint list trigger**: The new row trigger in the SharePoint connector picks up new rows in a list you specify on each poll interval and processes each as a job.

Use this trigger when monitoring a folder within a large SharePoint site containing more than 5,000 files.

- `docs/connectors/smb.md` — **SMB**: The SMB connector enables you to automate file-sharing workflows, uploading, downloading, and managing files and folders on SMB servers from Workato.
- `docs/connectors/smb/create-folder.md` — **SMB - Create folder action**: The Create folder action in the SMB connector lets you create a new folder in your network location, with an option to overwrite existing ones.
- `docs/connectors/smb/delete-action.md` — **SMB - Delete resource action**: The Delete resource action in the SMB connector lets you delete a file or folder you specify in your network location, including files in subfolders.
- `docs/connectors/smb/download-file-action.md` — **SMB - Download file action**: The Download file action in the SMB connector lets you download a file from your network share in binary mode using file streaming for use downstream.
- `docs/connectors/smb/list-action.md` — **SMB - List folder action**: The List folder action in the SMB connector lets you list all files and subfolders in a specified folder of your share. Subfolder items are excluded.
- `docs/connectors/smb/metadata-action.md` — **SMB - Get metadata of resource**: The Get metadata of resource action in the SMB connector lets you retrieve basic details about a file or folder, such as its name, path, and size.
- `docs/connectors/smb/new-file-trigger.md` — **SMB - New/updated file in directory trigger**: The New/updated file in directory trigger in the SMB connector fires when a file is created or updated in the first level of a specified directory.
- `docs/connectors/smb/upload-file-action.md` — **SMB - Upload file action**: The Upload file action in the SMB connector lets you upload a file to a specific path in your network share, overwriting any file with the same name.
- `docs/connectors/sms-by-workato.md` — **SMS by Workato**: SMS by Workato is a utility connector that sends low-volume SMS messages for recipe alerts, debugging, and testing before moving to a production provider.
- `docs/connectors/snowflake.md` — **Snowflake**: Connect to Snowflake to build powerful data workflows.
- `docs/connectors/snowflake/bulk-load-to-table-from-stage.md` — **Snowflake - Bulk load to table from stage action**: The Bulk load to table from stage action in the Snowflake connector lets you load a staged file into a target table using the COPY command.

- `docs/connectors/snowflake/upsert.md` — **Snowflake - Upsert action**: The Upsert action in the Snowflake connector lets you insert or update single rows or batches of rows in a table using primary key values.

- `docs/connectors/soap/execute-operation-action.md` — **SOAP - Execute operation action**: The Execute operation action runs any web service operation defined in the SOAP WSDL document.
