- `docs/connectors/sqs/new-message-trigger.md` — **Amazon SQS - New message trigger**: The Amazon SQS connector supports the New message and New message (Batch) triggers.
- `docs/connectors/sqs/receive-messages-action.md` — **Amazon SQS - Receive messages action**: The Receive messages action pulls up to 10 messages from a chosen SQS queue.
- `docs/connectors/sqs/send-message-action.md` — **Amazon SQS - Send message action**: The Send Message action allows you to send a single message to a chosen SQS queue.
- `docs/connectors/sqs/send-message-batch-action.md` — **Amazon SQS – Send messages (batch)**: Send multiple messages to an Amazon SQS queue.
- `docs/connectors/sqs/troubleshoot-runtime.md` — **Troubleshoot Amazon SQS runtime errors**: Use this guide to troubleshoot errors that occur at runtime when you use the Amazon SQS connector in Workato.
- `docs/connectors/stacklet.md` — **Stacklet**: The Stacklet connector enables you to automate governance workflows, integrate compliance monitoring with business processes, and streamline cloud resource management through GraphQL-based operations.
- `docs/connectors/stacklet/mutate-records-action.md` — **Stacklet - Mutate records action**: The Mutate records action creates, updates, or deletes one or more records in Stacklet.
- `docs/connectors/stacklet/query-records-action.md` — **Stacklet - Query records action**: The Query records action queries your records in Stacklet.
- `docs/connectors/stripe.md` — **Stripe**: Connect Stripe to Workato to automate payment processing, payouts, and financial workflows.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/create-charge-action.md` — **Stripe - Create charge action**: The Create charge action in the Stripe connector lets you create a charge for a specified amount, currency, account, and card in Stripe.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/create-customer-action.md` — **Stripe - Create customer action**: The Create customer action in the Stripe connector lets you create a new customer record, including its description, email, and card details.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/create-invoice-action.md` — **Stripe - Create invoice item action**: The Create invoice item action in the Stripe connector lets you create a new invoice item for a specified customer in Stripe.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/get-customer-action.md` — **Stripe - Get customer by ID action**: The Get customer by ID action in the Stripe connector lets you retrieve transactions for a specific customer using their customer ID.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/get-object-by-id-action.md` — **Stripe - Get object by ID action**: The Get object by ID action in the Stripe connector lets you retrieve the details of a specified object type using its object ID in Stripe.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/list-objects-action.md` — **Stripe - List objects (batch) action**: The List objects batch action in the Stripe connector lets you retrieve a list of objects in Stripe, with search criteria to refine the results.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/migration.md` — **Migrate your connection to Stripe Apps**: Migrate your Stripe connection in Workato from the sunset Stripe extension to Stripe Apps, including testing and replacing connections in recipes.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/new-charge-trigger.md` — **Stripe - New charge real-time trigger**: The New charge real-time trigger in the Stripe connector fires in real time when a new charge is created in Stripe.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/new-object-event-trigger.md` — **Stripe - New object event trigger (real-time)**: The New object event trigger in the Stripe connector fires in real time when new events related to a selected object type occur in Stripe.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/new-object-trigger.md` — **Stripe - New object trigger**: The New object trigger in the Stripe connector periodically checks for new objects, such as charges, customers, invoices, and payouts, in Stripe.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/new-objects-batch-trigger.md` — **Stripe - New objects trigger (batch)**: The New objects batch trigger in the Stripe connector periodically checks for new objects in Stripe and retrieves them in configurable batches.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/retrieve-invoice-action.md` — **Stripe - Retrieve invoice by ID action**: The Retrieve invoice by ID action in the Stripe connector lets you retrieve the details of a specific invoice using its invoice ID.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/search-charges-action.md` — **Stripe - Search charges action**: The Search charges action in the Stripe connector lets you search for charges associated with a given customer ID in Stripe.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/search-invoices-action.md` — **Stripe - Search invoice items action**: The Search invoice items action in the Stripe connector lets you search for invoice items associated with a given customer ID in Stripe.
- `docs/connectors/stripe/update-customer-action.md` — **Stripe - Update customer action**: The Update customer action in the Stripe connector lets you update an existing customer record, including its description, email, and card details.
- `docs/connectors/successfactors/successfactors-advanced-search-action.md` — **SAP SuccessFactors - Search records using OData query action**: Use the Search records using OData query action to retrieve records that match a specific OData query in SAP SuccessFactors.
- `docs/connectors/successfactors/successfactors-create-action.md` — **SAP SuccessFactors - Create record action**: The Create record action in the SAP SuccessFactors connector creates a record for the selected object using the values you provide.
- `docs/connectors/successfactors/successfactors-get-action.md` — **SAP SuccessFactors - Get record details action**: Use the Get record details action to retrieve a specific record by searching with its key columns in SAP SuccessFactors.
- `docs/connectors/successfactors/successfactors-merge-action.md` — **SAP SuccessFactors - Merge record action**: The Merge record action in the SAP SuccessFactors connector updates specific properties in a record, leaving fields unchanged when no new value is given.
- `docs/connectors/successfactors/successfactors-nav-fields.md` — **SAP SuccessFactors - Use navigation fields**: Use navigation fields in the SAP SuccessFactors connector to retrieve related records or associate records when you create or update them.
- `docs/connectors/successfactors/successfactors-search-action.md` — **SAP SuccessFactors - Search records action**: Use the Search records action to retrieve a list of records that match your search criteria for the selected object in SAP SuccessFactors.
- `docs/connectors/successfactors/successfactors-triggers.md` — **SAP SuccessFactors - Triggers**: Explore SAP SuccessFactors connector triggers in Workato, including the New or updated object polling trigger that runs when an object is created or updated.
- `docs/connectors/successfactors/successfactors-update-action.md` — **SAP SuccessFactors - Update record action**: The Update record action in the SAP SuccessFactors connector updates a record with specified values, reverting omitted fields to their default values.
- `docs/connectors/successfactors/successfactors-upsert-action.md` — **SAP SuccessFactors - Upsert record action**: The Upsert record action in the SAP SuccessFactors connector finds a record by object ID, then updates it or creates a new record if none exists.
- `docs/connectors/successfactors/successfactors-upsert-multiple.md` — **SAP SuccessFactors - Upsert records (multiple objects) batch action**: The Upsert records (multiple objects) batch action performs simultaneous operations on multiple SuccessFactors objects with transactional behavior.
- `docs/connectors/successfactors/successfactors-upsert-single.md` — **SAP SuccessFactors - Upsert records (single object) batch action**: The Upsert records (single object) batch action searches for multiple records by a specified field and updates or creates them in bulk.
- `docs/connectors/successfactors/successfactors.md` — **SAP SuccessFactors**: Set up and connect SAP SuccessFactors in Workato, including creating an integration system user and registering an OAuth 2.0 client application.
- `docs/connectors/supabase.md` — **Supabase**: Connect Supabase to Workato to query and update table rows, call database functions, manage storage objects, and trigger recipes on row changes.
- `docs/connectors/supabase/call-database-function-action.md` — **Supabase - Call database function action**: Call a Postgres function through Supabase's PostgREST RPC endpoint, with arguments and response fields sourced from the function's API schema.
- `docs/connectors/supabase/delete-rows-action.md` — **Supabase - Delete rows action**: Delete rows from a Supabase table that match one or more required filters, with the deleted rows returned in the output.
- `docs/connectors/supabase/download-object-action.md` — **Supabase - Download object action**: Download a file from a Supabase storage bucket, as a public or authenticated download.
- `docs/connectors/supabase/insert-or-upsert-rows-action.md` — **Supabase - Insert or upsert rows action**: Insert one or more rows into a Supabase table, with an optional upsert when a unique key already exists.
- `docs/connectors/supabase/new-updated-or-deleted-row-trigger.md` — **Supabase - New, updated, or deleted row trigger**: Invoke a recipe when Supabase sends a database webhook for an INSERT, UPDATE, or DELETE event on a table.
- `docs/connectors/supabase/query-rows-action.md` — **Supabase - Query rows action**: Query rows from a Supabase table with filters, sorting, pagination, and an optional total row count.
- `docs/connectors/supabase/search-objects-action.md` — **Supabase - Search objects action**: Search files in a Supabase storage bucket by folder prefix, name filter, and sort order.
- `docs/connectors/supabase/update-rows-action.md` — **Supabase - Update rows action**: Update rows in a Supabase table that match one or more required filters, with the updated rows returned in the output.
- `docs/connectors/surveymonkey.md` — **SurveyMonkey**: The SurveyMonkey connector lets you automate survey workflows: send survey invites and trigger recipes on completed survey responses.
- `docs/connectors/syncro.md` — **Syncro**: The Syncro connector lets you automate business workflows by integrating with your Syncro account. You can create connections, manage data, and run operations using your API key and subdomain.
- `docs/connectors/syncro/create-record-action.md` — **Syncro - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a new record in Syncro based on the selected object type.
- `docs/connectors/syncro/delete-record-action.md` — **Syncro - Delete record action**: The Delete record action retrieves a specific record from Syncro using the selected object and ID.
- `docs/connectors/syncro/get-record-action.md` — **Syncro - Get record by ID action**: The Get record by ID action retrieves a specific record from Syncro using the selected object and ID.
- `docs/connectors/syncro/search-record-action.md` — **Syncro - Search records action**: The Search records action retrieves a list of records from Syncro based on the selected object.
- `docs/connectors/syncro/update-record-action.md` — **Syncro - Update record action**: The Update record action updates an existing record in Syncro based on the selected object type and record ID.
- `docs/connectors/syslog.md` — **Syslog**: The Syslog connector enables you to automate logging workflows, forwarding messages from network devices to a central syslog server from Workato.
- `docs/connectors/tableau.md` — **Tableau**: The Tableau connector lets you automate analytics workflows: refresh workbooks, run extract refresh tasks, download views, and search views and workbooks.
- `docs/connectors/tableau/download-view-action.md` — **Tableau - Download view action**: The Download view action returns an image or PDF of the specified view.
- `docs/connectors/tableau/list-extract-refresh-tasks-action.md` — **Tableau - List extract refresh tasks action**: The List extract refresh tasks action returns a list of extract refresh tasks from the Tableau site.
- `docs/connectors/tableau/refresh-workbook-action.md` — **Tableau - Refresh workbook action**: The Refresh workbook action refreshes a workbook you specify.
- `docs/connectors/tableau/run-extract-refresh-task-action.md` — **Tableau - Run extract refresh task action**: The Run extract refresh task action runs an extract refresh task in Tableau.
- `docs/connectors/tableau/search-view-action.md` — **Tableau - Search views action**: The Search views action returns all site views that match the search criteria you provide.
- `docs/connectors/tableau/search-workbook-action.md` — **Tableau - Search workbooks action**: The Search workbooks action retrieves all workbooks that match your search criteria.
- `docs/connectors/talent-palette.md` — **Talent Palette**: The Talent Palette connector lets you automate talent management workflows: import members and organizations and check import job status.
- `docs/connectors/talent-palette/check-job-status-action.md` — **Talent Palette - Check job status action**: The Check job status action checks the status of bulk import jobs in Talent Palette.
- `docs/connectors/talent-palette/import-members-action.md` — **Talent Palette - Import members action**: The import members action imports members in bulk in Talent Palette.
- `docs/connectors/talent-palette/import-organizations-action.md` — **Talent Palette - Import organizations action**: The import organizations action imports organizations in bulk in Talent Palette.
- `docs/connectors/tango_card.md` — **Tango Card: Rewards Made Easy**: The Tango Card connector lets you automate rewards workflows: create and resend gift card orders, search processed orders, and get account details.
- `docs/connectors/tavily.md` — **Tavily**: Tavily is a search engine built for LLMs and AI agents, offering real-time web search, content extraction, and deep research through a REST API.
- `docs/connectors/tavily/crawl-website-action.md` — **Tavily - Crawl website action**: The Crawl website action traverses a website starting from a root URL and returns extracted content for each page.
- `docs/connectors/tavily/extract-page-content-action.md` — **Tavily - Extract page content action**: The Extract page content action retrieves the content of one or more URLs and returns it as Markdown or plain text.
- `docs/connectors/tavily/get-research-result-action.md` — **Tavily - Get research result action**: The Get research result action retrieves the status and result of a research task by its request ID.
- `docs/connectors/tavily/map-website-action.md` — **Tavily - Map website action**: The Map website action traverses a website starting from a root URL and returns a list of discovered URLs.
- `docs/connectors/tavily/run-deep-research-action.md` — **Tavily - Run deep research action**: The Run deep research action submits a research question to Tavily and returns a completed report with sources.
- `docs/connectors/tavily/search-web-action.md` — **Tavily - Search web action**: The Search web action executes a web search using Tavily and returns ranked results, an optional AI-generated answer, and optional images.
- `docs/connectors/teams/create-channel-action.md` — **Microsoft Teams - Create channel action**: The Create channel action creates a channel in a Microsoft Teams team.
- `docs/connectors/teams/get-channel-action.md` — **Microsoft Teams - Get channel action**: The Get channel action gets details of a specified channel in Microsoft Teams.
- `docs/connectors/teams/get-chat-action.md` — **Microsoft Teams - Get chat action**: The Get chat action gets details of a specified chat in Microsoft Teams.
- `docs/connectors/teams/get-message-action.md` — **Microsoft Teams - Get message action**: The Get message action gets details of a specified message in Microsoft Teams.
- `docs/connectors/teams/get-messages-batch-action.md` — **Microsoft Teams - Get messages batch action**: The Get messages batch action gets messages from a channel, thread, or chat in Microsoft Teams.
- `docs/connectors/teams/get-team-action.md` — **Microsoft Teams - Get team action**: The Get team action gets details of a specified team in Microsoft Teams.
- `docs/connectors/teams/get-user-action.md` — **Microsoft Teams - Get user action**: The Get user action gets user details by ID in Microsoft Teams.
- `docs/connectors/teams/list-channels-batch-action.md` — **Microsoft Teams - List channels (batch) action**: The List channels batch action lists channels in the specified team that the authenticated user can access in Microsoft Teams.
- `docs/connectors/teams/list-chats-batch-action.md` — **Microsoft Teams - List chats (batch) action**: The List chats batch action lists chats the authenticated user participates in.
- `docs/connectors/teams/list-teams-batch-action.md` — **Microsoft Teams - List teams (batch) action**: The List teams batch action lists teams the authenticated user is a member of.
- `docs/connectors/teams/reply-to-message-action.md` — **Microsoft Teams - Reply to message action**: The Reply to message action replies to a message in Microsoft Teams.
- `docs/connectors/teams/search-messages-batch-action.md` — **Microsoft Teams - Search messages batch action**: The Search messages batch action searches for messages in Microsoft Teams.
- `docs/connectors/teams/send-message-action.md` — **Microsoft Teams - Send message action**: The Send message action sends a message in Microsoft Teams.
- `docs/connectors/telegram.md` — **Telegram**: Connect Telegram to Workato to send messages and media, manage chats, and automate bot interactions with Telegram's Bot API.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/answer-callback-query-action.md` — **Telegram - Answer callback query action**: Show a notification, alert, or URL to a Telegram user after they tap an inline keyboard button on your bot's message.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/delete-message-action.md` — **Telegram - Delete message action**: Remove a message your Telegram bot sent from a chat, for example to retract an outdated notification.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/download-file-action.md` — **Telegram - Download file action**: Fetch a file's binary content from Telegram's servers using the file path from the Get file action.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/get-chat-member-action.md` — **Telegram - Get chat member action**: Check a Telegram chat member's role and permissions, such as admin rights, before granting bot access to a feature.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/get-file-action.md` — **Telegram - Get file action**: Get a Telegram file's size and server path before fetching its contents with the Download file action.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/get-sticker-set-action.md` — **Telegram - Get sticker set action**: Look up a Telegram sticker set's stickers, type, and thumbnail image by its unique name.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/new-event-trigger.md` — **Telegram - New event trigger**: Fire a recipe on a fixed set of Telegram events, such as new messages or channel posts. Deprecated in favor of New events.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/new-events-trigger.md` — **Telegram - New events trigger**: Fire a recipe on the Telegram event types you choose, such as new messages, channel posts, or chat membership changes.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/send-animation-action.md` — **Telegram - Send animation action**: Post a GIF or muted video animation to a Telegram chat, provided as a file upload, file ID, or URL.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/send-chat-action.md` — **Telegram - Send chat action**: Show a status indicator, such as typing or uploading a photo, in a Telegram chat so users know your bot is active.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/send-document-action.md` — **Telegram - Send document action**: Send a file, such as a PDF, to a Telegram chat, provided as a file upload, file ID, or URL.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/send-location-action.md` — **Telegram - Send location action**: Share a set of coordinates, optionally as a live-updating location, in a Telegram chat.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/send-message-action.md` — **Telegram - Send message action**: Post a plain-text or formatted message from your Telegram bot to a chat or channel.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/send-message-with-buttons-or-voice-action.md` — **Telegram - Send message with buttons or voice action**: Send an interactive message with tappable inline buttons, or a voice note, from your Telegram bot to a chat.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/send-photo-action.md` — **Telegram - Send photo action**: Post a photo, optionally with a spoiler cover or caption, to a Telegram chat.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/send-sticker-action.md` — **Telegram - Send sticker action**: Send a static, animated, or video sticker from your Telegram bot to a chat.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/send-venue-action.md` — **Telegram - Send venue action**: Share a named venue's address and location, optionally with a Foursquare or Google Places ID, in a Telegram chat.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/update-message-action.md` — **Telegram - Update message action**: Edit the text of a message your Telegram bot already sent, for example to reflect a status change.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/update-message-caption-action.md` — **Telegram - Update message caption action**: Edit the caption on a photo, video, or other media message your Telegram bot already sent.
- `docs/connectors/telegram/update-message-media-action.md` — **Telegram - Update message media action**: Swap the photo, video, or file attached to a message your Telegram bot already sent for a new one.
- `docs/connectors/textract.md` — **Amazon Textract**: The Amazon Textract connector lets you extract structured data from documents using AWS's OCR and document analysis capabilities.
- `docs/connectors/textract/analyze-document-action.md` — **Amazon Textract – Analyze document action**: The Analyze document action extracts data from documents stored in S3 using advanced analysis features.
- `docs/connectors/textract/get-document-action.md` — **Amazon Textract – Get document analysis action**: The Get document analysis action retrieves the results of a previously started document analysis job in Amazon Textract.
- `docs/connectors/textract/get-lending-action.md` — **Amazon Textract – Get lending analysis action**: The Get lending analysis action retrieves the results of a lending document analysis job previously started in Amazon Textract.
- `docs/connectors/textract/start-document-action.md` — **Amazon Textract – Start document analysis action**: The Start document analysis action initiates an asynchronous job to analyze a document stored in an S3 bucket using Amazon Textract.
- `docs/connectors/textract/start-lending-action.md` — **Amazon Textract - Start lending analysis action**: The Start lending analysis action starts the classification and extraction of data from lending documents stored in an Amazon S3 bucket.
- `docs/connectors/tiktok-shop.md` — **TikTok Shop**: Connect TikTok Shop to Workato to automate e-commerce workflows, monitoring and retrieving order information from the in-app selling platform.
- `docs/connectors/tiktok-shop/get-record-details-by-id-action.md` — **TikTok Shop - Get record details by ID action**: The Get record details by ID action retrieves details about a specific record in TikTok Shop.
- `docs/connectors/tiktok-shop/new-record-trigger.md` — **TikTok Shop - New record trigger**: The New record trigger monitors TikTok Shop for newly created records, for example, newly created orders.
- `docs/connectors/tiktok-shop/new-updated-record-trigger.md` — **TikTok Shop - New/updated record trigger**: The New/Updated Record trigger monitors TikTok Shop for newly created or updated records, for example, newly created orders.
- `docs/connectors/tiktok-shop/search-records-action.md` — **TikTok Shop - Search records action**: The Search records action retrieves a list of TikTok Shop records that match your search criteria.
- `docs/connectors/tiktok.md` — **TikTok**: Connect TikTok to Workato to read creator profiles, list videos, and publish videos or photo carousels through TikTok's Content Posting API.
- `docs/connectors/tiktok/check-post-status-action.md` — **TikTok - Check post status action**: Check the publish status of a TikTok post by publish ID, with optional polling until the post completes or fails.
- `docs/connectors/tiktok/get-record-action.md` — **TikTok - Get record action**: Retrieve the connected TikTok user's profile, or the creator posting limits and privacy options for publishing.
- `docs/connectors/tiktok/list-records-action.md` — **TikTok - List records action**: List a TikTok user's public videos with paging, or retrieve specific videos by video ID.
- `docs/connectors/tiktok/publish-photo-carousel-action.md` — **TikTok - Publish photo carousel action**: Publish a photo carousel to TikTok from public image URLs, as a direct post or a draft in the creator inbox.
- `docs/connectors/tiktok/publish-video-action.md` — **TikTok - Publish video action**: Publish a video to TikTok from a public URL or a file upload, as a direct post or a draft in the creator inbox.
- `docs/connectors/trackvia.md` — **TrackVia**: The TrackVia connector lets you automate data workflows: create, update, and delete records, manage files and users, and trigger on record changes.
- `docs/connectors/trackvia/create-record-action.md` — **TrackVia - Create record action**: The Create record action in the TrackVia connector creates a new record in a selected TrackVia application and view.
- `docs/connectors/trackvia/create-user-action.md` — **TrackVia - Create user action**: The Create user action in the TrackVia connector creates a new user from an email, first name, and last name you provide.
- `docs/connectors/trackvia/delete-all-records-in-view-action.md` — **TrackVia - Delete all records in view action**: The Delete all records in view action in the TrackVia connector deletes every record in a TrackVia view you specify.
- `docs/connectors/trackvia/delete-record-action.md` — **TrackVia - Delete record action**: The Delete record action in the TrackVia connector deletes a record you specify by its ID from a selected application and view.
- `docs/connectors/trackvia/deleted-record-trigger.md` — **TrackVia - Deleted record trigger**: The Deleted record trigger in the TrackVia connector fires in real time when records are deleted from a selected TrackVia application and view.
- `docs/connectors/trackvia/get-all-view-records-action.md` — **TrackVia - Get all view records action (Batch)**: The Get all view records action in the TrackVia connector retrieves records matching your search criteria from a view and processes them in batches.
- `docs/connectors/trackvia/get-file-from-record-action.md` — **TrackVia - Get file from record action (File)**: The Get file from record action in the TrackVia connector retrieves a file from a specified record, returning the file name and content.
- `docs/connectors/trackvia/get-record-action.md` — **TrackVia - Get record action**: The Get record action in the TrackVia connector retrieves a single record by its ID from a selected application and view.
- `docs/connectors/trackvia/new-record-trigger.md` — **TrackVia - New record trigger**: The New record trigger in the TrackVia connector fires in real time when new records are created in a selected TrackVia application and view.
- `docs/connectors/trackvia/update-record-action.md` — **TrackVia - Update record action**: The Update record action in the TrackVia connector updates a record you specify by its ID in a selected application and view.
- `docs/connectors/trackvia/updated-record-trigger.md` — **TrackVia - Updated record trigger**: The Updated record trigger in the TrackVia connector fires in real time when records are updated in a selected TrackVia application and view.
- `docs/connectors/trackvia/upload-file-to-a-record-action.md` — **TrackVia - Upload file to a record action (File)**: The Upload file to a record action in the TrackVia connector uploads a file to a specified field in a record you select.
- `docs/connectors/tradacoms-edi.md` — **TRADACOMS EDI**: Parse and generate TRADACOMS EDI documents for UK retail electronic data interchange, with no authentication required.
- `docs/connectors/tradacoms-edi/generate-tradacoms-document-action.md` — **TRADACOMS EDI - Generate TRADACOMS document (generic) action**: Build a TRADACOMS document from structured messages, segments, and components, with message numbers and counts calculated automatically.
- `docs/connectors/tradacoms-edi/generate-tradacoms-file-action.md` — **TRADACOMS EDI - Generate TRADACOMS file (typed) action**: Generate a TRADACOMS transmission file from typed header, detail, VAT, and trailer fields for a selected file type.
- `docs/connectors/tradacoms-edi/look-up-tradacoms-code-value-action.md` — **TRADACOMS EDI - Look up TRADACOMS code value action**: Translate a standard TRADACOMS ANA code, such as a transaction code or VAT category, into its meaning.
- `docs/connectors/tradacoms-edi/parse-tradacoms-document-action.md` — **TRADACOMS EDI - Parse TRADACOMS document (generic) action**: Parse any TRADACOMS document into its interchange structure of segments, messages, and an integrity report.
- `docs/connectors/tradacoms-edi/parse-tradacoms-file-action.md` — **TRADACOMS EDI - Parse TRADACOMS file (typed) action**: Parse a TRADACOMS transmission file into typed documents with named fields for the selected file type.
- `docs/connectors/trello.md` — **Trello**: The Trello connector lets you automate project workflows: create boards, lists, and cards, move and comment on cards, and trigger on new cards.
- `docs/connectors/trello/add-color-to-card-action.md` — **Trello - Add color to card action**: The Add color to card action in the Trello connector lets you add a named color label to a card you specify.
- `docs/connectors/trello/add-comment-to-card-action.md` — **Trello - Add comment to card action**: The Add comment to card action in the Trello connector lets you add a comment to a card you specify.
- `docs/connectors/trello/create-board-action.md` — **Trello - Create board action**: The Create board action in the Trello connector lets you create a new board, set its permissions, and optionally copy from an existing board.
- `docs/connectors/trello/create-card-action.md` — **Trello - Create card action**: The Create card action in the Trello connector lets you create a new card in a list you specify, with a name, description, and due date.
- `docs/connectors/trello/create-list-action.md` — **Trello - Create list action**: The Create list action in the Trello connector lets you create a new list at a position you set on a board you specify.

- `docs/connectors/trello/search-boards-action.md` — **Trello - Search boards action (batch)**: The Search boards action in the Trello connector lets you retrieve information about a board you specify by name.

- `docs/connectors/ukg-pro/create-record-action.md` — **UKG Pro - Create record action**: The Create record action in the UKG Pro connector lets you create a new record, such as a pending hire, in a UKG Pro instance.

- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault.md` — **Veeva Vault**: Connect Veeva Vault to Workato to retrieve, create, and update documents and data records across any Vault object.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/bulk-delete-records-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Bulk delete records (batch) action**: The Bulk delete records (batch) action deletes up to 500 records by ID in a single request in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/bulk-upsert-records-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Bulk upsert records (batch) action**: The Bulk upsert records (batch) action posts up to 500 records in a single request to the Vault bulk endpoint in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/create-document-relationship-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Create document relationship action**: The Create document relationship action links a source document version to a target document in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/create-document-version-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Create document version action**: The Create document version action adds a new draft version of an existing document in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/create-record-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Create record action**: The Create record action creates a record, such as a product, in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/delete-record-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Delete record action**: The Delete record action deletes a single record, such as a product, by its ID in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/download-document-rendition-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Download document rendition action**: The Download document rendition action downloads a document's viewable rendition as a PDF file in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/get-document-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Get document action**: The Get document action retrieves full metadata for a single document, including its renditions and version history, in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/get-record-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Get record action**: The Get record action retrieves the full metadata for a single Vault object record by its ID in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/new-updated-document-trigger.md` — **Veeva Vault - New/updated document trigger**: The New/updated document trigger runs when a document is created or updated in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/new-updated-record-scheduled-vql-trigger.md` — **Veeva Vault - New/updated record (scheduled VQL) trigger**: The New/updated record (scheduled VQL) trigger runs your VQL SELECT statement on a fixed schedule and fires for each new or updated record in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/run-vql-query-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Run VQL query action**: The Run VQL query action submits a raw Vault Query Language (VQL) query against documents, objects, or system tables in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/search-audit-trail-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Search audit trail action**: The Search audit trail action retrieves audit history records for documents, objects, system events, domain events, or login events in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/search-records-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Search records action**: The Search records action searches records, such as products, in Veeva Vault using a VQL query.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/update-record-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Update record action**: The Update record action updates a record, such as a product, in Veeva Vault.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/upload-document-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Upload document action**: The Upload document action creates a single document record in Veeva Vault by uploading its source file.
- `docs/connectors/veeva-vault/upsert-record-action.md` — **Veeva Vault - Upsert record action**: The Upsert record action creates a new record in Veeva Vault, or updates the existing record if a match is found.
- `docs/connectors/vercel.md` — **Vercel**: Vercel is a platform for building and deploying web applications.
- `docs/connectors/vercel/assign-alias-action.md` — **Vercel - Assign alias action**: The Assign alias action assigns an alias to a Vercel deployment.
- `docs/connectors/vercel/cancel-deployment-action.md` — **Vercel - Cancel deployment action**: The Cancel deployment action cancels an in-progress deployment in Vercel.
- `docs/connectors/vercel/create-check-run-action.md` — **Vercel - Create check run action**: The Create check run action starts a check run on a Vercel deployment.

- `docs/connectors/vercel/new-deployment-event-trigger.md` — **Vercel - New deployment event (real-time) trigger**: The New deployment event trigger monitors Vercel in real time for deployment events and starts a recipe when they occur.
- `docs/connectors/vercel/new-deployment-trigger.md` — **Vercel - New deployment trigger**: The New deployment trigger checks Vercel for new deployments and starts a recipe for each one.
- `docs/connectors/vercel/promote-deployment-action.md` — **Vercel - Promote deployment action**: The Promote deployment action points production traffic at an existing Vercel deployment without rebuilding it.
- `docs/connectors/vercel/register-deployment-check-action.md` — **Vercel - Register deployment check action**: The Register deployment check action creates a deployment check on a Vercel project.
- `docs/connectors/vercel/report-check-result-action.md` — **Vercel - Report check result action**: The Report check result action updates a Vercel check run with a status and conclusion.
- `docs/connectors/vercel/search-deployments-action.md` — **Vercel - Search deployments action**: The Search deployments action searches deployments for the authenticated user or team using filters.
- `docs/connectors/veza.md` — **Veza**: Connect Veza to Workato to automate identity and access governance workflows, enforcing least-privilege access and securing sensitive cloud data.
- `docs/connectors/veza/create-custom-provider-action.md` — **Veza - Create a custom provider action**: The Create a custom provider action allows you to create a custom provider using the custom application template programmatically. This is useful for integrating third-party applications or custom data sources into Veza's access management platform.
- `docs/connectors/veza/push-custom-provider-data-source-action.md` — **Veza - Push a custom provider data source action**: The Push a custom provider datasource action in Veza allows you to send access and authorization data from a custom application or system to Veza. This is essential for maintaining visibility and governance over permissions within a custom provider.
- `docs/connectors/veza/register-new-data-source-for-custom-provider-action.md` — **Veza - Register a new data source for a custom provider action**: The Register a new data source for a custom provider action allows you to register a new data source under an existing custom provider in Veza. A data source represents a logical grouping of access data, such as user roles, permissions, and resources for a specific system.
- `docs/connectors/walmart-seller-center.md` — **Walmart Seller Center**: The Walmart Seller Center connector enables you to automate e-commerce workflows, managing product listings, inventory, orders, and fulfillment on Walmart.
- `docs/connectors/walmart-seller-center/bulk-update-inventory-action.md` — **Walmart Seller Center - Bulk update inventory action**: The Bulk update inventory action enables you to update details of multiple items existing in an inventory in a single call.

- `docs/connectors/walmart-seller-center/search-records-action.md` — **Walmart Seller Center - Search records action**: The Search records action enables you to retrieve a list of records that match your search criteria in the Walmart Seller Center.
- `docs/connectors/walmart-seller-center/update-record-action.md` — **Walmart Seller Center - Update record action**: The Update record action enables you to update the details of a specific record in the Walmart Seller Center.

- `docs/connectors/wordpress.md` — **WordPress**: Connect WordPress to Workato to automate content management for blogs, websites, and more.

- `docs/connectors/wordpress/delete-post-action.md` — **WordPress - Delete post action**: The Delete post action in the WordPress connector lets you delete a post by its ID and returns whether the deletion succeeded.

- `docs/connectors/wordpress/new-comment-trigger.md` — **WordPress - New comment trigger**: The New comment trigger in the WordPress connector runs when a new comment is detected, checking for new comments every five minutes by default.

- `docs/connectors/wordpress/search-post-action.md` — **WordPress - Search post action**: The Search post action in the WordPress connector lets you search for a post by its title and return the matching post details.
- `docs/connectors/workato-edi.md` — **Workato EDI connector**: The Workato EDI connector, powered by Orderful, enables you to automate EDI workflows, exchanging purchase orders, invoices, and shipment notices.
- `docs/connectors/workato-edi/convert-data-format.md` — **Workato EDI - Convert data format action**: Use the Convert data action to convert data from JSON to X12 or X12 to JSON.
- `docs/connectors/workato-edi/create-record.md` — **Workato EDI - Create record action**: Use the create record action to create a record in Orderful, such as a transaction or acknowledgment.
- `docs/connectors/workato-edi/download-transaction-file.md` — **Workato EDI - Download transaction file action**: Use the Download transaction file action to download file content from an Any File transaction's download URL.
- `docs/connectors/workato-edi/generate-label.md` — **Workato EDI - Generate label action**: Use the Generate label action create a custom shipping label.
- `docs/connectors/workato-edi/get-record.md` — **Workato EDI - Get record action**: Use the Get record action to retrieve details about a record, such as a transaction, transaction message, organization, acknowledgment, or delivery.

- `docs/connectors/zoom.md` — **Zoom**: Zoom is a cloud platform for video and audio conferencing, collaboration, chat, and webinars.
- `docs/connectors/zoom/best-practices.md` — **Zoom - Best Practices**: Best practices for building recipes with the Zoom connector in Workato, including filtering trigger events and formatting webinar poll results.
- `docs/connectors/zoom/cloud-recording.md` — **Zoom - Download cloud recording action**: The Download cloud recording action in the Zoom connector lets you download cloud recordings directly from Zoom and send them to downstream apps.
- `docs/connectors/zoom/event-actions.md` — **Zoom - Webinar, meetings, and cloud recordings**: The event actions in the Zoom connector let you schedule, update, search, and get details for meetings, webinars, and cloud recordings.
- `docs/connectors/zoom/new-event-trigger.md` — **Zoom - New event trigger**: The New event trigger in the Zoom connector monitors Zoom for new meeting, webinar, cloud recording, and user events in real time.
- `docs/connectors/zoom/registrant-actions.md` — **Zoom - Registrants**: The registrant actions in the Zoom connector let you add, get, and update registrants for Zoom webinars, meetings, and cloud recordings.
- `docs/connectors/zoom/user-actions.md` — **Zoom - User actions**: The user actions in the Zoom connector let you create, update, search, get, and delete users in your Zoom organization to automate onboarding.
- `docs/connectors/zscaler-digital-experience.md` — **Zscaler Digital Experience (ZDX)**: Zscaler Digital Experience (ZDX) is a cloud-native service that monitors application, device, and network performance.
- `docs/connectors/zscaler-digital-experience/get-record-action.md` — **Zscaler Digital Experience (ZDX) - Get record action**: The Get record action retrieves a single application, user, or device from Zscaler Digital Experience (ZDX) by its unique ZDX ID.
- `docs/connectors/zscaler-digital-experience/search-records-action.md` — **Zscaler Digital Experience (ZDX) - Search records action**: The Search records action searches Zscaler Digital Experience (ZDX) for records that match the filters you specify.
- `docs/connectors/zuora.md` — **Zuora**: The Zuora connector lets you automate subscription billing workflows: create, update, search, and query records across your order-to-revenue processes.
- `docs/connectors/zuora/create_record.md` — **Zuora - Create record action**: The Create record action in the Zuora connector lets you create a single record of a selected object type with dynamically generated fields.
- `docs/connectors/zuora/query_record.md` — **Zuora - Query record action**: The Query record action in the Zuora connector lets you query records using Zuora Object Query Language, a SQL-like language for the SOAP API.
- `docs/connectors/zuora/search_record.md` — **Zuora - Search record action**: The Search record action in the Zuora connector lets you search records by object type using dynamically generated search parameters.
- `docs/connectors/zuora/update_record.md` — **Zuora - Update record action**: The Update record action in the Zuora connector lets you select a record by its unique Zuora ID and update it with the data you provide.
